143. (28 Sep) Environment image (owner: a red neon photo glowed on the mixer only when seated). How it works: the image is painted inside a sphere (radius 4) over RoomEnvironment and baked by PMREM into scene.environment: infinitely far, so reflections depend only on direction; a glossy horizontal panel reflects the horizon at shallow (seated) angles and the upper sphere when standing, so a flat photo stretched 360 x 180 put the sign in a band at the horizon. Now (env.js): a picture of about 2:1 (1.8-2.2) is taken as an equirectangular panorama and wraps the sphere; any other picture becomes a window 100 deg wide (height by its aspect, max 150 deg) at eye level straight ahead (-z world, u = 0.25 with the mirrored repeat), edges faded over 18 %, studio lighting everywhere else. Panoramas are kept at 2048 wide (others 1024). Checked: test image renders upright and unmirrored straight ahead at eye level. envLight added to window.vire. Owner will use equirectangular images where possible.
144. (28 Sep) Neon sign rebuilt in Blender in the owner's preferred clear-glass style (his 'O' / 'D' neon references: clear glass tube, thin glowing core, rounded glass ends with electrodes, soft glow) instead of one solid glowing tube (read as cartoony). blender/neon/neon_glass.blend (builder text block neon_glass_lib; made from paths.json, the same logo centrelines; the old tubes are in the 'ref' collection; Blender was not re-opened on neon.blend because opening a file stops the MCP server, the objects were appended). NeonGlass: 13 mm clear tubes, 8 sides, a ring every 10 deg of turn or 10 cm, rounded glass caps on the 4 open ends with dark electrodes (vertex colour) inside, glass bulbs (8.5 mm) for the two dots: 3,016 tris. NeonCore: 3.6 mm core, 4 sides, stops 21 mm before each open end (at the electrodes), small cores in the dots: 1,416 tris. Clamps/rims unchanged (#133; saddles 7 mm fit the 6.5 mm glass). Changing the light count recompiles the lit materials once per combination (a short hitch the first time a lamp goes up or down).

The originals stay for picking/logic on layer HIDE_LAYER = 5 (not 1/2: three's WebXR eye cameras use those); main.js's raycaster enables that layer. 158. (29 Sep) Spectator spike built in web/ (not yet in deploy_web). Owner confirmed the M33 has ARCore. (a) js/net-link.js: WebRTC data link, channels 'state' (unordered, no retransmits, dropped if >16 KB buffered) and 'ctl' (reliable). Handshake via ntfy.sh (public pub/sub: each side listens on topic cly3dj-v1-<id> by SSE, posts to the other's; offer/answer sent once with all candidates, no trickle). The PeerJS cloud relay (0.peerjs.com) was tried first: it accepts sockets and says OPEN but forwards nothing. No libraries (cloud and VM egress block npm/CDNs). (b) Quest: start screen Settings > Spectator camera Off/On (default Off; settings.spect), 5-digit code kept in localStorage vire.spectCode and shown with a status line. js/spectator-host.js is imported only when On; frame() calls spect.tick(dt): nothing when no phone, else a ~150-byte head-pose packet (rig space) 20x/s; layout snapshot on connect and after every stage.save(); answers ping with its clock. Never touches the audio engine. sw.js LATE list gained the two files. (c) Phone: web/spectator.html + js/spectator.js: code entry, Connect, Start camera (immersive-ar, hit-test, dom-overlay, 'local' space); tap the floor under the middle of the DJ table to place the rig (its front faces the phone), rotate buttons, Re-place, Hide UI (a tap brings it back), live head marker (red wire sphere + nose), info line with round trip, pose age (clock offset from pings) and packets/s. Gear = outline boxes from the layout (cases, decks, mixer, crate, milk crates; neon left out). Tested in the owner's Chrome (two tabs on localhost:8000/vire): handshake OK, layout arrived (6 boxes), 18-20 packets/s, pose age ~11 ms. Backup: backups/pre-spectator (index.html, main.js, sw.js). No server.js change, no restart needed.

The repo's cly3dj/ came from the clone and matches it (CRLF line endings from the repo's `* text=auto`); it is the release folder now, replacing deploy_web. Two channels on the same origin: cly3dj/ = release (what he plays out with), cly3dj-dev/ = test builds (new; first content = web/ with #158, same exclusions as deploy_web: Collection junction, both Rekordbox XMLs, cly3dj_taps.json(.bak), check.html, models/plastic_crate.glb, empty vendor/three/addons). Same origin = shared OPFS songs, localStorage settings/layout and Cache Storage (keys are full URLs, no clash). Flow: edit in web/ (Pinggy still works for instant checks) -> copy to cly3dj-dev/ -> owner commits + pushes in GitHub Desktop -> tests at dirrogate.github.io/cly3dj-dev/ -> when good, copy to cly3dj/. Never run plain `git status` in the repo from the VM: it left a .git/index.lock the VM could not remove (needed a delete grant); use `git --no-optional-locks status` or diff only.
160. (29 Sep) Spectator calibration built (web/ and cly3dj-dev/; spike versions of both spectator files kept in backups/pre-spectator/*.spike.js). Phone bar: Calibrate, Fix (tap X), rotate 1 deg, Rough place, Head (marker on/off), Hide UI, Exit. Calibrate: (1) phone taps the tape X (transient-input hit test at the finger, falls back to the centre ring) -> cyan diamond marks it; (2) phone asks the Quest for 'lens': a panel floats in the headset (canvas texture, 0.6 m ahead; DOM toasts are invisible in XR) and the DJ touches the phone's back lens with the controller tip (xr.inputs[i].tip, the blue ball; a hand pinch also works) and pulls the trigger (selectstart on renderer.xr.getController(i)) -> tip in rig space + Quest time; phone takes its own lens position from a 3 s pose log at that moment (clock offset from pings); (3) same for the X. Solve: yaw = heading(lens-X on phone) - heading(lens-X on Quest) (flat vectors, gravity shared), t = Xp - R*Xq, applied to the rig group; quality = difference of the X-to-lens distance on both devices (cm, shown on both; >5 cm = redo). Verified in node on 200 random poses (error 4e-15 m). Fix = re-tap the X only, keeps yaw. Rotate buttons pivot on the X once calibrated. When calibrated the phone sends its camera pose (rig space) + fov/aspect 5x/s; the Quest draws a blue wire frustum (3.5 m) in the rig, hidden after 2 s without data. Desktop two-tab test: link, layout, cal messages, panel/toast OK, no errors.
161. (29 Sep) Spectator phone draws the real scene (owner: build it all out, then fix backwards). Backup: backups/pre-mirror (main.js, spectator-host.js, spectator.js, spectator.html). (a) main.js camera role: index.html?role=camera (spectator.html now just redirects there). CAMERA_ROLE = pixel ratio 1, no shadow map, no setupXR (xr = null), no loadLibrary, no spectator host; #start/#hud hidden; imports js/spectator-client.js with { THREE, renderer, scene, camera, rig, room, stage, cases, decks, deckInst, neon, newMilk, stepWallGlow, stepBlobs, Record3D, BG }, which replaces frame() with its own loop. main.js also keeps artBlobs (track id -> the APIC Blob) beside artCache. (b) Quest (spectator-host.js): 30 packets/s. Scene mirror: registry of every node under each stage item + flight case, id = root key + child-index path (records skipped), rebuilt every 2 s; each tick sends only nodes whose local pos/quat/scale/visible changed (eps 2e-4); full snapshot on the reliable channel at connect; once a second the final pose of anything that moved in the last 5 s goes again on the reliable channel (state channel drops packets). Records (decks, held, loose): 'rec' once (track meta), 'renv' per side (8192-bin envelope as Uint16 base64, ~22 KB), 'rart' per side (the MP3's own cover bytes, no re-encode), transform in rig space + mesh flip each tick, 'recdel' when gone. Hands in XR: 25 joint positions per tracked hand, or the grip pose per controller. (c) Phone (spectator-client.js): same UI/calibration as #160 but builds its own DOM and places main's `rig` (hidden until placed). Mirror runs 70 ms behind the Quest clock (ping offset) and interpolates (lerp/slerp) between samples; a node that was resting gets a hold sample just before its new move. Missing extra milk crates are created (newMilk). Records built with the same Record3D (groove rules untouched: envelope -> redraw). Hands: depth-only spheres at the joints + palm + forearm capsule (controllers: fist sphere + forearm), renderOrder -10, colorWrite off; 'Hands' button shows them pink for tuning. Also calls deckInst.update(), neon flicker, wall glow and blobs each frame. AR: scene.background null, room hidden, environmentIntensity 0.28. Desktop two-tab test: 250/250 nodes resolved, lid toggle mirrored, record pulled + placed on the Quest arrived with envelope (8192) and label, drawn with grooves on the phone (screenshot). Not yet tested on Quest + M33 (hands, AR, performance).

163. (29 Sep) Bug (owner): lowering the flight case left the decks and mixer buried inside it on the spectator phone. Cause: FlightCase.size() rebuilds the case's parts, and only transforms were mirrored, so the phone kept the old 0.88 m case while the gear (a synced transform) came down onto the lower top. Fix: host sends case sizes (W, D, H) in the full snapshot and in any tick where they changed ('cs'); phone calls the same c.size() (at most ~8x/s during a live drag, immediately on a full snapshot or layout message) and re-finds the rebuilt parts. Tested localhost two tabs: case 0.88 -> 0.6 on the host gave phone H 0.6, deck y 0.618, case top 0.616; back to 0.88 followed. Backups of the #161 versions: backups/pre-mirror/spectator-*.161.js.

(a) Mixer screen: the MR GUI toggle (108 x 28 px, 700 22px) and the small phone icon (14 x 24, LED green/grey) moved to deck A's top row, in line with 'A • LEAD', right-aligned 8 px inside A's panel so the divider to B stays clear; hit area padded. 176. (30 Sep) LED wall + colour scheme (owner; Spout parked for later). (a) js/ledwall.js: makeLedWall() = 16:9 screen 1.6 x 0.9 m at scale 1 in a 25 mm dark bezel, 5 cm deep, origin at its centre; off = dark glossy panel, on = MeshBasic VideoTexture (toneMapped off), video fitted 'cover'. Stage item 'ledwall' (base = (H/2 + bezel) x scale), default [-2.0, 1.6, -0.55, 0]; moves like the neon (not stackable), desktop wheel / two hands in XR resize it (xr.js 'ledScale', GEAR.ledwall), size in localStorage 'vire.ledScale' (0.4..4). (b) LedPlayer: up to 5 videos picked in Settings each session ('LED wall videos: Choose videos…', multiple; the Quest browser can't open or remember a folder), always muted, random clip, never the same one twice in a row, next on 'ended'; stopping frees the decoder. (c) Mixer screen: LED WALL switch on deck B's top row, right-aligned, same look as MR GUI (red = video, grey = off); with no videos picked it says so. Always shown (not tied to the spectator setting). (d) Spectator phone: the wall's position/scale mirror as a stage item; the Quest sends {k:'led', on, name, t, qt} on every clip change and every 2 s; the phone plays its own copy of the file with the same name (its start screen has 'LED wall videos (the same files as the Quest)'), seeking only when > 0.3 s out; without the file its wall stays dark. (e) Colour scheme: title 'Cly' white + '3DJ' red (#e0202a) on the start screen and the phone page. Tested in the owner's Chrome with two generated webm clips: wall plays and alternates clips, LED WALL toggles, phone tab followed every clip change within the same frame tick and got a time update every 2 s. Backups: backups/pre-ledwall/.
177. (30 Sep) VideoVinyl (owner: MP3 + a video named exactly like the Rekordbox track title incl. _a/_b, e.g. 'Ray of Light_a.mp4'; 960x540 or smaller). js/videovinyl.js: DeckVideo (muted <video> + VideoTexture) follows the deck: steady forward play (0.25 < rate < 4) = video.playbackRate = deck rate, re-seek if > 0.15 s out; stopped / scratched / reversed = paused and seeking to the playhead at most every 80 ms; stops at the video's end if it is shorter than the track. Sources: headset = videos imported with the songs (storage.js import filter + mp4/m4v/webm/mov; vvIndex from the OPFS index by file base name, lower case); PC = web/videos/<title>.mp4 (HEAD probe, cached). vvStep() per frame per deck (track = d.record && d.track). Mixer screen: 'VV  ·' before the artist when a deck's video is loaded. LED WALL now cycles OFF -> LED CLIPS (skipped if no clips picked) -> LED DECKS -> OFF; DECKS = deck A's video on the screen scaled by its gain, deck B's added on a second plane with AdditiveBlending (ledwall.setDecks); gains = deckGains(mixVal) = squared channel fader x constant-power crossfader, identical to audio.js, so cuts cut the picture. Videos are never cached by the service worker. Phone: one picker for LED clips + VideoVinyls (matched by name); host sends vv {mode, gains, decks:[i, key, pos, rate]} in every state packet; phone extrapolates pos by rate and runs the same DeckVideo.follow; setDecks with the Quest's gains. rsync to cly3dj-dev now also excludes web/videos. Tested localhost with a 60 s 640x360 testsrc video (keyframe every 5 frames) named after the loaded track: video within 10-30 ms of the audio at 0 % and +8 % pitch (playbackRate 1.0799 vs deck 1.0800), paused and parked on the playhead when the motor stopped, wall showed it in DECKS; phone tab opened the same-named file, gain 0.64 (= 0.8^2), parked at the video's end after the track passed 60 s. Test video moved to _to_delete/vv_test. Backups: backups/pre-videovinyl/.
178. (30 Sep) Bug (owner, Quest, GitHub dev copy): a picked environment image never showed (reflections or skybox) in any mode. Cause (old, from the #143 edit): in #fEnv.onchange the drawImage(bmp, ...) call sat after a '//' comment on the same line, so the canvas stayed blank and a black JPEG was stored in 'vire.envimage'; my #172/#174/#175 tests called envLight.setImage() directly and missed it. Fixed (drawImage on its own line). Tested through the real picker path (File -> #fEnv change -> stored dataURL has content -> reload -> applyEnv -> skybox + reflections at 100 %). Owners must re-pick the image once: the stored one is black. Lesson: test features through the same UI path the owner uses. Owner's localhost env settings reset to Studio / 50 % / sky off after the test.
179. (30 Sep) Stale code after pushes, found while re-testing #178 on dirrogate.github.io/cly3dj-dev: GitHub Pages sends max-age=600 and the service worker's fetch(req) went through the browser HTTP cache, so after a push the page kept running the old js/main.js (transferSize 0) even after clearing Cache Storage, unregistering the SW and a hard reload. sw.js now fetches with { cache: 'no-cache' } (navigation requests by URL): every app file is revalidated with the server (304 if unchanged) and falls back to the offline copy as before. sw.js itself is always re-checked by the browser, so this reaches the Quest on its next load; from then on a reload after a push picks up the new code. Could not be exercised on localhost (the desktop app's browser pane doesn't register the SW over http).
180. (30 Sep) Owner: records seem to reflect only the studio; passthrough should reflect more. (a) Checked on localhost with a picked test panorama at 100 %: records DO reflect the chosen image (its yellow top showed on the vinyl), just faintly, because the owner-approved record gloss is specularIntensity 0.15 (#58/#93, groove rules). The bright white streak on records is the key DirectionalLight's highlight, not the environment. Record gloss left unchanged pending the owner's call. (b) Passthrough reflection strength: scene.environmentIntensity in AR was a fixed 0.28; now Settings > 'Reflections in passthrough' 0-100 % (settings.arRefl, default 60 %), applied at AR start and live while in AR. Phone camera uses 0.6 in AR to match the default. Backups: backups/pre-videovinyl/main.179.js, index.179.html.
181. (30 Sep) Owner, two items. (a) Tonearm released over a record whose grooves haven't rendered yet (d.record && d.track && !d.loaded, radius between REC.IN and REC.EDGE) now hovers: armRelease stores a.pendingR; updateArm, once d.loaded, sets cueTime from that radius, lowers the arm (wantDown) and on landing seeks + needle on. Grabbing the arm or a manual move cancels it. Tested on localhost: arm released during load, dropped and played at 109 s once loaded. (b) xr.js grabStart: grip on a record sitting on the platter (label or just outside it, height -3.5..+5 cm of the record top) starts the same 'lift' as the sleeve pull. Desktop can't test grip; untested on Quest. Backups: backups/pre-videovinyl/main.180.js, xr.180.js.
182. (30 Sep) Owner: grounded panorama skybox, mono and stereo (audience sees 2D, so grounding matters more than stereo). New js/skybox.js: sphere R 20 m whose lower half is flattened onto the floor (three's GroundedSkybox formula, y1 = -1.5 h), centred at the DJ spot rig (0.05, 0, 0.62) = XR origin; each pixel looked up from its exact direction in the shader (textureGrad with a seam-free u, no mip seam). Pano height h = camera height when shot (Settings > Pano height 0.5-3 m, default 1.5). Stereo: auto by aspect (1:1 over-under, 4:1 side-by-side, 2:1 mono) or forced in Pano type (incl. right-eye-first); in the headset mesh 0 is on XR layer 1 (left eye), mesh 1 on layer 2; desktop/recordings use the left eye. Pano turn rotates sky, scene.environmentRotation and backgroundRotation together (checked: mirror-sphere reflections match the sky at 0 and 90 deg). Key light from the image's brightest area (>= 35 deg up, colour half-way to the light's), Settings on/off. Full file kept in OPFS 'vire-sky/pano' and loaded when shown (texture capped at 8192x4096 pixels, bitmap closed after GPU upload); localStorage keeps only the 2048 left-eye copy for lighting. Per-image height/turn/type/key in 'vire.skyPrefs'. Real-time shadows fall on a ShadowMaterial floor while the sky shows; blob shadows unchanged. Non-panorama pictures keep the #172/#175 background. Tested on localhost with a synthetic 4096 box-room pano (straight walls and checker floor from the centre, window found for the key light) and the owner's 4096 over-under kitchen. Test images: ViRe_Custom/test_panos. Backups: backups/pre-skybox/*.181.*. Lesson: device_commit_files may skip a re-commit from the same staged path; stage under a new name.
183. (30 Sep) Owner's M33: Chrome 154.0.8037.57, Android 16, so past the three.js #33404 fix (Chrome 149.0.7821+). Before building the green-screen 'virtual set' (phone composites pano + keyed camera + gear, and could then move freely), a test: phone start screen button 'Camera access test (no Quest needed)' -> js/camtest.js (plain WebXR + WebGL2, lazy-loaded): immersive-ar with required 'camera-access', draws getCameraImage full screen, right half paints green as magenta; shows whether the picture arrives, camera size, tracking, fps. Desktop only checked the module loads (no AR there). sw.js LATE += camtest.js, skybox.js. Backup: backups/pre-skybox/spectator-client.182.js.
184. (30 Sep) #183 test passed on the M33 (camera picture 2942/2972 frames, 861x1920, tracking on, 25 fps, upright, not mirrored, green cloth keyed cleanly). Owner: live keyer on the phone for quick effects videos, optional per take. New js/vset.js (makeVirtualSet): AR session asks for optional 'camera-access'; while 'Set' is on, each XR frame getCameraImage -> GPU blit into our own texture (so a frame without a picture repeats the last one instead of flashing the room; falls back to direct use if the blit framebuffer is incomplete; renderer.resetState() after). Back to front: grounded skybox (renderOrder -1000), full-screen key quad (-999, opaque pass, CustomBlending src alpha, frame alpha kept 1), depth-only hands (-10), gear. Key: g - max(r,b) with smoothstep(Strength, +Softness), Spill pulls green down to max(r,b); 'Show matte' check view; sliders kept in 'vire.vsetKey'. Panorama picked on the phone's start screen (Virtual set panorama…, saved in the phone's OPFS); height / turn / type / key-light follow the Quest (host getSky -> ctl 'sky' on change; phone flags a different file by name:size). Set on also switches reflections + key light to the pano, off restores the AR look (env 0.6). Buttons Set / Key in the AR bar; Hide UI hides the key panel too. Desktop check (phone role, fake camera canvas): pano behind, keyed shape, gear in front, matte view correct. Not yet tried on the M33. Known: the pano is centred at the DJ spot, so indoor panos bend when the phone is metres away (walls sit on the 20 m sphere); far outdoor scenes are fine. Backups: backups/pre-skybox/*.183.js.
185. (30 Sep) Owner: phone as media storage, pushing single items (not projects) to the Quest, and a Video page on the mixer HUD to choose where each goes. Folders (owner: VV and LED clips merged): Pano, Video pano, Video, Images. js/medialib.js (both sides): OPFS 'vire-media/<folder>/<name>' + 320 px JPEG thumbs in '_thumbs'; streamed import; resumable receive via '<name>.part' (keepExistingData + seek, move() on finish). net-link: third data channel 'file' (reliable, binary, 64 KB chunks, ~4 MB in flight, bufferedamountlow); onOpen only for state+ctl. Protocol on ctl: phone 'mls?' -> Quest 'mls' [folder,name,size]; 'mput' {f,n,size,thumb b64} -> 'mgo' {off} or 'mok'; chunks on 'file'; Quest saves when bytes == size -> 'mok' + new list; 'mdel'. js/phonelib.js: phone Library screen (start screen button): folder tabs, Import (Android picker), thumbnails, '✓ on Quest' badges, Select all, Push to Quest (skips what the Quest has, resumes, MB/s, Wake Lock), Remove from Quest / phone, storage use. Quest mixer: deck B top-row button is now VIDEO (red while the LED wall plays) -> Video page: tabs PANO / VIDEO PANO / VIDEO / IMAGES / MIXER, 8 thumbs a page with SKY / A / B / LED tags, bottom row ‹ › + targets (Pano, Video pano: SKY, SKY OFF; Video: DECK A, DECK B, LED NOW, + LED; Images: LED NOW, + LED) + LED OFF/CLIPS/DECKS cycle (moved here from the top row). SKY uses the library file directly (settings.skyMedia; ensureSky restores it after reload); Video pano = looping muted VideoTexture on the grounded skybox (skybox.setVideo, openPanoVideo; paused while hidden). DECK A/B = vvOverride {rec,name}: that clip replaces the title match until the record comes off (owner). LED: LedPlayer.add/play, images supported (8 s each in the playlist, ledwall.setImage). Phone mirrors: sky 'media' id -> vset loads its own library copy (stills and video panos); VV key 'media:Video/<name>' and LED names resolve from the phone library. Tested with two origins (localhost = Quest, 127.0.0.1 = phone, separate storage) over the real ntfy/WebRTC link: import 4 files, push byte-exact (SHA-256 match) with thumbs, SKY still + video pano, DECK A override on a real record and cleared by the next record, LED NOW mirrored on the phone, image on LED, resume from a 120 KB partial. Not yet on Quest + M33 (speed there unknown). Backups: backups/pre-medialib/.
186. (30 Sep) Auto key (to-do #1) + key formula change. Key value is now (g - max(r,b)) / max(g, 0.15) (shader keyOf and JS analyseKey identical): relative to the green level, so shaded wrinkles key like the lit screen; skin / grey / beige <= 0; the 0.15 floor stops dark noise keying. Sliders re-ranged (Strength 0-0.8, Softness 0.02-0.4), defaults thr 0.1 / soft 0.1; saved key settings carry v:2 (old v1 values ignored). Auto (key panel): next camera frame is blitted down to about 120 x 240 and read back once; pixels with key > 0.12 must fill >= 30 % of the view; fully keyed from their 3rd-lowest percent x 0.9, Softness 40 % of that (0.05-0.3), Strength the rest; works with Set off (reads the camera just for the measurement). Checked on the owner's green-cloth test screenshot (#183): cover 99 %, thr 0.086 / soft 0.058, matte clean incl. shaded folds, wall / grey pillow / red cushion kept. Fixed: phone Library screen showed on load (#spLib display:flex beat [hidden]). Backups: backups/pre-medialib/vset.185.js, spectator-client.185.js.
187. (30 Sep) To-do #2 Look match + #3 light wrap / colour match (phone). New js/lookmatch.js (Look button + panel in the AR bar; on by default; Strength, Grain, Room light; saved in 'vire.look'). (a) Camera grade: every 8th frame vset samples the camera to 32 px (sample() now takes a size; cached per size) -> grey-world cast (clamped +-15 %) and exposure sqrt(lum / 0.42) (0.7-1.35), smoothed ~0.8 s. (b) Grain: hash noise per pixel per frame, amount x (0.02 + 0.05 x darkness). (a)+(b) = one full-screen quad at clip z 0.99999 with depthFunc GREATER (passes only where the gear wrote depth; camera, skybox and key quad write none; hand occluders keep alpha 0 so the real hands stay untouched in AR), 2 x multiply blending (DstColor/SrcColor, alpha kept), renderOrder 10000. (c) Room light: session optional 'light-estimation' -> requestLightProbe; per frame SH -> THREE.LightProbe, primary light -> key (direction world->rig, y >= 0.2, colour / intensity as three's XREstimatedLight), environmentIntensity 0.3 (reflections only); restores the key / env when off; while the virtual set is on the pano owns the key (grade off, grain stays). (d) Virtual set shader: colour match (camera x mix(1, pano cast clamped 0.8-1.25, amount)) and light wrap (6 key samples 8 camera px away -> edge = share keyed out -> add 0.9 x pano average colour x edge x amount); pano colour measured from the lighting canvas (32 x 16). Key panel sliders Light wrap (0.5) and Colour match (0.4), in 'vire.vsetKey'. Desktop checks: simulated warm camera grade on the gear; kitchen pano (cast 1.10/0.98/0.88) warms a grey keyed figure and lights its edges. Room light and real camera stats untested (need the M33). Backups: backups/pre-medialib/vset.186/187.js, spectator-client.186.js.
188. (30 Sep) To-do #4 + #5: grade the phone from the headset and watch it. Mixer Video page has a CAMERA tab (PANO / VIDEO PANO / VIDEO / IMAGES / CAMERA / MIXER): top row SET, AUTO KEY, MATTE, PREVIEW, LOOK, ROOM LIGHT; steppers (− value +) for Strength, Softness, Spill, Light wrap, Colour match, Look strength, Grain; status line (phone fps, camera started, calibrated, camera access, preview live/waiting, auto / look messages). Phone reports 'cst' {set, can, ar, cal, K, L, matte, pv, fps, auto, look} on change / every second; Quest sends 'cset' {what: set|key|look|matte|auto|preview, key, v}; the phone clamps, applies, updates its own panels and reports back (phone panels stay usable). spectator-host: cam getter + camSet(); 'cst' -> onCam. Preview: net-link 4th channel 'prev' (unordered, no retransmits, dropped when > 64 KB queued; onOpen/onClose ignore file+prev). Phone every ~150 ms (when PREVIEW on, in AR): vset grabs the camera that frame (S.pvNeed / pvReady), second render into a 480 px tall sRGB render target with the XR camera's matrices (renderer.xr.enabled off for that render; raw uniform shows the un-keyed camera when the set is off; reticle / X hidden), readRenderTargetPixels, flip, JPEG 0.6 -> sendPrev. Note: an isXRRenderTarget hack double-encoded sRGB (washed out); plain sRGB RT storage is right. Quest: 'preview' stage item (movable window, default rig (0.78, 1.28, -0.22), 0.62 m tall, aspect from the frames), texture from createImageBitmap(flipY), only visible while PREVIEW is on, excluded from the phone mirror registry; a reconnecting phone gets PREVIEW back. Tested on two origins over the real link: steppers and toggles applied on the phone (thr 0.10 -> 0.12, grain 0.5 -> 0.55, look off, preview on), CAMERA tab shows the phone's values, a preview frame rendered on the phone (XR camera stubbed) showed upright in the headset window. Not yet in XR on the devices (fps cost of the preview on the M33 unknown). Backups: backups/pre-camtab/.
189. (30 Sep) Owner: don't widen the mixer display; make it a grabbable tablet (like an iPad) with a very shallow slot on the mixer (reference: a lap desk with a tablet slot). models.js: group 'tablet' (u.tablet) = the old stand box as the body + screen (0.0115 in front) + glass; docked pose = the #115 stand pose (u.tabletDock), same 0.19 x 0.075 screen. Slot: 0.206 x 0.0012 x 0.016 near-black matte strip on the mixer top at z -0.168 (where the tablet's lower edge sits). xr.js grabStart 0a (before everything but the lamp): grip (controllers) or pinch (hands) inside the tablet's box (|x| < 11.5 cm, |y| < 5 cm, |z| < 3.5 cm) -> 'tablet' grab keeping the hand-relative matrix; grabMove -> main.tabletHold(world) sets the mixer-local pose; release -> stays in the air, or snaps into the slot within 8 cm (buzz). Pose saved in 'vire.tablet'. It stays a mixer child, so the phone mirrors it (the audience sees the DJ holding it) and screen pokes / taps work wherever it is (they use the screen's own local frame). Desktop: docked look unchanged; hold / release / snap checked with simulated hand poses (a floating tablet reads fine). Mouse can't pick it up (headset only). Backups: backups/pre-tablet/.
190. (30 Sep) Test on GitHub dev (dirrogate.github.io/cly3dj-dev, owner's pushes up to 'remote tablet hud', repo in sync with origin): all 12 changed files served new, page ran the new code under the service worker; only errors = the expected 404s for api/taps and cly3dj_taps.json (not deployed). Two browser tabs (Quest page + spectator.html) linked over ntfy/WebRTC: CAMERA tab + on Strength / Grain / PREVIEW reached the phone (thr 0.10 -> 0.11, grain 0.55, preview on); the tablet taken out and parked in the air on the Quest showed at the same place on the phone (256 mirrored parts, new tablet node paths fine); a phone preview frame (XR camera stubbed) showed in the headset window. (a) Mixer legends TRIM/HI/MID/LOW/FILTER/PAN: were centred 36 mm from their knob (LOW read as the mic PITCH knob's); now start 19.5 mm from the knob centre, just outside its dotted scale, toward the mixer centre (A left-aligned, B right-aligned; label() takes an align, textures.js honours t.align). Shape unchanged (4 x 2 big openings; the photo's crate has rows of small slots: a Blender remodel if wanted). (d) Site / PWA icon: rendered in the app (deck A with the Sousa crest slipmat, near top-down like the Vinyl Reality splash, whole platter + tonearm; 2048 px 4x MSAA with real-time 4096 shadows, downscaled to 1024; for the shot only: env 0.55, key 2.4 from front-left, a blue point light by the tonearm, a red one bottom-left; other stage items hidden; everything restored). renders/cly3dj_icon_1024.jpg; web/icon-192.png, icon-512.png (replaced; old ones in backups/pre-cosmetic/) and new icon-maskable-512.png (80 % safe zone on #0b0d12) added to the manifest (purpose maskable). 192. (30 Sep) Owner: milk crate remodel without adding triangles, proportions from the owner's photo of a produce crate, deep blue. models.js makeMilkCrate: one 1024 canvas atlas (quadrants: long wall = columns of 9 x 30 mm oval slots in two blocks either side of a centre rib; short wall = 9 mm square holes + a 120 x 34 mm hand hole under the rim; floor = 9 x 16 mm slots in four fields around a solid cross; solid quadrant for the boxes). Alpha cut-out (alphaTest 0.5 + alphaToCoverage), normal map from a 3 px blurred mask (rounded slot edges), colour map darkens toward holes + faint grain. Walls = outer + inner panels 6 mm apart (real depth through the holes), floor = top + underside panels; rim lip, 4 corner posts, base skirt = boxes. 164 triangles, 1 draw call, one shared material + geometry; shadows through the holes via a MeshDepthMaterial with the same map. MILK 0.345 cube x 0.28 -> W 0.36, D 0.42, H 0.34 (stacking, blobs, record physics read MILK / userData.inner). Colour 0d2c78, roughness 0.55. The baked GLB (#124/#125) is no longer loaded (functions kept). Also: hover tip over a milk crate said 'Drag to move undefined' (NAMES had no milk keys) -> 'the milk crate'. Backups: backups/pre-milkcut/.
193. (30 Sep) Owner: crate 10 % shorter, 12 % longer, and worn (too pristine). MILK -> W 0.36, D 0.47, H 0.306 (slot layouts recompute from MILK). Default milk-crate positions spread for the longer crates (z 0.15 / 0.68 / 1.21 and 0.3 / 0.83 / 1.36, x -1.0 / -1.47); saved layouts keep their places. Wear, all in textures (seeded, same every load, still 164 triangles): canvas layers for stress whitening (knocked rim edge, random knocks, round the hand holes, scuffs on the boxes), grime (gradient to the bottom of the walls, blotches on the floor / boxes), 55 scratches, dents (height, blurred, + whitened ring). Colour now lives in the map (material colour white) so wear can whiten the deep blue (13,44,120 -> 150,160,185) or dirty it (20,22,26); new roughnessMap (0.5 base, rougher where dirty / scratched / whitened); normal map from rounded slot edges + dents. Rim, posts and skirt boxes: each face gets its own random patch of the worn solid quadrant, sized to the walls' texel density (was one flat texel). Backup: backups/pre-milkcut/models.192.js.
194. (30 Sep) Owner: faint cross-hatch on the crate, and check seams for z-fighting. (a) The cross-hatch was the #192 'grain' term, sin(x*0.9)*sin(y*0.7) = a ~7 px periodic grid; replaced by soft random mottling (48 x 48 seeded noise, upscaled + 6 px blur, +-3.5 %). (b) Seams: the corner posts' outer faces were coplanar with the wall panels (and at 1 mm proud, with the base skirt's outer faces); posts now 0.028 square = 2 mm proud of the panels, 1 mm of the skirt. Checked the rest: rim pieces meet end to end (no overlapping coplanar faces), skirt tops overlap only inside the posts, floor underside 0.5 mm above the skirt bottoms, inner panels 1 mm behind the skirt's inner faces. Flicker test at a corner: 6 renders with the camera stepped 1.5 mm, 35 of 25,600 pixels changed (edges shifting through the holes, not z-fighting). (c) Floor looked pale (heavy box scuffing was applied to the floor quadrant too): the floor now gets 8 light scuffs, the rim / post / skirt quadrant keeps 26. Backup: backups/pre-milkcut/models.193.js.
195. (30 Sep) Owner's first device tries + requests. (a) BUG: spectator.html showed one big 'Library…' button over a blank page and no code box. The start-screen button and the Library screen both had id 'spLib', so the screen's full-page style (#spLib position:fixed inset:0) hit the button. Button renamed spLibBtn. (My desktop tests had clicked straight through it.) (b) Controllers: mixer knobs, faders and pitch faders now take the trigger only (grip never turns / slides them); hands still pinch. (c) Tablet: pick-up = grip only (hands: pinch). Resize = trigger (or pinch) on a light L-shaped handle at its lower-right corner and drag (size ~ distance from the centre), or grip it with the second hand while the first holds it and pull apart; 0.7x to 3x; docked it is always 1x (fits the slot) and the free size comes back when picked up; size saved in 'vire.tablet'. (d) Preview moved from the floating window onto a lid that folds up from the tablet's top edge (laptop / fold-phone), 0.2 x 0.13 m at 1x, tilted 0.3 rad further back, phone picture letterboxed; only while PREVIEW is on. The floating 'preview' stage item is gone. (e) Owner: the detached tablet was a one-sided plane. Cause: makeMixer's mergeStatic merged the tablet's body box into the mixer's static mesh (only the screen moved). The tablet is now skipped by mergeStatic: 2 cm body, solid from behind; the lid has its own 8 mm body. Headset-only parts (lid, corner handle) carry userData.noMirror and the host's mirror registry skips them. Desktop checks: phone start page shows the code box and buttons, Library opens / closes; detached tablet at 1.5x with the lid open, solid from behind; docking resets 1x, picking up restores 1.5x. Grip / trigger / stretch themselves need the headset. Backups: backups/pre-195/.
196. (30 Sep) Owner: tablet too thick; preview as a slide-out screen; chrome corner. (a) Tablet body 20 mm -> 7 mm (TABLET_T, iPad-thin), screen 1 mm proud. Material: new tabletBodyMat() = black brushed metal (MeshPhysical 16171a, metalness 0.85, roughness 0.55 x a 16 x 256 streak texture, anisotropy 0.7); the body was already its own mesh (#195), so no extra draw call. (b) Preview: a 3 mm lid (0.196 x 0.078 m at 1x, same brushed black) lives inside the tablet and slides up out of the top edge in 0.45 s (smoothstep, like the deck lamps) when PREVIEW goes on, and back inside when off (hidden when fully in). Out, 4 mm stays in the tablet. The lid is only as tall as the tablet so it can hide inside it: a portrait phone picture is narrow (~3.5 cm at 1x); enlarge the tablet for tuning. Replaces the #195 hinged fold. (c) Corner handle: a chrome tab (metalness 1, roughness 0.12, the flight case mirror look) set diagonally into the lower-right corner (half inside the body, never over the screen), with a diagonal arrow pressed in (normal map from a blurred canvas arrow). xr.js corner zone moved to it. Fixed on the way: a crate-lid constant name clash (LID) and two lines lost from the tablet code during the edit (both caught on the first reload). Desktop checks: slide out 0 -> 0.5 at 220 ms -> 1, off = hidden; thin side profile; tab visible. Backups: backups/pre-196/.
196b. (30 Sep) Owner: no, the corner must not stick out. The #196 diagonal chrome tab is gone; back to the #195 L flush on the tablet's face at the lower-right corner (16 x 16 mm plate, upright arm 6 mm, bottom arm 3.5 mm, rounded), now brushed silver (MeshPhysical d9dde3, metalness 1, horizontal streaks in the roughness, anisotropy 0.6) with a raised diagonal down-right arrow near the top of the upright arm (normal map from a blurred canvas arrow, normalScale 2.2). One plane cut out with an alpha map, noMirror. xr.js resize zone centred on it (0.095, -0.035). Backups: backups/pre-196/main.196.js, xr.196.js.
197. (30 Sep) Owner: (a) the arrow on the L's joint, not its upper arm: bottom arm 3.5 -> 4.5 mm, arrow (r 1.5 mm, 0.65 mm stroke) centred in the corner square where the arms meet. (b) Tablet held on its side: the preview lid comes out of whichever short edge is on top (pvDir 'px' / 'nx', centre 0.194 m out along x, 4 mm stays in), making a tall portrait panel above the tablet; the picture is turned (rotation.z -/+ 90 deg) and fitted to the turned area. Edge chosen from world up in tablet space (atan2(up.x, up.y)): 'up' <-> side beyond 55 deg, back below 35 deg (dead zone); docked = always 'up'. Changing side = slide in, switch, slide out. Checked: landscape lid at y 0.075; rolled 85 deg -> lid x 0.194, picture turned -90 deg, portrait panel above the tablet. Fixed on the way: name clash '_up' (renamed _pvUp). Backup: backups/pre-196/main.196b.js.
198. (30 Sep) Owner: the menu goes portrait too when the tablet is held on its side. main.js: tabletDir(cur) is the #197 edge test shared by the preview lid (pvWantDir) and the screen (stepScrDir, called in frame()), same 55/35 deg dead zone, docked = landscape. Portrait pages draw on a second canvas mixScreenP (304 x 768); the screen's map is swapped to it and the texture is turned about its centre (-90 deg for +x edge up, +90 for -x), and mixScreenPress maps the screen uv back (px: x = 1 - v, y = 1 - u; nx: x = v, y = u). Layouts: MIXER = MR GUI + VIDEO in a strip on top, decks stacked (drawDeckPanel shared with landscape, tap-save state on its own line); VIDEO = tabs in 2 rows of 3 (MIXER last), 2 x 6 thumbnails a page (vpPer 12 vs 8), targets on their own row above pages / LED; CAMERA = buttons 2 x 3, the 7 steppers in one column, status word-wrapped (wrapText). Buttons shrink their font to fit. Portrait keeps about 26 px clear at the top and bottom for the silver corner L (top-right with +x up, bottom-left with -x up). Tested on localhost both ways (taps hit VIDEO and CAMERA, 14 test items page 12 + 2, dock returns to landscape). Backup backups/pre-198. Deployed main.js to cly3dj-dev.
199. (30 Sep) Owner: neon flicker only once every 5 to 7 minutes (random each cycle), and subtle. neon.js: wait 300-420 s (was 30-120 s); burst = 2-4 dips to 60-80 % (40-110 ms) with 92-100 % between, no long dark gap (was 3-9 dips to 4-34 % plus an optional dark gap). The glow halo and the red point light follow as before. vire.neon.userData.flickerNow() still triggers one for testing. Backup backups/pre-199. Deployed neon.js to cly3dj-dev.
200. (30 Sep) Owner: the blue milk crate wasn't grabbable; make it throwable, gone within 1 s once it crosses 1 m, and short throws bounce with realistic physics. (a) Grab: xr.js hitMovable used the old fixed box (x/z 0.19, up to 0.29 m) but #193 made the crate 0.47 long and 0.306 tall, so its ends and rim were outside it; the box now follows MILK (W/2 and D/2 + 3 cm, from 2 cm under to 6 cm over the rim). Catching a crate mid-bounce stops its flight. (b) main.js releaseMilk: thrown (over 0.5 m/s) or let go in the air, it flies (stepFlyingMilk, 120 Hz substeps): gravity, bounce keeps 35 % of the landing speed (while over 0.6 m/s), each landing trims 30 % of its sideways speed and kicks a little tilt, then it skids to a stop (friction 0.45), spin fades, tilt springs back flat on its base (corners never sink into the floor). It lands on anything under it (stackFloor) and knocks back off the sides of the case / stacks. (c) Past 1 m horizontally from your head it keeps moving while shrinking away over 1 s (was 0.6 m / 0.2 s); an extra crate is then deleted with its records, the first crate ('milk') pops back in beside the record crate (placeMilk, shared with spawnMilk). Tested on localhost: 1.2 m drop = one ~12 cm bounce, corner rock, rest in 1.4 s; short toss stays, rests in 1.8 s; 4 m/s throw and a toss drifting past 1 m both shrink away; the first crate returns. Backup backups/pre-200. Deployed main.js + xr.js to cly3dj-dev.
201. (30 Sep) Owner: (a) the tablet's translucent screen wasn't flush (the #115 glass plate stood 3 mm off the face and nearly as big as the body); make it sit inside the frame. models.js: the body is now one merged mesh with a 1 mm recess and a bezel (5 mm sides, 2.5 mm top/bottom); screen on the recess floor, glass exactly the recess size (0.19 x 0.075) 0.3 mm below the bezel. (b) 'The crate falls through the floor': the floor itself holds (rig y 0 = the Quest's floor); two causes fixed in main.js. The #200 shrink-away scaled the crate about its base origin, so it looked like it sank into the floor: now it shrinks about its middle (visual lift, f.off). And a fast drop could tunnel through a case / crate top (tops only counted within 3 cm of the bottom after the step): now anything below the bottom before the step catches it. Tested: tablet edge-on shows the bezel and recess; a 3 m/s drop from 2.2 m lands on deck B on the case; a shrink never dips below the floor. Backup backups/pre-201. Deployed models.js + main.js to cly3dj-dev.
202. (30 Sep) Owner: put the silver L and its embossed arrow on the TOP-right corner so people see the whole tablet lifts out; resizable only once out of its dock. main.js: L drawn with its arm along the top, arrow on the joint now points up and out (up-right); plate at (0.092, 0.032); TAB_CORNER (0.095, 0.035); xr.js resize zone moved to match. Landscape VIDEO button moved 22 px in (clear of the L). Portrait: the L is top-left (+x edge up) or bottom-right (-x edge up), so MR GUI and the Video tabs start 22-24 px in and the LED button sits 26 px from the right. Resize gating was already in place (#195): the corner drag and the two-grip stretch only start when the tablet is out of the slot; docked, a trigger/pinch on the L does nothing special and a grip/pinch just picks it up, and docking always resets it to size 1. Backup backups/pre-202. Deployed main.js + xr.js to cly3dj-dev.
203. (30 Sep) Owner: the phone's screen recordings flicker (gear missing on some frames; the Quest preview never does), and the preview is a narrow sideways strip when the phone films landscape. Frame check of flicker_recording.mp4: ~12 fps, 1080x2408 portrait, 9 of 16 frames around 20 s had no gear at all = missed AR frames (Chrome then shows the bare camera). Built the in-app recorder route (owner chose it) plus the frame fixes:
  - recorder.js (new): MediaRecorder on a 2D canvas (captureStream(0) + requestFrame per composed frame), MP4/H.264 when Chrome has it (else WebM), phone mic optional (asked on Start camera), ~6 Mbit/s at 720p / ~11 at 1080p. Resolution 720p / 1080p / Full (camera picture's short side); short side = the setting, the long side follows the view (720 x 1608 on the M33). Written to OPFS 'vire-media/Takes' while recording (complete once stopped), thumbnail made, then downloaded to Downloads if 'save' is on. Held on its side at REC start = recorded landscape and upright (orientation locked for the take).
  - spectator-client.js: compose(W, H) renders camera picture (vset quad, raw when the set is off) + gear with the XR view's own pose/projection (viewCam, not last frame's XR camera) into the WebGL canvas's default framebuffer, BEFORE the AR render; copied to 2D canvases (GPU drawImage). Preview now uses it too (no readPixels, 4 a second, turned upright by rotDir, short side 300); while recording the preview reuses the recorded frame. Late-frame counter (frame interval > 1.5 x median) shown on the phone and in the Quest status. Phone bar: ● REC (red ■ m:ss while on) and resolution; a small red REC badge stays on screen in clean mode. Start screen: mic sound and save-to-Downloads checkboxes. Recording stops on AR exit or when the page is hidden.
  - vset.js: Look match's camera sample read back asynchronously (WebGL2 pixel buffer + fence, collected a frame or two later); camera picture grabbed whenever the recorder runs (S.recNeed), camera size kept (S.camW/H).
  - main.js CAMERA tab: ● REC / ■ m:ss and 720P / 1080P / FULL RES buttons (landscape 8 in a row, portrait 3 rows of 3); status adds REC time, MB, size, fps and late frames; last take / recording error on the note line.
  - phonelib.js: 'Takes' tab (phone only) with Save to Downloads and Share; medialib.createWritable. sw.js LATE + recorder.js.
  Tested on the PC: 4 s take in the no-AR path = playable MP4 (3.8 s) in Takes; rotation and sizes checked; CAMERA tab layouts both ways. Not testable here: the AR compose path, frame rate and file on the M33. Backup backups/pre-203. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
204. (1 Oct) Owner's device test of #203: flicker only with the preview on; REC saved only the camera picture; the camera looked blown out; calibration hard because the virtual flight case covers the floor mark. (a) ROOT CAUSE of the flicker: three.js (r169) binds the AR layer's framebuffer once, just before the animation-loop callback; renderer.resetState() (vset.js camera copy, Look match sample) and setRenderTarget() (preview / compose) inside the callback unbound it, so that frame's AR render went into the hidden canvas and the phone showed the bare camera. Every preview frame, every 8th frame with Look match, and EVERY frame with the virtual set on (so the set never showed on the device). Fix: spectator-client keeps renderer.getRenderTarget() at the start of the callback and re-binds it just before the AR render. (b) compose(): clears to opaque black (AR clears to alpha 0; the copy of a see-through canvas came out washed out = the blown-out camera) and reuses the AR frame's shadow map (no second 2048 shadow render). Gear missing from REC not reproduced on the PC (compose draws gear correctly there); likely the same framebuffer mix-up, to re-test. (c) Calibration: in place / calibrate / fix modes every rig material goes 50 % see-through (transparent, no depth write) and is restored on leaving (ghost()). Tested on the PC: compose with gear, ghost on / off (80 materials). Deployed spectator-client.js to cly3dj-dev.
205. (1 Oct) Owner: jitter_rec.mp4 (in-app take) = gear present but 5 fps average (1608 x 720, 22 s), camera blown out, gear twitching on a tripod. Decision: back to the native AR view + Samsung screen recorder, in-app recorder deleted for good. Removed: recorder.js (moved to _to_delete in both folders), compose(), REC / resolution buttons (phone bar + CAMERA tab), Takes tab, recNeed; medialib.dropTakes() frees the phone storage the takes used. Preview = the original #188 method again (render to an sRGB target after the AR frame, readPixels, JPEG, ~150 ms), plus: turned upright when the phone is held on its side (rotDir from the XR camera's roll, 55/35 deg dead zone). Kept: #204 AR framebuffer re-bind (the real flicker fix), Look match's background read-back, the late-frame counter. Calibration view (place / calibrate / fix) is now outline boxes only: every rig material stops writing colour / depth, and each top-level piece of gear gets a 25 % cyan box outline in its own frame (15 boxes on the default stage); restored after. Backup backups/pre-205. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
206. (1 Oct) Owner: a media sync between the Quest's and the phone's libraries, adding only; then (mid-build) as a page listing both libraries with multi-select. Built: SYNC tab on the mixer's VIDEO page (landscape: after CAMERA; portrait: tabs now 3 rows, MIXER last, thumbnails 10 a page). It lists every item of both libraries (Pano, Video pano, Video, Images, plus the Quest's VideoVinyl clips stored with its songs when the source is Headset, offered as Video) with QUEST / PH marks; default shows only items missing on one side (SHOW ALL toggles); tap rows to select, SELECT ALL, REFRESH, COPY n. Each picked item goes to the side that lacks it, one at a time; a name on both sides is never overwritten, nothing is deleted. spectator-host.js: syncList (asks the phone 'mls?'), syncCopy: phone -> Quest = 'mpull' (phone runs its #185 push via phonelib sendOne, resumable), Quest -> phone = 'qput' / 'qgo' / 'qok' + chunks on the 'file' channel (hostLink gained sendBin); progress and last result shown on the tab, toasts suppressed during sync. spectator-client.js: answers 'mls?', receives 'qput' (medialib.beginReceive, resumable), onBinary handler. phonelib.js: push refactored into sendOne(), pull() queue with wake lock. VideoVinyl by title now also finds Library Video clips on both sides (Quest vvSource falls back to libVV; phone onVV to its libVV), so synced clips play as VVs. Tested on the PC with two tabs over the real link: one image each side, SELECT ALL + COPY, both ended with both images (thumbnails too). Backup backups/pre-206. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
207. (1 Oct) Owner: remove the milk crate physics; the #200 bounce / fall went through real furniture in passthrough. main.js: releaseMilk now only settles onto a case / crate top within 6 cm (settleStack) and otherwise stays where it was let go, upright (like the other gear). Tossing still removes an extra crate: let go faster than 1.2 m/s (or dragged beyond 3 m on the desktop) = it shrinks away where it is in 0.3 s and is deleted with its records; the first crate is never removed. Kept from #200: the grab box sized to the real crate. Removed: stepFlyingMilk, gravity / bounce / skid / tumble, the 1 m vanish radius. Tested: first crate let go at 0.8 m with a fast hand stays at 0.8 m; a tossed extra crate is removed. Backup backups/pre-207. Deployed main.js to cly3dj-dev.
208. (1 Oct) Fixed camera mode on the phone (plan fixed-cam-switcher-plan.md, build step 1). New js/fixedcam.js; start screen: "Fixed camera: tripod, no AR" + main / selfie choice. Plain camera feed (getUserMedia, 1920x1080 ideal, exact facing with a fallback) as scene.background (VideoTexture, 'cover' crop via texture repeat / offset); the gear drawn from one fixed camera pose in the Quest's rig space (rig at the origin), the camera's projection matching the crop exactly (setViewOffset on the full-video fov). Tap calibration: tap mark n on the screen -> the Quest asks to touch MARK n (new cal step 'pt') -> trigger -> calpt; Solve after 4+ marks: Levenberg-Marquardt on pixel error over position, yaw / pitch / roll and focal length from 216 starting guesses (synthetic test: exact pose from 4 coplanar floor marks, 0.3-0.5 px with 1 px noise, <300 ms); taps (yellow rings) vs solved marks (green squares) shown 8 s; error in px to the Quest ('done' px). Manual nudges (hold to repeat; fine 1 cm / 0.2 deg / 0.4 deg zoom, coarse 5 cm / 1 deg / 2 deg) in the camera's own axes. Saved per camera (vire.fixcam.main / .selfie). Exposure / focus / white-balance lock and exposure +/- where Chrome's track capabilities allow. Swap test times main <-> selfie reopen. Preview to the tablet = the canvas itself scaled (program picture). Quest blue outline via 'cam' state (crop fov / aspect). cst reports fixed / calibrated. Tested in the cloud container (Playwright + SwiftShader, fake camera stream with 5 drawn marks): full tap flow solved to 0 px, gear lines up. Not tested on the M33 (camera, lock, swap timing).
209. (1 Oct) PerfCap phase 1 (perfcap-plan.md). New js/perfcap.js: records the exact messages the spectator link sends the phone (layout, full snapshots, state packets with nodes / records / head / hands, led, sky, records' grooves and labels, mr) whether or not a phone is connected (spectator-host 'out' sender: phone + PerfCap; tick runs while recording), so a render page can replay them through the phone's mirror code. Format: OPFS vire-perf/<take>/part-NNN.gz, gzip NDJSON lines { t, c, m }, a new part every 5 minutes (a crash loses at most the open part); header line { k:'perfcap', v:1, wall, rate, marks }, end line. SAVE TAKE downloads <take>.c3perf ('C3PERF1' line, JSON index of part sizes, parts back to back); perf.read() reads a take or that file back. MARKS mode: each trigger press records a numbered floor mark (rig space) into the take and keeps it for later takes. Tablet CAMERA tab: ● PERFCAP / ■ m:ss, MARKS n, SAVE TAKE (landscape 9 in a row; portrait 3 rows of 3); status line shows PerfCap time and size. Needs Settings > Spectator camera On (the sampler lives in the spectator host). Tested in the container: a take with header, layout, full, state, led, sky, 2 marks, end; downloaded file read back. Backups backups/pre-208. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
210. (1 Oct) Spatial anchor for the gear (owner: gear moved after re-centring). main.js: the rig is pinned with a WebXR anchor ('anchors' optional feature): on session start saved persistent handles (localStorage vire.rigAnchors, newest first, max 6 rooms) are tried with restorePersistentAnchor; if none restores, a new anchor is made at the rig's start pose on the next XR frame and requestPersistentHandle saves it. anchorFollow() each XR frame sets rig position + yaw only (stays upright); untracked frames keep the last pose. GEAR HERE on the mixer screen (XR only, tap twice within 3 s) deletes the old anchor, puts the rig in front of the head facing the DJ (usual -0.05/-0.62 offset turned by head yaw, y 0) and re-pins. Pin status text beside it. The rig can now turn, so spectator-host.js converts tips, hand joints, grips and head with rig.worldToLocal + inverse rig quaternion (was a position subtraction). Deployed to cly3dj-dev (main.js, spectator-host.js). Untested on the Quest. Owner's question on calibration tapes answered: any 4 to 6 sharp natural points work; tapes only make them easy to find and touch.
211. (1 Oct) Auto points for the fixed camera calibration (owner: why tapes, let the phone pick points). fixedcam.js: Calibrate now runs a Shi-Tomasi corner detector on the visible camera crop (480 px wide grey copy, 5x5 window, NMS, sub-pixel), picks 6 spread-out corners (greedy: distance to the chosen ones x sqrt(strength), min 8% of the diagonal), numbered left to right, each refined at full camera resolution. For each point the phone sends the Quest {k:'cal', step:'pt', n, of, img} where img = JPEG of the whole shot with the point ringed + a 4x close-up with a crosshair (about 10 KB); spectator-host.js shows it on a second panel under the prompt text. Trigger = touched (pairs automatically, next point), Quest grip (squeezestart) sends {k:'calskip'} = swap for the best spare corner; phone buttons Skip point / New points (re-detect, keeps touched ones) / Undo / Solve; tapping the screen moves the current point to the tap, snapped to the sharpest corner within 25 screen px. Auto-solves after the last point. Fewer than 4 corners found = falls back to the old tap-your-own-marks flow (taps now also snap to a corner within 12 px). Container test (synthetic 3D scene streamed as the camera): 6 of 6 picks were real 3D corners, solve 1.2 px, camera position 1.1 cm off, fov 52.2 vs 52. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
212. (1 Oct) Gear hidden during the fixed camera point calibration, on both devices (owner OK'd): real corners under the virtual decks / case were covered in the phone view and in passthrough. spectator-client.js: the loop calls ghost(fixed.on ? !!fixed.S.cal : GHOST.has(mode)) (ghost() is a no-op when unchanged), so the existing #205 ghost (colour + depth writes off, 25% cyan box per top-level gear piece) runs while S.cal exists; the gear returns when it solves or is cancelled (so the green-square check shows with the gear back). spectator-host.js: the same ghost() for the Quest's rig (skips lights, boxes under 2 cm or over 6 m), on from the first 'pt' prompt until 'done', cancel or link close. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
213. (1 Oct) Owner test of fixed camera (selfie, ~40 fps on the HUD): (a) HUD camera status said 'no camera access' + look 'Start the camera first' in fixed mode (those are AR-only fields). cst now carries fcam / fpx / fcal; main.js shows 'Phone N fps · fixed camera (selfie|main) · calibrated X px | calibrating | not calibrated', extra line 'look match / key are AR-only for now'. (b) Auto points gave a lens outline in the wrong place: 4 to 6 points fit a wrong pose closely (low px, position several cm off; container test without lens: 0.5 px but 7.7 cm off). Fix: Calibrate step 1 = touch the phone's own lens (new step 'flens' on the Quest, text names FRONT (selfie) / BACK (main); grip or Skip lens skips it). solvePose(pts, pix, W, H, lens) adds 3 residuals (position - lens) x 600 px/m and starts from the lens; rms reported from the pixel part only. Container: with lens 0.1 to 1 cm off (6 points, 1 cm touch noise). Outlier drop: with 6+ points (5+ with lens) and rms > 2 px, leave-one-out; drops a point if the fit improves by more than half (without the lens only from 7 points: fewer always 'improve'). (c) Mark my own: button during auto calibration switches to tap-your-own marks (keeps touched pairs; Quest gets step 'hold' = prompt hidden, gear stays ghosted). Taps snap to a corner within 12 px. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
214. (1 Oct) Look without AR for fixed camera mode, all set from the Quest (owner: control everything from the Mixer HUD while watching the phone's preview, push media if needed). New js/fixlook.js (phone): per camera (localStorage vire.fixlook.main / .selfie) F = { on, strength, grain, amb, kTurn, kHeight, kInt, kTemp, kRGB, env, envTurn }. (1) Camera grade + grain: lookmatch.js reused, look.st.active set in fixed mode, a 32x18 drawImage of the fixed video every 4th frame -> look.onCamera (its 0.8 s smoothing keeps strobes from flickering the gear); look.L.on/strength/grain driven from F (not saved to vire.look; restored on exit). (2) 360 light photo: F.env = 'Pano/<name>' from the phone's library -> leftEyeCanvas + brightestDir -> envLight.setMix(1) + setImage (light and reflections only, never shown), scene.environmentRotation.y = envTurn; 'keyFromPano' aims the key at the brightest spot (turned with the photo, height clamped 20-80 deg) and takes its colour (60 % towards it, kRGB; a kTemp step clears it). (3) Manual key: turn / height / power / colour temperature (Tanner Helland fit), ambient = scene.environmentIntensity. Defaults = the key light's normal place, so nothing jumps. Restored (key, look.L, env mix, environment rotation) when fixed mode stops. spectator-client: flook.sync(fixed.on, facing) + flook.frame() before look.frame; cset what 'flook' (key, v) -> flook.set; cst gains FL (state) + flErr; window.spect.flook. fixedcam exposes video. main.js: new LOOK tab on the VIDEO page (7 tabs; landscape widths shrunk, portrait MIXER moved to row 3 col 2): LOOK / PREVIEW / PHOTO KEY / PHOTO OFF / RESET + 8 steppers (Grade, Grain, Ambient, Photo turn | Light turn, Light height, Light power, Light colour), status line names the camera and the photo. PANO tab gains LIGHT PHONE: needs the phone in fixed mode; spect.syncCopy([{f, n, q:true, p:false}]) copies it (phone answers qok at once if it already has it), then cset flook env. sw.js LATE += js/fixlook.js. Container tests: phone page (fake camera) grade samples flow, manual light + photo load + photo key work, restore on stop; HUD tab drawn in both orientations, steppers send cset. Untested on devices. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
215. (1 Oct) Controller and hand models vendored (owner: no online fetches). three's XRControllerModelFactory / XRHandModelFactory fetched profiles + GLBs from cdn.jsdelivr.net at runtime. Now web/vendor/webxr-input-profiles (from @webxr-input-profiles/assets 1.0, MIT, README.txt): meta-quest-touch-plus (Quest 3/3S), meta-quest-touch-pro, oculus-touch-v3 (Quest 2), generic-hand; profilesList.json trimmed to these four (2.6 MB). Downloaded through the desktop browser pane (the cloud container and the device VM can't reach jsdelivr) into Downloads, moved in. xr.js: cmf.setPath(PROF) with PROF = new URL('../vendor/webxr-input-profiles', import.meta.url) (no trailing slash: motion-controllers adds '/'), hmf.setPath(PROF + '/generic-hand/'). Other controllers fall back to 'generic-trigger', which isn't shipped: no model drawn (caught), input still works. sw.js LATE += profilesList, touch-plus profile/left/right, hand left/right. Verified on the owner's PC in the browser pane against the Node server (localhost:8000/vire/, RTX 4050): fetchProfile resolves all three controller profiles to the local GLBs, GLTFLoader loads touch-plus, hands 200. Note: the browser pane refuses service workers ('unknown error fetching the script'), a pane limit, not the app. Testing note: the browser pane runs on the owner's PC with his GPU and reaches localhost:8000 when the Node server is up: use it for render / visual tests; the cloud container (SwiftShader) only for headless logic. Deployed to cly3dj-dev (js/xr.js, sw.js, vendor/webxr-input-profiles/).
216. (1 Oct) Release: cly3dj-dev copied to cly3dj (22 files: fixedcam, fixlook, perfcap, main, spectator-client/host, xr, sw, vendor/webxr-input-profiles), CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; local commit 529ca04 in dirrogate.github.io (author Dirrogate via -c, no global git identity in the VM), main ahead of origin by 1: owner pushes in GitHub Desktop. Project doc cly3dj-project.md updated to #215.
217. (1 Oct) Owner test (fixed camera, selfie): calibration and Look OK, but pulling a record from the crate froze the Quest. Cause: #209 introduced `out` (the link + PerfCap sender) while recordsTick kept a local array also named `out`, so out.send('ctl', rec/renv/rart/recdel) threw 'out.send is not a function' whenever a record appeared or left with the spectator on; spect.tick threw every frame before renderer.render, so the picture froze. Not the library size, not the copy itself. Fix: local array renamed rows. main.js: spect.tick wrapped in try/catch (one toast, console.error) so a spectator error can never stop the headset's frame again. Verified in the browser pane on the owner's PC (localhost:8000/vire/, RTX 4050): Spectator on + PerfCap, pullSelected -> frames keep running (~150 fps), rec + renv recorded, 78 state packets with record rows. Also: fixed camera nudges from the Quest: LOOK tab gets NUDGE > page (◀ LOOK, PREVIEW, CALIBRATE / CANCEL CAL, STEP fine/coarse, RESET VIEW tap twice) with Move, Up/down, Near/far, Zoom, Turn, Tilt, Roll (one step per tap). cset what 'fnudge' -> fixed.nudge / setCoarse / resetView / calStart|calCancel; cst gains FX { fov, coarse, cal }; fixedcam exports nudge, setCoarse, resetView, calCancel, coarse. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
218. (1 Oct) Record crate on the spectator phone: real sleeve covers, the picked record's label and the lid screen. main.js: Quest layoutSleeves ends with spect.crate(crateView(...)) = { k:'crate', n, sel, shut, items: [[idx, key, title, artist, bpm, missing]] for the visible slots }; key = coverKey(track) = FNV hash of title|artist|duration (stable across re-imports); coverTracks map key -> track; coverFor(key) = fetchArt + artBlobs. drawCrateScreen ends with spect.crateScreen(canvas). Camera role: currentList() returns remoteCrate.list (sparse array, window only), crateRemote.onCrate builds it, sets crateState.sel, layoutSleeves (shut from the Quest, not the phone's lid state); fetchArt -> phoneArt: OPFS 'vire-covers/<key>.jpg', else asks the Quest ('cover?'), 15 s timeout; drawCrateScreen returns early (onScreen draws the Quest's JPEG). spectator-host.js: crateTick in tick sends the crate view when its JSON changes, lid screen as a 512x480 JPEG at most every 0.4 s when dirty; covers 256 px JPEG q0.82 on request (queue, 400-entry cache); PerfCap: each crate key's cover written once per take (perf only); resend on link open / perfBegin. PREP COVERS button on the SYNC tab (portrait 'COVERS'): 'covhave?' / 'covhave' (500 keys per ask), then sends the missing ones, progress in the status line, tap again to stop. Verified in the browser pane on the owner's PC (RTX 4050, Node localhost:8000/vire/): host tab + phone tab linked over the real handshake; crate view mirrored (sel 5, picked record 'Alfalfa' label), 10 real covers, lid screen identical; PREP COVERS on the 724-track collection: 712 covers (12 have none), 9.9 MB, 14 KB average, about 40 s streamed from the PC; host stayed at 80+ fps. Deployed to cly3dj-dev.
219. (1 Oct) Release: cly3dj-dev (#217 freeze fix + HUD nudges, #218 crate on the phone) copied to cly3dj (js/fixedcam.js, main.js, spectator-client.js, spectator-host.js); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
220. (1 Oct) DJ CAM on the LED wall + avatar, Quest only (owner; plan in project doc quest-director-plan.md). The Meta cast only carries the DJ's eye view (no separate spectator output for WebXR, unlike VRL's native desktop mirror), so a virtual camera is rendered onto the LED wall; when the DJ looks at the wall it is in the cast. New js/director.js: Avatar (models/avatar/dirroface.glb, the owner's Ready Player Me rig, 67 bones, 14.7k tris, unchanged for now) on layer AV_LAYER = 6, so the XR eye cameras never draw it and only the DJ CAM camera does. Solver per frame from xrPose (XR camera = head; hand joints or controller grips; handedness picks the side): scale = standing eye height / 1.726 (clamped 0.8-1.3; standing height rises at once, sinks 4 mm/s), body yaw follows the head and is pulled half way toward the hands (max 0.9 rad off the head), hips follow lazily (0.22 m leash) and crouch when the head drops > 12 cm, spine (3 bones) bent so hips -> eyes points at the real eyes, neck 40 % of the head turn, head exact (camera * F^-1 * rest), then the root shifted so the eyes sit exactly on the real eyes; two-bone arm IK (elbows down / out / back), hand orientation from fingers + index->pinky basis (same construction for rest and target), fingers aimed along the tracked phalanges (hands) or curled by grip / trigger (controllers); legs two-bone IK to planted feet under the hips, feet flat; mouthOpen from the MIC level. Desktop: demoPose (a DJ nodding, left hand scratching deck A, right on the mixer). DjCam: PerspectiveCamera in rig space, 7 presets from the live gear positions (FRONT, WIDE, LOW, PLATTERS, FADERS, CRATE, TOP), slight hand-held drift; renders into a 1024 x 576 HalfFloat MSAA target every 3rd headset frame (30 fps) / every 2nd on desktop, Reflector pattern (xr.enabled off, shadow autoUpdate off, restore the XR render target). While it renders it hides the LED wall, the XR hand / controller models, tip dots and hand occluders, and the neon sign for FRONT / LOW / FADERS (its back faces those angles); in passthrough it adds the studio floor and dark backdrop. ledwall.js setCam(tex, mirror): tone-mapped material (the target is linear HDR), MIRROR flips left / right. main.js: LED WALL cycles OFF -> CLIPS -> DECKS -> CAM -> OFF; new DJ CAM tab on the VIDEO page (LED CAM, MIRROR, AVATAR, the 7 angles, status with cam ms), tabs narrowed to fit 8 (portrait: MIXER moved to column 3); settings in 'vire.djcam' {preset, mirror (default on), avatar}. PC test (RTX 4050, desktop): avatar loads, all angles framed, cam render ~1.5 ms CPU per frame, no errors; first CAM frame compiles shaders (~0.6 s stall on the PC, expect more on the Quest: switch CAM on before the set). Not tested on the Quest: frame rate with CAM on, real head / hands / controllers driving the avatar, Meta cast of the wall. No Node restart needed (static files). Backup: backups/pre-220.
221. (2 Oct) Robot DJ avatar for the DJ CAM (owner: the Ready Player Me human looked wrong at the elbows and face). The owner asked for a Daft-Punk-like helmet like Vinyl Reality's; declined (recognisable design, and our copy-nothing-from-VRL rule) and built an original instead, owner's picks: faceted visor helmet, pearl white, human-skull proportions, angular jaw, fixed slit mouth, short neck, no neck ring, his logo (chest_logo.png, the Gemini crest) embossed on the chest plate, scanner bar that sweeps while he talks. Modelled in Blender 5.1 on the owner's PC by script: blender/robot/robot_build.py (source of truth) + robot.blend. Head = loft of filleted polygon cross-sections through a profile table (KEYS: z, width, face width, front y, back y); LOW = one vertex per crease corner on 12 rings (edges follow the creases = retopo mesh), HIGH = same loft at 260 x ~175 with fillets, crown seams, screws, raised crown strip, mouth slit; slot cut at eye level (+-11 mm) between the two side corners with 10 mm walls; visor glass band and 15 scanner cells (cell i has uv ((i + .5) / 15, .5)). Hex temple pods (high: bevels + boolean vent slats). Bust = faceted torso (ellipsoid cut by planes, smooth-min edges) with chest-plate outline + rivets; logo as bump from the PNG alpha in the high material; short graphite neck. Gloves: FingerSeg (along +Z, 30 mm), Knuckle, Tip, Cuff, Palm, HandBack. One shared 2048 bake on the RTX 4050 (OptiX, ~50 s): tangent normal, diffuse colour, AO (lows hidden from rays), roughness, metal (emission trick) -> tex/robot_n.png, robot_c.png, robot_orm.png (R AO, G rough, B metal); parts spread apart while baking, cage 15 mm / 35 mm rays. Export web/models/avatar/robot.glb (3.9 MB, textures 1024 PNG, ~1.7k triangles in all): Helmet (+pods), Visor_x, Scanner_x, Bust, FingerSeg, Knuckle, Tip, Cuff, Palm, HandBack, one material Robot (colour, ORM, normal, clearcoat). web/js/robot.js RobotAvatar.load(): head group at the XR eye pose; bust at eyes + 7 cm back - 12.5 cm down, yaw-smoothed, slight pitch; scanner = MeshBasic with a 15 x 1 DataTexture (sweep speed / brightness from the MIC level, slow dim sweep when quiet); visor = dark transparent glass; gloves = InstancedMesh segments (15), knuckles (15), tips (5) + palm, back plate (flipped for the right hand), cuff, driven by hand joints or synthJoints() for controllers / desktop. main.js: DJ CAM tab STYLE: ROBOT / HUMAN (default robot, 'vire.djcam'.style); avatars made on first use. PC test: loads, ~2.9 ms cam frame, no errors. Notes: the Claude file-commit bridge writes the previous upload on the first call; commit twice and check md5. Not tested on the Quest. No Node restart needed. Backups: backups/pre-221.
222. (2 Oct) Offline start fixed (owner outdoors, no Wi-Fi: the installed app showed the browser's no-internet page). Cause: the service worker filed the page under the exact address it was opened with; visits to .../cly3dj/ were saved under that key only, and the home-screen icon opens start_url index.html, which had no saved copy. sw.js rework: (a) every page address in scope (folder, index.html, any ?query) is saved and served under one key, <scope>index.html; redirected responses are stored as clean copies (a redirected response can't answer a navigation); (b) install precaches the whole app (APP list: pages, manifest, icons, all js, all models incl. avatars, vendor three files, controller / hand profiles; ~90 files, ~22 MB) instead of only what a session used; keep APP in step when files are added; (c) network first but never waits more than 2.5 s: on a Wi-Fi with no internet (hotspot without data) requests used to hang; after one slow or failed request it serves saved copies straight away for 30 s and refreshes them in the background; (d) /api/ (PC server taps) is never cached. Cache name unchanged (vire-app-v1) so older saved copies stay. Tested headless with mocks (folder visit then offline index.html, ?role=camera, a module, hang case 2.5 s then instant, missing file fails). The Claude browser pane can't register service workers (Electron), so not tested there. The Quest needs one online visit after the push for the new worker to install and save everything. No Node restart needed. Backup: backups/pre-222.
222. (2 Oct) Four owner asks. (1) LED wall portrait: grab the middle of the screen (|x| < 22 % of W, |y| < 30 % of H, wall-local) with grip / trigger and twist the wrist: ledwall.rotation.z follows the hand's X axis measured in the wall's un-turned frame (xr.js wallRoll, kind 'ledTurn'); let go = ledTurned(): settles on 0 or +-90 deg (upside-down counts as landscape, eased in ledwall tick), ledBase uses half the width when portrait (raised off the floor if needed), saved. Grabbing nearer the edges still moves it; a second hand still resizes. Desktop: double-click the wall. ledwall.js: the screen (and DECKS screenB) counter-turns by the settled angle so the picture stays upright, and every picture is now fitted WHOLE at its own aspect ('contain', was 'cover' crop) by scaling the screen plane (the dark frame shows as bars); tick() per frame (stepWallGlow, so Quest and phone) refits when the turn or the picture's shape changes; screen / screenB are noMirror (the link carries only a uniform scale; each side fits its own). layout.js: items keep a 5th value = rotation.z when non-zero (snapshot / apply), so the phone gets it in 'layout' and in the node mirror. (2) Delete media on the Quest: long press (0.6 s; trigger via the ray, pinch, or fingertip) on a VIDEO page thumbnail = red frame + X; tap X = media.remove + thumb, and it is taken off the LED list, the sky and deck VideoVinyl overrides if used; any other tap cancels. Hold tracking: mixScreenPress starts a 600 ms timer, mixScreenRelease (pointerUp kind 'screen', fingertip leaving the screen in xr.js) cancels it. (3) LED LIST: VIDEO / IMAGES bottom bar button 'LED LIST n' opens the playlist page (rows: thumb, name, play now, up, down, remove; BACK, RANDOM / IN ORDER, NEXT, LED mode). LedPlayer gains order ('random' | 'list'), next(), remove(), move(). Kept between sessions in 'vire.ledList' ({ order, items: [[folder, name]] }, library items only), restored on load. (4) Unsorted songs: an XML is no longer required for 'This headset' as the library source. library.js addUnsorted(lib, index): every imported audio file not used by the XML becomes a track (file-name title, '_A' / '_B' suffix pairs sides) in a new 'Unsorted (on this headset)' playlist (2nd in the crate) and in the Collection; emptyLibrary() when there is no XML. main.js scanUnsorted reads each file's ID3 (title, artist, BPM, key; not the cover) in the background and re-sorts. Tested in the browser pane on the owner's PC (Node localhost:8000/vire/, Pinggy up, RTX 4050): portrait / landscape with portrait and landscape pictures (upright, whole, letter / pillar-boxed), turn saved and re-applied; long press -> X -> file deleted; LED LIST shows and restores after reload; 3 MP3s imported without XML -> Unsorted with ID3 titles / artists / BPM, one loaded on deck A (186 s). Test data removed and the pane's source set back to PC. Not tested on the Quest: the wrist twist, ray / fingertip long press. Known: Unsorted durations show 00:00 in the crate until the record is loaded. Deployed to cly3dj-dev. Backup: backups/pre-222.
223. (2 Oct) Release: #222 (LED wall portrait turn + contain fit, long-press delete on the VIDEO page, LED LIST, Unsorted songs without XML) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (js/layout.js, ledwall.js, library.js, main.js, xr.js); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
224. (3 Oct) Owner asks: VIDEO menu renamed VISUALS; Pano + Video pano merged into one PANO tab (tiny camcorder / picture badge bottom-right of each thumbnail; actions use the item's own folder; LIGHT PHONE dimmed for a video pano). TOOLS button bottom-left of the mixer HUD opens a tools page: DJ TOOLS > RECORD MAKER (sound = blank generic grooves with crackle, a song from the current list, or a clip from Video; label picture from Images or none; name only; STAMP RECORD saves to OPFS vire-press and adds it to Unsorted), VJ TOOLS > SCROLLER (original 6x7 bitmap font, rainbow sine scroller on the bottom band of the LED wall over whatever plays; WAVE / SPEED; popup keyboard with happy, sad and thumbs-up emoji; ENTER starts it). New js/tools.js (font, Scroller, pressings, blankWav); main.js tools block; sw.js precaches tools. Not on the spectator phone; pressings can't be deleted from the UI yet. Tested on PC: VISUALS/PANO badges + selection, scroller, keyboard, song and blank stamping onto decks.
225. (3 Oct) Owner bug: Record Maker, after naming and STAMP RECORD the tablet showed "(no name yet)". The record was stamped, but the only confirmation was toast(), a desktop-only overlay that is invisible in the headset, and the name was then cleared. Fix: makerSay() shows the answer on the tablet in place of the STAMP button (green "STAMPED ... in the crate, Unsorted", red for errors and "pick a song/clip first"; 6 s, tap to clear). Keyboard ENTER now runs the done callback before redrawing. Owner: the top-level MIXER button renamed DJ MIXER (VISUALS page, landscape 120 px wide, portrait, and the tools pages' header). Lesson: anything the DJ must read in XR goes on the tablet, never only in toast().
226. (3 Oct) Delete stamped records from the crate (owner's design): long press (0.6 s) a stamped record's row on the crate lid screen, a red "X DELETE" appears on that row and a hint in the footer; tap it to delete, tap anywhere else to keep. Only stamped records (r.pressed) offer it; refused while a copy is out (hand, deck, loose). Deletes the pressing (OPFS vire-press index + label), removes it from lib.tracks / records / every playlist / search results. A short tap on the selected stamped record still pulls it, now on release (so a hold doesn't pull it). Release hooks: pointer drag kind 'crateScreen' (mouse and controller ray) and fingertip off the screen in xr.js poke (ctx.crateScreenRelease). Results show in the lid screen's footer (crateSay: green / red, 4 s), since toast() is invisible in the headset. Tested on PC: offer, cancel, short-tap pull, refusal while held, delete.
227. (3 Oct) Blank record crackle (owner: sounded like clicking). Old blankWav: 22.05 kHz, ~7 loud single-sample-decay clicks/s over near-silent hiss. New (tools.js, still generated, not baked: an 8 kHz file would cut everything above 4 kHz, where crackle lives): 44.1 kHz mono, seeded; band-limited hiss breathing once per turn (1.8 s), Poisson crackle bed ~320/s with power-law sizes rung through 2.6 / 4.8 kHz band-passes (gain-normalised biquads), ~1.8 pops/s at 900 Hz, two fixed scratches ticking once per turn (louder in the run-out), sub-40 Hz rumble; gaps at 44/90/135 s keep the groove bands.
