Levels: playing RMS -41 dBFS, peaks -11. Async, yields every 1 s of audio: 1.4 s total in the PC browser with no frame gap over 10 ms. Excerpt for the owner: renders/blank_crackle_test.mp3.
228. (3 Oct) Finger spin (owner; edge hold dropped). xr.js: per hand, index finger knuckle-to-tip direction and "straight" (proximal-to-tip > 5.5 cm, not pinching, tip 5 cm from thumb); exposes fingers(), tips(), buzz. main.js: releaseHeld in XR with the disc within ~45 deg of flat and another hand's upright (within ~35 deg) index tip under the centre hole (3 cm radius, -2..+5 cm along the axis) -> the record becomes a loose entry with L.spin; a falling flat record over such a finger is caught the same way (stepLoose). Start speed = wrist twist at release, at least 9 rad/s (~1.4 turns/s); max 45. stepSpin: centre rides 4 mm above the fingertip; axis leans half with the finger; wobble 0.02 + 1/w rad (max 0.35) precessing faster as it slows; friction 0.25 + 0.035 w rad/s^2 (about 15-20 s from a good spin). Other fingertips / controller tips brushing the rim (within 3 cm of the disc plane, R -3.5..+2.5 cm) add spin along the edge or drag it slower against it. Drops into normal loose physics (finger velocity + spin, pushed the way the finger leans) when: finger tilts > 40 deg, finger acceleration > 45 m/s^2, finger untracked > 0.15 s, or spin < 2.5 rad/s. Pinch to take it (normal loose pick-up). Test hook window.vire._spin. Tested on PC with a fake finger: rides the tip, decays, flick speeds up, tilt drops it, lands.
229. (3 Oct) Sleeve dig (owner's rule: deep in the sleeve = the sleeve, at the top of the popped-up record = only the record). xr.js grabStart 4b (after the popped-record pull, before dig): hand on the selected sleeve (|x| < 14 cm, within 3 cm of its slot along the rack, 5 cm above the floor to 7 cm below the crate's top edge) -> ctx.sleeveGrab: the jacket (1024 px cover from the art cache, plain card back with title) comes up in the hand, keeping its grab pose; the slot's instance is scaled to 0 (layoutSleeves, sleeveOut) and its pool cover skipped. The record peeks 5 cm out of the opening (crate disc geometry/materials reused). Other hand (pinch / grip / trigger) on the exposed record: 'sleeveSlide', the record follows that hand along the sleeve's axis (tick buzz on controllers); at 31 cm it is clear -> pullSelected into that hand and the sleeve flies back to its slot (0.35 s smoothstep). Letting go of the sleeve sends it back with the record. Cost only while a sleeve is out: one box, three shared-material meshes, one 1024 canvas made once. Not mirrored on the spectator phone yet. Test hook window.vire._sleeve. Tested on PC with fake anchors: zones (deep yes, top / off-slot no), lift, slide out 26 cm, record in the second hand, sleeve back, release-without-pull.
230. (3 Oct) Owner: letting go of knobs / faders with bare hands was hit and miss; pitch should semi-lock at zero like an SL1200. Double-pinch-to-lock rejected (each pinch would nudge the control). xr.js: (1) faders and pitch move relative to the grab (v0 + mapped(P) - mapped(P0)); before, the cap jumped to the pinch point. (2) ctlLetGo per hand frame while pinching a knob / slider / pitch: tracks the tightest pinch (dmin) and a 300 ms value history; when the fingers open 5 mm past dmin the control freezes and rolls back to its value ~60 ms earlier; re-closing within 3 mm of dmin resumes with a re-based grab (no jump). Controllers unchanged (their release is clean). main.js setPitch: from the user, |p| < 0.5% clicks to exactly 0 (returns 'detent', the hand code buzzes) and holds until |p| > 1.2% (~4 mm of travel), then lets go; programmatic sets keep pitchLock = (p == 0). Old detent was a plain snap under 0.35% with no hold. Detent tested on PC; the let-go freeze needs the headset.
231. (3 Oct) Sleeve back + separate labels from ID3 picture types (owner). id3.js returns picture (cover: Front 3 > Other 0 > any; Back 4 and Media 6 only as a last resort), back (type 4), media (type 6). main.js: labelArt (track id -> Media bitmap) and backBlobs (track id -> Back blob) filled wherever a tag is read (fetchArt, fetchSide); labelOf(t) = Media else cover, used by the crate disc and returned by fetchSide as the record's label (each side from its own file: A file = sleeve front + back + A label, _b file = B label). Sleeve-dig back canvas now 1024 px, draws the Back cover over the plain card. Built-in example record "Sleeve Art Demo" (web/examples/Sleeve Art Demo_a/_b.mp3, 64 s each, original generated music, built by tools/make_sleeve_demo.py with a hand-written ID3v2.3 tag; previews in renders/sleeve_demo_*.jpg): addExamples puts it in Unsorted + Collection in every library mode; long press + DELETE hides it on that device (localStorage vire.hiddenExamples). Not cached for offline (the worker never caches MP3s). Tested on PC: tags read, both Media labels on the deck, back cover on the lifted sleeve.
232. (3 Oct) Both labels in one MP3 (owner). id3.js reads each picture's description: 'Label B' / 'Side B' (any picture type, also '_'/'-' separators) or a Media picture described just 'B' -> mediaB; 'Label A' / 'Side A' or any other Media picture -> media (A label); Back cover only when not described as a label; labels never become the cover unless nothing else is there. main.js: labelArtB (track id -> B label from inside that file); prepareArt puts the A file's B label on the B face when the record has no _b file (a one-file record's B face: no grooves, label only) or the _b file brought no picture; fetchSide treats a _b file's own 'Label B' as its label. A _b file's own pictures still win. Example "Sleeve Art Demo (One File)" (web/examples, one side): Front cover (sleeve + A label), Back cover (one-file explanation), an 'Other' picture described 'Label B'. Tested on PC: two-file record labels 900/900, one-file 1200 (front) / 900 (Label B).
233. (3 Oct) Release: #224 to #232 (VISUALS + merged PANO, TOOLS: Record Maker / Scroller, tablet result banner + DJ MIXER, crate long-press delete, blank crackle, finger spin, sleeve dig, precise let-go + pitch detent, ID3 sleeve back / labels + example records) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (js/id3.js, main.js, tools.js, xr.js, sw.js, examples/); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop. Not yet tried on the Quest: finger spin, sleeve dig, let-go freeze, pitch detent by hand.
234. (3 Oct) Pictures and videos from the Quest's own files (owner). Start page: "Add pictures / videos…" (input image/*,video/*) -> importMedia: folder by shape (2:1, or a square >= 3840 px = 360: Pano / Video pano; else Images / Video), media.importFile, result in the note. In VR: a "+ IMPORT" tile after the last thumbnail on VISUALS (PANO, VIDEO, IMAGES; tab decides the folder, PANO by type). Browsers open a file picker only inside a user-activation event, and hand pokes come from the frame loop, so xr.js 'down' (select / squeeze event: hand pinch or controller button) calls ctx.tabletImportSelect(pinch point / tip) first, like the crate mic (#73); that pinch is then not also a tablet grab (st.noGrabT). A poke shows a red tablet strip saying to pinch or trigger. Progress / result on the tablet (vpSay). Untested on the Quest: whether Horizon OS shows the picker over an immersive session or pauses it; the start-page route always works. Tested on PC: shape sorting (2:1 -> PANO, 4:3 -> IMAGES), tile, poke message.
235. (3 Oct) Owner: the in-VR + IMPORT tile did nothing on the Quest (Horizon OS doesn't open the file picker from an immersive session) -> removed (tile, xr.js 'down' hook, noGrab); the start page's "Add pictures / videos…" (#234) stays. Stage pieces switchable (owner): VJ TOOLS has LED WALL: ON/OFF and NEON: ON/OFF, DJ TOOLS has MILK CRATE: ON/OFF (the first milk crate; extras still come from the crate screen). settings.pieces, applied at start (applyPiece): off = visible false, every mesh's raycast disabled (restored on), skipped by xr hitMovable, supportUnderItem, the milk record catcher in surfaceUnder and blob shadows; LED off stops the LED mode (videos / cam) and the scroller (SCROLLER button greyed), neon off skips the flicker update. Positions stay in the saved layout. Not mirrored to the spectator phone. Start page re-laid out: launch buttons and library status under the title, then six cards with bold headings (Music library, Turntables, Microphone, Passthrough & spectator camera, Light & surroundings incl. Shadows, Visuals: LED wall, neon & media) in CSS columns (column-width 440 px: two columns on a wide window, one on a narrow one); all 59 element ids unchanged. Tested on PC: one column at 800 px, two at 1280; switches, persistence across reload.
236. (3 Oct) LED wall off on the Quest, still running for the spectator phone (owner). ledwall.js LedPlayer.setSilent: while silent no video is decoded; a clock (clip length from its metadata only) stands in for the video, ends the clip on time (next clip / list order as usual) and gives state().t, so the phone, which plays its own copy (#176), stays in step; switching back on reopens the clip at the clock time. main.js applyPiece('ledwall') -> led.setSilent(!on); DECKS / CAM stop when off (they only ever show on the Quest) and setLedMode refuses them while off. Scroller: created on the phone too; the host sends ctl {k:'scroll', on, text, wave, speed} on change (and every 3 s); the client starts / stops its own copy and ticks it in its loop. On the Quest the scroller keeps running (not drawn) while the LED wall is off; the SCROLLER button stays usable. DJ CAM is not on the phone (rendered on the Quest only). Tested on PC: silent clock (decoder closed, playhead runs, clip ends on time, resumes at the right point). Phone side untested.
237. (3 Oct) Release: #234 to #236 (start page "Add pictures / videos…", two-column start page, LED wall / neon / milk crate switches in TOOLS, LED + scroller kept running for the phone, in-VR import removed) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (index.html, js/ledwall.js, main.js, spectator-client.js, spectator-host.js, xr.js); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
238. (3 Oct) LIVE CAM (owner): the spectator phone's composite (camera + gear) as live video on the Quest's LED wall. net-link.js: the phone's offer carries a sendonly video transceiver (empty) so the picture starts / stops mid-set with replaceTrack, no new handshake; the encoding is capped at 2 Mbit/s and 30 fps, priority / networkPriority very-low, degradationPreference maintain-framerate; 'state' and 'ctl' data channels ask for high priority. Quest: LED mode LIVE (OFF > CLIPS > DECKS > CAM > LIVE, LIVE only offered while the phone is linked), liveVideo from pc.ontrack; entering LIVE sends ctl {k:'live', on}; leaving it (any mode, LED LIST play / NEXT / LED NOW via clipsMode, LED wall switched off, phone gone) sends off. Phone: on 'live' it streams liveOut (2D canvas captureStream + requestFrame): fixed mode = its screen canvas scaled to 854 px long side at ~30 fps; AR mode = the AR view rendered again at 480 px tall (like the preview) with renderer.readRenderTargetPixelsAsync, ~20 fps, turned upright on its side; its own LED wall shows a Cly3DJ ● LIVE card (scroller still over it), so no feedback tunnel. Found while testing: a // comment inserted mid-line in a one-line object literal broke the module (node --check in script mode didn't catch it; check with node --input-type=module --check). Tested on PC: the WebRTC video path in one page (track ready at connect, start / stop mid-session, params accepted, 29 fps decoded). The two-tab phone link did not connect in the browser pane (relay never reached the background host tab; no errors); the full Quest + phone run is untested.
239. (3 Oct) SOLO (owner): LED mode after LIVE, the phone's camera picture only, no gear, to walk the phone round for a pure live view. Quest: modes OFF > CLIPS > DECKS > CAM > LIVE > SOLO (LIVE and SOLO only while the phone is linked; isLive() covers both, a hoisted function because applyPiece runs at start before the LED code); ctl {k:'live', on, solo}. Phone: fixed mode draws the camera video itself (fixed.video) into the stream canvas; AR mode renders only the camera quad (vs.quad on layer 30, pvCam.layers.set(30), raw camera even with the virtual set on), so SOLO costs less than LIVE. Same stream, cap and priorities as #238; the phone's own LED wall shows the LIVE card. Tested on PC: Quest page starts with the LED wall switched off, phone page loads. End-to-end with the phone untested.
240. (3 Oct) Owner: LED mode names on the tablet: DECKS -> VIDVINYL, CAM -> AVACAM (LED button: LED OFF / CLIPS / VIDVINYL / AVACAM / LIVE / SOLO; DJ CAM tab button LED AVACAM). Labels only: the internal modes stay 'decks' / 'cam', so saved state and the phone link are unchanged. The DJ CAM tab keeps its name.
241. (3 Oct) Owner: the VISUALS tab DJ CAM is now AVACAM (label only; the internal tab key stays 'DJ Cam').
242. (3 Oct) Owner tweaks. (1) Finger spin: falls off past ~60 deg of finger tilt (SPIN.TILT_DROP 1.05 rad, was 0.7). (2) Sleeve dig grab zone covers the whole sleeve incl. edges and lower corners (|x| < 17.75 cm, crate floor up to 7 cm under the top edge), so a corner grab makes turning it to read the back easy; the popped-up record still only pulls the record. (3) Sleeves in the open, no physics: sleeves[] (each its own card box, 1024 px front and back, a disc with the record's own label, disposed when it goes home); let go = it stays exactly there (case, milk crate, real table in passthrough); let go over the open record crate = back in; at most 3 lying about, a 4th sends the oldest back (fly-back to its slot, shrinks away if its record isn't in the current list). A lying sleeve: grab anywhere on it to pick it up (checked first in grabStart), or slide its record out with any hand (the empty sleeve stays). Records come out of a sleeve via pullRecord(r) (pullSelected uses it too), so it works whatever list the crate shows; a record whose sleeve is out doesn't pop up in the crate. (4) tidyRecords() on every VR / passthrough start (the Quest can keep the page alive between sessions; nothing about records is saved): sleeves, the held record, loose / spinning / milk-crate records go back; a deck keeps its record only while its motor runs. Tested on PC with fake anchors: corner zone, 4 sleeves -> 3 kept (oldest home), slide out of a lying sleeve, re-grab, tidy.
243. (3 Oct) Kinematic sleeves (owner: no gravity, but no cutting through the decks). Colliders = decks, mixer, flight case as oriented boxes measured from their visible meshes when a sleeve is grabbed (refreshSleeveColliders; records, the tablet and invisible parts skipped). Not colliders: record crate and milk crates (sleeves go in there), other sleeves, records. A held sleeve moves toward the hand pose in steps of at most 1.5 cm / 10 deg (up to 40 a frame) and after each step is pushed out of every box along the shortest separating axis (OBB SAT, 15 axes, up to 4 passes), so it stops at a surface and slides along it while the hand goes through, and catches up when the hand returns; first contact buzzes the controller (xr.buzzAnchor). Bug found while testing: a step length tied to the remaining distance grew while the sleeve was blocked and the last step leapt through the deck; fixed by a hard 1.5 cm cap. Let go = stays put (no gravity), never inside the gear. Tested on PC: hand pushed 60 cm below the deck top and a 90 cm one-frame swipe both leave the sleeve on the deck; sliding sideways it glides along the deck, onto the mixer, stops at the next deck; it follows the hand back up.
244. (3 Oct) Release: #238 to #243 (LIVE CAM and SOLO on the LED wall, VIDVINYL / AVACAM names, AVACAM tab, sleeves in the open + corner grab + spin tilt + tidy-up on VR start, kinematic sleeves) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (js/main.js, net-link.js, spectator-client.js, spectator-host.js, xr.js); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
245. (3 Oct) Licensed avatar (owner: AvatarRobot.glb from EntroPi Games, author of Vinyl Reality, given with permission; owner embosses the dirrogate Gemini logo on both cups). web/models/avatar/avatar_robot.glb + js/robot2.js (RobotAvatar2, replaces RobotAvatar for the robot style; old robot.js/robot.glb kept). Rig: skinned helmet (Head + Neck bones), two 20-bone hands (left mirrored, scale -1; three.js renames duplicate bones Root_1 etc., matched with the suffix stripped). Head bone driven from the HMD pose (Rf = head.q * rotY(pi), eye offset (0, 1.615, 0.04)); smoothed neck yaw; finger bones from XR joints via basis(dir, N). Headband geometry removed at load (dropHeadband: union-find on welded components, drops the >20 cm wide, <5 cm deep part above the head); the painted black fuzzy stripe in the texture is kept (owner). Helmet shell chrome like the flight case ball corners (metalness 1, roughness 0.14, map kept); neck and collar keep the model's matte material (splitByBone: triangles touching a Neck-weighted vertex = group 1). Visor: black glass (MeshBasicMaterial 0x050507 + onBeforeCompile LED grid, 31 x 11 cells sampled from a nearest-filter DataTexture laid round the visor curve). Quiet = Knight Rider scanner on the middle rows; mic (pose.mic = micLevel(), 0 when MIC off) cross-fades to 5 KITT voice bars (centre tallest, new heights every 70 ms, one dark column between bars). Mouth does not move. Crest: models/avatar/gemini_emboss.png (512 px height map from blender/robot/chest_logo.png, blur 2.2) on a plane over each cup's centre disc (Head space x +-0.1312, y -0.0355, z -0.0564, r 0.0146), chrome, bumpMap + alphaMap. sw.js precaches robot2.js, avatar_robot.glb, gemini_emboss.png. Tested on the PC (AVACAM close-ups both cups, visor scanner and simulated voice). Untested: real hand tracking on the Quest (left-hand mirror). Copied to cly3dj-dev.
246. (3 Oct) Release: #245 (licensed EntroPi AvatarRobot: chrome helmet, matte neck, KITT visor, Gemini cup crests) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (js/main.js, js/robot2.js, sw.js, models/avatar/avatar_robot.glb, models/avatar/gemini_emboss.png); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
247. (3 Oct) Owner tweaks. (1) Robot fingertip on the real one: the robot's palm is ~5 cm longer than a hand, so with its Root on the wrist the index tip poked ~7 cm past the tracked tip (into the platter / record hole). robot2.js measures the index tip in Index_Distal space at load (farthest skinned vertex) and each frame shifts the whole hand so that tip sits on the tracked index tip (controller: the blue tip ball, now sent as hands[i].tip by director.js xrPose); the shift is kept in the hand's frame and eased (dt*12), not updated with a controller while the trigger curls the index. Desktop check: residual 0 mm, shift ~4.7 cm. (2) Tighter sleeve colliders: localBoxes() gives one box per visible part >= 5 cm (knobs, buttons left out); a box wrapping another with 80 %+ of its volume gives way to it (case alu trim -> the panels), contained boxes dropped. Deck: plinth, top plate, platter, tonearm area; case: 129 x 87 x 51 panel box instead of 133 x 90 x 55; 23 boxes in all; a quick sphere test skips far boxes. (3) Sleeve auto-return: a held sleeve whose corners or centre come within 10 cm of the record crate box goes back in (lid open), armed once it has been 25 cm clear of the crate or 5 s after the grab (SLEEVE.HOME_NEAR / ARM_CLEAR / ARM_MS); controller buzz. Tested on the PC with a stand-in anchor (near at once: stays; 55 cm out then 12 cm: goes home; held 20 cm above: home after 5 s). Backups: backups/pre-247.
248. (3 Oct) Owner tweaks. (1) Robot fingers through the platter (owner's AVACAM screenshot, desktop demo): the demo pose had the fingertips 2 to 4 cm under the plinth / mixer tops (director.js demoPose wrists +4 cm), and the robot's fingers are thicker than real ones, so the tip target is now the tracked tip raised 12 mm world-up (robot2.js TIP_LIFT): the robot's finger pad rests where the real one does. Desktop check: lowest hand vertex at or above the gear tops. (2) LED wall tilt: the middle grab (#222 twist) now also tilts: tip the hand toward / away from you and the wall pitches about its own horizontal axis, up to ~45 deg either way (ledwall rotation order YXZ; xr.js wallPitch); the first 10 deg of motion pick twist or tilt and the grab stays on that one; a tilt stays where it is let go (ledTilted toast + save). Layout saves the tilt as items.ledwall[5] (roll in [4]); the phone gets it through the mirrored quaternion. (3) Milk crate switched off now leaves the scene (applyPiece detaches it, re-adds it on ON); milks() lists only crates in the scene; layout ridersOf skips hidden / detached pieces so a moved case can't carry it back in. Backups: backups/pre-248 (pre-247 has robot2.js / director.js before #247).
249. (3 Oct) Owner: records must not come off the platter during a rough trigger scratch; with controllers only the grip takes a record off. xr.js grabStart: the trigger's lift zone (label edge + 5 cm, #136) is now hand-pinch only; with a controller trigger that ring scratches like the grooves. Grip lift (#181) unchanged. LED wall flicker reported (cause not yet found; discussed). Copied to cly3dj-dev.
250. (3 Oct) (1) Sleeve colliders per piece (owner: slide covers / records under the decks; wants cylinders for the feet and tight boxes for the body). partBoxes(geo): connected pieces of each mesh (welded 0.1 mm), cached per geometry; pieces >= 3 cm get their own box; round pieces (disc / cylinder by the 45 deg test) get a box 10 % narrower (about the disc's area, corners a few mm past the rim). Deck: 4 separate feet (6 cm, were one slab across the whole underside), plinth, top plate, platter, tonearm parts; the 3.7 cm gap under the plinth between the feet is free. Feet are 29 cm apart side to side and 19 cm front to back, so a 31.5 cm sleeve (or a 30 cm record) tucks under the edge but can't pass between them (same on a real SL1200). 93 boxes in all; first measure ~19 ms on the PC, done at XR start (tidyRecords) so the first grab doesn't hitch. (2) AVACAM flicker (owner: AVACAM worst, wall above and tilted down): the camera picture now has mipmaps + anisotropic filtering (director.js DjCam rt), so the sharp 1024 px render shown small and at an angle no longer sparkles. Also suggested: test with the Quest's experimental Positional Timewarp off, and read 'cam ms' on the perf panel. Backups: backups/pre-249 (xr.js), pre-250 (main.js, director.js). Copied to cly3dj-dev.
251. (3 Oct) Robot jaw 'glitching' about an inch when the head moves (owner, Quest, AVACAM, timewarp on or off): the helmet's lower edge was skinned partly to Head and partly to Neck, and the bust (Neck) follows the head's yaw slowly, so each head move stretched the chin between the two. robot2.js rigidPieces(): every loose piece of the helmet mesh follows only the bone most of its weight leans on (Head 3960 vertices: shell, inner, cups; Neck 536: bust / collar). Desktop check with the neck forced 0.5 rad off the head: helmet intact, bust turns under it. Also noted: AVACAM draws every 3rd headset frame (30 fps at 90 Hz, 24 at 72), so the robot's motion on the wall steps at that rate. Backup: backups/pre-251. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
252. (4 Oct) (1) Owner: with controllers the deck power dial and START / STOP answer the grip only. xr.js: power dial grab = hand pinch or controller grip (was trigger); START / STOP = controller grip within 3 cm of the button (tip pokes skipped for controllers, like 33 / 45 and the lamp); bare hands unchanged (pinch the dial, poke the button). (2) AVACAM still erratic after #251: the front / wide / low angles aimed at a point set from the DJ's raw head depth, so every lean swung the whole picture; DjCam.aimAt now uses a ~2 s average of it. Foveation (0.5, #114) discussed, not changed. Backup: backups/pre-252. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
253. (4 Oct) Owner batch. (1) Records put down against a turntable no longer lean 45 deg on it (leanIfAgainstSide skips decks: they stand on feet, the record lies flat partly under). (2) BEAT 1 buttons moved in line with the PAN knobs (models.js z 0.030, outer side). (3) Moving / turning / tilting / resizing stage items (decks, mixer, record crate incl. its shut-lid handle, milk crates, LED wall, neon, flight cases) = grip only on controllers (xr.js: hitMovable only when gripOk); bare hands pinch as before. (4) Pitch: #230's centre click removed entirely (its +-0.5 % dead zone made records drift after matching); the green LED shows zero (within 0.05 %). (5) Fine pitch: while one hand holds a pitch fader, the other hand's trigger (or pinch) makes the fader move at quarter speed; switching re-anchors so nothing jumps; a buzz marks it. (6) X2 range button like the SL-1210MK7: per deck, past the far (-) end of the pitch fader, blue LED on at +-16 %; default +-8 % (saved per deck, settings.pitchRange); the fader keeps its place so the pitch scales with the range; controllers press it with the trigger, hands poke it, desktop click. Look (owner: the first rubber button was ugly): on the GLB deck X2 is a copy of the 33 button's brushed cap with an 'X2' legend and a blue LED bar (deck-glb.js); the rubber button stays only on the procedural fallback deck. Printed scale on the GLB deck now 8 6 4 2 0. LOCK checks each deck's own range. Cue glitch (all sound in the left speaker after B CUE) discussed: likely B PAN knocked (knob sits just above CUE) or SPLIT on; not changed yet. Backup: backups/pre-253. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
254. (4 Oct) Owner: (1) SPLIT button flashes (350 ms) while split cue is on. (2) RESET MIX on the tablet's main page, beside TOOLS: two taps within 3 s (like GEAR HERE); PAN, HI / MID / LOW, FILTER and TRIM of both channels back to the middle and split cue off; faders, crossfader, master, playback, records and pitch untouched. Gains ramp via engine _ramp (~50 ms); pan now eases in the worklet (panS, 12 ms) so a jump never clicks. (3) Sticky PAN (xr.js knob turn): centred, it holds until turned 8 % of its travel away; turned back within 3 % of the middle it settles there (small buzz). Tested on the PC: RESET MIX one tap = armed, second tap resets (B.pan 0.1 -> 0.5, A.hi 0.2 -> 0.5). Backup: backups/pre-254. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
255. (4 Oct) 45s project agreed (plan: project doc 45s-plan.md; owner: open 38 mm hole, the deck's 45 adapter needed, wobble + wow without it, imported rips need it too, 7" label = same picture cropped). Spider on the GLB deck = one 3.9 cm piece, 192 triangles, merged in a body mesh (fewer facets not worth it). Built now: crate screen 45 icon (a small 7" record with a '45') before the milk-crate icon, search field one button narrower; tap = jump to the '45s' list (made empty if none), tap again = back to where you were; lit while showing. Backup: backups/pre-255. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
256. (4 Oct) 45s step 1: the 45 adapter ('spider', GLB part pPipe1, a 3.9 cm metal ring in each deck's top-left recess) is its own pivot group (deck-glb.js u.spider, home pose u.spiderHome; a direct child of the deck, so mergeStatic leaves it alone; instancing follows its world matrix). main.js spiders: grab with the grip (controllers) or a pinch (hands) within 3.5 cm (xr.js, checked before the platter / record actions); let go within 3 cm of a free spindle (up to 8 cm above the platter) = it sits on the spindle inside the platter group (turns with it, on top of a 12" if one is there; d.spiderOn); within 4.5 cm of an empty recess (either deck) = back in it (d.recess); anywhere else = stays there, levelled, in rig space. Tidy-up at VR start (tidyRecords) puts both home. Desktop mouse and the phone mirror don't move it yet (phone: step 5). Tested on the PC with a stand-in hand: A's spider onto B's spindle, lying free, home again. Backup: backups/pre-256. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
257. (4 Oct) 45s step 2: per-record size. textures.js REC7 (R 87.3 mm, EDGE 85.8, OUT 84.2, IN 54.5, RUNOUT 48.5, LABEL 44.5, HOLE 19.1 = 38.2 mm open hole, THICK 1.5 mm) and dimsOf(rec) (rec.size === 7); drawRecordSide / timeToRadius / radiusToTime take the dims (12" defaults unchanged); the ring strip still spans 0..R (1024 texels), groove rules unchanged; recordMaterial gets uHoleN (the 45's open centre is discarded in the shader) and uLabelN per side; label canvas radius = LABEL x 1.12 per size (bump worker gets the hole fraction). Record3D builds its disc from the dims (held.dims). main.js: SLEEVE7 (184 mm sleeve, peek 3 cm, out 18 cm) via sleeveDims(r); sleeves, sleeve dig / slide, sleeve colliders, crate jackets (instance scale), covers and the riding disc (crateDisc.userData.size) all per record; held 7" held by its own edge. Test record: EXAMPLES 'single45' ('Test 45 (Sleeve Art Demo)', the two-file demo as a 7") in the 45s list and the Collection. Tested on the PC: 45s list jacket + riding disc, pulled 7" (open hole checked against a bright plane), 7" sleeve dig, 12" crate unchanged. Decks still treat a 45 as a 12" (step 3). Backup: backups/pre-257. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
258. (4 Oct) Flicker hunt (owner: worst on AVACAM but also erratic with AVACAM off). Temporary test switches on TOOLS (home page, bottom row): GLASS TEST (hides the tablet's glass plate; label 'GLASS TEST: GLASS OFF' while on), CAM 2ND FRAME (AVACAM every 2nd headset frame instead of every 3rd), FPS (main page, landscape, beside RESET MIX: frames in the last second, frames over 1.5x the median frame time in the last 10 s, worst frame; green / amber / red; redrawn every second). Glass ruled out on paper: additive, no depth write, 0.3 mm over the screen (depth precision ~0.0003 mm at arm's length). Real glass (MeshPhysical transmission) discussed: renders the opaque scene again per eye into a mipmapped target, too costly on Quest. Backup: backups/pre-258. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
259. (4 Oct) Tablet glass realism (owner chose 1 and 2, not fake refraction: it blurred the text before). models.js: (1) roughness map on the glass (256 px canvas, one tile per 10 cm): clean ~0.04 (sharp mirror) with 7 soft smears and 5 fingerprint whorls up to ~0.3, so a light caught in the glass shows a greasy haze and prints; roughness 1 x map G. (2) Bevel: four 1.2 mm strips turned 45 deg outward round the glass edge (MeshPhysical black, roughness 0.04, specularF90 1, additive), children of the glass so GLASS TEST hides them too. No extra passes, a few triangles. Desktop check: a light's reflection on the glass shows a fingerprint whorl. Backup: backups/pre-259. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
260. (4 Oct) 45s step 3: a 45 on the decks. main.js: recD(d) = the deck record's dims; placeOnDeck (height, slipmat stays visible under a 7"), dropNeedleAt, armGrab / armDrag (lead-in drop zone per record) / armRelease / pendingR, the stylus ride in updateArm and the desktop arm click all use it. Without the 45 adapter on the spindle (d.spiderOn) a 7" sits 10 mm off-centre (WOB_E; the 38 mm hole on the 7 mm pin allows 15): disc offset in the record group so it orbits, tonearm radius + e cos(record angle - stylus angle), and the worklet's play rate x (1 + e/r cos(thr - wobPh)) (keys 'wobble', 'wobPh', sent when the depth changes by 0.1 %): measured on the PC 0.83 to 1.28 x once a turn. A tablet-less toast says the adapter is missing. With the adapter on the spindle the 45 plays centred (spider sits on the mat inside the hole). A 12" put on a deck whose spindle has the adapter sends it back to a free recess (toast). The adapter can't be grabbed from under a record. Lifting a 45 off a deck re-centres its disc. xr.js: RD(d) per-record zones (lift zone = label + 1.2 cm on a 7", scratch on its grooves, the mat outside a 7" = the rim nudge zone), loose-record grab radius per record. Not done: the 45's lighter mass in the platter physics (it uses the record-weight setting). Backup: backups/pre-260. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
261. (4 Oct) 45s step 4 + weight. (1) Platter physics: the 'record' message carries size; a 7" uses 40 g, R 87.3 mm, hole 19.1 mm for its inertia and the slipmat's normal force (deck-worklet setRecord(mass, R, holeR)); 12" records keep the record-weight setting (d.massSetting). (2) RECORD MAKER: SIZE button on the LABEL row (SIZE: 12" / SIZE: 7" 45, red when 7"); the preview disc shrinks to 7" with the bigger label and the open hole; a song longer than 6:30 is refused for a 7" (duration kept from the pick); pressings save size (vire-press index); addPressings puts 7" pressings in the 45s list (sorted) and the Collection; after STAMP the crate jumps to that list ('in the crate, 45s'). Tested on the PC: blank 7" stamped, landed in 45s as size 7, still there after a reload (a 'DUBPLATE 10-04 07:16' test pressing is left in the PC browser's storage; long press + DELETE removes it). Backup: backups/pre-261. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
262. (4 Oct) 45s step 5. Phone mirror: records carry size in the 'rec' message (the phone builds a 7" Record3D) and the disc's off-centre offset as row[11] (the wobble shows on the phone); the crate view items carry size (7" jackets stand smaller on the phone too); the 45 adapters go as 'sp' in the state packet when anything changes (recess i / spindle of deck i / free with rig-space pose; spidersState / spidersApply in main.js, crateRemote.spiders on the phone; resent on connect). Plain 7" sleeves (no front picture): kraft paper with the title and artist, an 89 mm centre hole (drawPlain7); the crate jacket shows the label and the 45's own hole through it; a pulled sleeve has a real hole (alphaMap + alphaTest on front and back) so the record inside shows through. Picture sleeves (cover art or a pressed label picture) stay full pictures. Tested on the PC: spider state round trip (recess -> deck B spindle -> home -> applied back), kraft jacket in the 45s list, pulled plain sleeve with the record showing through its hole. Not mirrored (as before): sleeves lying in the open. Backup: backups/pre-262. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
263. (4 Oct) Owner: lift the 45 adapter out and its recess shows a black disc. Cause: the turntable AO bake (models/turntable_ao.png) was made with the adapter in place, so the plate under it is baked dark (AO 12 to 14 of 255). Fixed in code, no Blender rebake: deck-glb.js clearSpiderAO() rasterises every plate triangle near the adapter into the AO atlas (barycentric -> model x/z), refills texels inside the adapter's footprint with the median AO of the ring just outside it (1.12 to 1.5 R), feathered over the last 12 %; it reads as a faint recess outline. Runs once at load on the R8 AO texture. Backup: backups/pre-263. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
264. (4 Oct) Owner: the strobe dot ring looked smudgy. The GLB rim texture is 128 px with the dots' lighting painted in. deck-glb.js crispDots(): redrawn once at 512 px in the same layout (white chamfer strips top 0-11.5 / bottom 122-128 of 128, 4 dots a tile at x 15 + 32k, rows small / big / medium / medium at y 27.5, 55, 83, 107 with radii 5.8, 13.2, 8.4, 9.6, so the strobe shader's row bands and the motion blur are unchanged): each dot a raised satin stud (contact shadow, dome gradient, upper-left highlight arc, lower-right shadow arc); mipmaps + 8x anisotropy; shared by both decks. Blender AO rebake and baked surface detail (brushed plate, edge bevels) parked until the owner's usage resets. Backup: backups/pre-264. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
265. (4 Oct) Fine pitch with one controller (owner DJs with one): while the trigger holds a pitch fader, squeezing the grip on the same controller = quarter-speed fine mode (re-anchors, buzz); the other hand's trigger / pinch still works (bare hands: only that way). A grab ends only on its own button, so letting go of the grip leaves the fader held. Backup: backups/pre-264/xr.js. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
266. (4 Oct) Platter dots doubled (owner: red dots over the silver, 'overlaid over the original'). Two causes: (1) #264's redrawn dots all sat at x 15 while the model's 128 px normal map has each row at its own phase (19.4 / 15.9 / 17.5 / 12.8 px, y 28.7 / 56.2 / 83.9 / 108.1), so bumps and dots sat side by side; now albedo and a new 512 px normal map (crispDotsNormal, same DOT_ROWS list) are drawn together. (2) The red strobe lamp's frozen sample kept the offset it had when the platter stopped; below half the strobe rate it now eases back onto the real dots (main.js). Also the stud normals fade out as the dots smear at speed (normal map isn't blurred). Owner's attached Technics-style maps not used: different layout (3 staggered rows), no gain over the 512 px redraw. Copied to cly3dj-dev.

268. (4 Oct) Passthrough hand cut-out removed (owner): tracked hands always show the 3D hands, in passthrough too (setHandMode('3d')); 'My real hands (camera)' dropped from the settings list, default hands '3d'. The occluder code in xr.js stays but is never switched on. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
269. (4 Oct) 3D hands' skin 5% darker (owner): 0xc8906f -> 0xbe8969 (xr.js SKIN). Copied to cly3dj-dev.
270. (4 Oct) Finer controls (owner: nudges move too much). Thumb flick 0.01 % of pitch (was 0.05 %, hold repeats every 80 ms). Spindle twist 1/32 of a real spindle (was 1/16): 10 deg of wrist = 1.6 ms, a full wrist turn = 56 ms, haptic tick every 1.25 ms; desktop wheel 0.16 ms a notch (Shift 0.03). Platter rim nudge: friction force halved (controller trigger 0.18 to 1.0, hand 0.18 to 1.0, pinch 0.3). Copied to cly3dj-dev.
271. (4 Oct) Rim nudge: owner wants MORE friction, not less (#270 halved it). Now 1.5x the pre-#270 force: controller trigger / hand depth 0.53 to 3.0, pinch 0.9. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
272. (4 Oct) What the owner meant: a forward / back rim nudge moved the record too far; half as much wanted, same for the spindle. Rim friction back to the pre-#270 values; instead the worklet counts the finger at half its speed difference from the motor (RIM_NUDGE_GAIN 0.5), so a push or drag shifts the beat half as far. Spindle 1/64 of a real spindle: 10 deg of wrist = 0.8 ms, full wrist turn = 28 ms, tick every 1 ms; desktop wheel 0.08 ms (Shift 0.016). Copied to cly3dj-dev.
273. (4 Oct) Holding the record (owner): trigger kept pressed on the record's edge holds the record still (scratch hold, never lifts) while the platter keeps spinning under the slipmat, like an SL-1200. The edge band used to be the rim nudge (from R - 4 mm); now the record counts to R + 3 mm (controllers) / R + 2 mm (hands) and only the platter rim beyond it nudges. Slipmat visual still turns with the platter (hidden under a 12").
274. (4 Oct) Owner: nudge / record-hold numbers from #270-#273 are FROZEN (flick 0.01 %, spindle 1/64 = 0.8 ms per 10 deg, rim nudge gain 0.5 at normal friction, trigger on the record to its edge holds it). Crate: a grab on the selected sleeve anywhere up to 1.5 cm above its top edge takes the sleeve with the record inside (sleeve test now checked before the record test, its cap raised from 4 cm below the top); grabbing the record riding out above that takes the record alone. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
275. (4 Oct) Slipmat travels with the record (owner: holding a 45 should stop the mat too): the slipmat meshes sit in their own pivot inside the platter (u.matPivot), turned by how far the record has slipped against the platter (d.matSlip, accumulated per frame; jumps over 0.3 rad, i.e. cue snaps, are ignored). No record: the mat turns with the platter. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
276. (4 Oct) Controller mode: 3D hands holding the controllers (new js/ctlhands.js). The generic hand GLB (same skin as tracked hands) is placed in grip space (grip -Z runs along the handle: back of the hand out (+X right / -X left), knuckle line along the handle POSE.handle [0,-0.6,1], middle knuckle at POSE.knuckle [0.026,0,0]) and posed by curling each finger joint about its own X axis: trigger value curls the index (open 18/22/12 deg to 40/55/35), grip value the other three (55/75/40 to 75/85/45), a touched thumbstick / A / B lowers the thumb. The hand copies the grip pose each frame (not a child of the grip, measureTip reads its meshes). DJ TOOLS > CONTROLLERS: 3D HANDS / CONTROLLER (saved in vire.ctlLook, default 3D hands); the controller model stays visible inside the hand. Tuned on the PC only. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
277. (4 Oct) Controller hands: the grip also curls index and thumb toward each other (extra POSE.pinch index 20/30/20, thumb 12/30/30 deg at full grip), stopping 2 cm of skin short of touching (tip centres >= 3.6 cm; a short search backs the extra curl off until the gap holds). PC check: open 6.7 cm, full grip 3.6 cm. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
278. (4 Oct) Robot DJ on the spectator phone (avatar-stage-plan.md step 1). Tablet DJ CAM page: ROBOT ON PHONE (djSet.phoneRobot). While on and in VR the Quest adds 'av' to each state tick (phoneRobotState: mic level, handedness per input, controllers' trigger / grip / tip ball, rig-local); head and hands already went every tick. The phone (spectator-client.js) loads its own robot2.js + avatar_robot.glb on the first 'av', poses it each frame from those numbers (same controller pose as director.js xrPose; tracked hands only with all 25 joints), turns on AV_LAYER on its camera, and hides its hand cut-outs while the robot shows (gone 1 s after the last 'av'). PC test: the robot loads and its head lands on the sent head pose; not yet tried on the phone. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
279. (5 Oct) Controller hands, owner's headset test: (1) 3D HANDS mode hides the controller model (grip.children[0]); (2) the blue tip ball (and the touch point) sits 6 mm past the index fingertip of the relaxed hand (ctlhands h.tipLocal, fixed in grip space so a curl never moves the scratch / press point); the ball is now semi-transparent (opacity 0.5) in every mode; (3) the grip pinch hardly showed: the thumb now also swings 20 deg across (about its metacarpal's Z) toward the index and the gap is 3 cm between tip joints (2 cm of skin). PC: 5.2 cm released, a clear C at full grip. The ctl hand is hidden from AVACAM like the controllers. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
280. (5 Oct) Controller hands (owner): the blue ball AND the touch point now follow the live index fingertip (ctlhands h.tipLive, 6 mm past the tip joint; scratching is done with the trigger squeezed, so the curl is steady). The grip now closes thumb and index into an O (POSE.O: index 25/70/50, thumb 5/30/15, thumb swing 45 about Z + roll 20 about Y, mirrored for the left hand; tips meet ~1.6 cm centre to centre, round opening ~4 cm), blended by the grip value over whatever the trigger / thumb were doing, so grip + trigger also shows the O (faders, knobs, power dial, record grabs look real on casts). The #277 2 cm gap search is gone. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
281. (5 Oct) Turntable front feet have no sleeve / record colliders (owner: slide records under the decks; they may cut through those feet). refreshSleeveColliders skips deck pieces with top < 3 cm and min z > 5 cm (deck-local; the four front-foot boxes); the back feet and the body keep theirs. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
282. (5 Oct) Owner tweaks: (1) blue tip ball opacity 0.3 (was 0.5); (2) controllers nudge the platter with no button: the ball on the platter's side (outside the record, same zone as a bare fingertip) starts a rim nudge, moved forward / back = nudge that way, force from how deep the ball is in the rim (0.35 to 2.0), gain 0.5 as frozen; a trigger / grip grab ends it; the record itself still needs the trigger; (3) knobs, faders and pitch faders now take the GRIP (hands: pinch), not the trigger (#195 reversed) so the 3D hand's O closes on them; fine pitch while holding a pitch fader with the grip = the same controller's trigger (or the other hand's trigger / pinch); (4) 3D hand drawn higher by however far the fingertip sank under a button cap (st.pokeLift, eased), so the finger rests on the cap instead of poking through. Not tried in the headset. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
283. (5 Oct) Owner: knobs / faders with 3D hands + grip didn't reach their full range (fine with the bare controller). Cause: the touch point was the live index fingertip, which moves several cm as the grip value changes (the O closes with it), so a held fader slipped. Now while the grip is held the ball and touch point are a fixed point in grip space: the middle of the closed O (ctlhands h.oLocal, from the full-grip pose; ~5 cm from the relaxed fingertip). A grip press re-reads the tip from that point before grabbing. Trigger / no button: the live index fingertip as before. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
284. (5 Oct) Owner: with the grip, knobs / faders needed a whole-arm arc for their range; back to the TRIGGER for knobs, faders and pitch faders (#282's grip reverted; fine pitch = same controller's grip again). The trigger now closes the thumb-and-index O too (o = max(trigger, grip); the grip also curls the other three fingers). Buttons drive the hand as pressed / released, eased over ~80 ms, not the analog value. Ball / touch point = index fingertip: relaxed with no button, the closed O's index fingertip (ctlhands h.tipO, fixed in grip space) while trigger or grip is held, and a press re-reads the tip from there before grabbing. Platter touch-nudge unchanged. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
285. (5 Oct) Controller-mode blue ball moved by the owner's readings off 1 mm crosshair renders of the right Touch Plus (top / side / front; renders made from the GLB in Python): measured nose point + (-3 mm X, -9 mm Y (side said -10, front -8), -5 mm Z = 5 mm forward); X mirrored (+3 mm) on the left controller. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
286. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
287. (5 Oct) Third controller look, GLOVE (owner): the robot DJ's glove from avatar_robot.glb on each controller. A second RobotAvatar2 loads lazily with everything but the two hand nodes hidden (meshes on layer 0, so the headset sees them; hidden from AVACAM). The skin hand is still posed but not drawn; its 25 joints feed robot2's own hand solver, which also puts the glove's index fingertip on the blue ball (12 mm TIP_LIFT as for the avatar; offset frozen while a button is held). Measured: glove knuckle width 6.4 cm, middle finger 9.9 cm, wrist to knuckles 14.1 cm (cuff) vs the generic hand's 6.0 / 8.6 / 9.2 cm, so drawn at 94 % (fingers and width match; the cuff sits back over the wrist). DJ TOOLS > CONTROLLERS cycles 3D HANDS / GLOVE / CONTROLLER; start page list has 'Robot glove'. Interaction exactly as 3D hands. Copied to cly3dj-dev.

New js/phonehands.js draws the DJ's hands as the Quest does, per DJ TOOLS > CONTROLLERS (sent as 'cl' each state tick): 3D skin hands, the robot glove (#287, 94 %) or the Touch Plus models. Controllers: the Quest's hands entries now carry the eased trigger / grip / thumb-touch and handedness ([i,'c',[pose7,t,g,thumb],'l'|'r']), so the phone poses the same ctlhands.js hand at the grip pose. Tracked hands (25 joints, positions only): the generic hand's bones set straight from the joints (-Z along each segment, +Y from the palm plane; PC check: joints exact, twist within ~12 deg); the glove follows through robot2's solver. Hidden 400 ms after the last data, and while ROBOT ON PHONE shows. Later idea (owner): realistic human hand textures. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
289. (5 Oct) Owner: the blue ball / touch point moves from the index tip to the THUMB tip (6 mm past its tip joint; relaxed live, fixed at the closed O's thumb tip while trigger / grip is held), so it lines up with knob and fader caps. The glove still lines its index fingertip up with the skin hand's index tip. Hand-to-controller alignment: renders made (top / side / front, middle knuckle at grip (26, 0, 0) mm) for the owner to give offsets. Copied to cly3dj-dev.

291. (5 Oct) Owner: (1) blue tip ball 150 % (9 mm radius); (2) the touch point switches to the closed-O thumb tip from the select / squeeze EVENTS (st.held), not the gamepad snapshot, which could lag a frame and grab from the relaxed tip then jump (a held fader then fell short of one end); (3) DJ TOOLS > HAND FIT page: move the 3D hands / glove on the controllers (x out / in, y up / down, z back / forward, 5 mm steps) and turn them about the middle knuckle (rx fingers up / down, ry swing, rz roll, 5 deg), both hands (left mirrored), saved in vire.handFit (ctlhands POSE.adj), sent to the phone ('ha') so its hands match. The owner lines them up and reports the numbers. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
292. (5 Oct) Owner: the thumb-tip ball still wandered (knobs / faders erratic); before the O, the trigger worked because the ball didn't move. Now the thumb never moves: it always sits in its closed-O pose (thumb-stick touch no longer lowers it) and only the index curls over to meet it. PC check: ball at the same grip-space point (-4, 0.3, -35.7 mm) relaxed, trigger, grip, half press; gap 53 mm open, 16 mm closed. Hand alignment left for another day (HAND FIT stays). Fallback if this fails: back to #279-style index-tip ball, no O. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
293. (5 Oct) Owner: knobs / faders (3D hands AND bare controller) only worked with the hand or controller an inch into the mixer. Causes found: (a) since #284 a press moved the touch point to the closed O's fingertip, 4.8 cm up and back from the ball the owner aimed (PC: relaxed index tip (3.8, -50.5, -47.3) mm vs O tip (-5.5, -6.4, -25.5)), so the grab looked for a control above the aimed one; (b) a held fader's hand position was clamped to the fader's travel, so a grab a little off the cap could never reach one end. Fixes: one fixed touch point and ball = the relaxed hand's index fingertip (ctlhands h.tipRest, as #279); held faders / crossfader / pitch use the unclamped hand position and re-anchor at an end (no dead travel coming back); knob / fader grab reach 3 -> 4 cm. Thumb moves naturally again (#292 reverted), ball radius 13.5 mm (150 % again). Next fallback if it still fails: no O. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
294. (5 Oct) Owner's grab feel: you aim the ball, which sits on the inner side of the thumb pad; on a press the thumb and index close into an O AROUND the ball. Touch point / ball = fixed grip-space point (ctlhands h.tipRest: 60 % along the relaxed thumb's last segment, 8 mm toward the index tip). The press slides the drawn hand by h.oShift (ball minus O centre, ~2.8 cm) eased with the press, so the O's centre lands exactly on the ball (PC: both at (6.8, 15, -50.1) mm). The grab point never moves; the glove and the phone hands follow the same slide. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
295. (5 Oct) Owner: the index rests curled into the palm (POSE.index.rest 60/80/45) and only comes up to close the O on a press; the ball is still measured with the old straight index, so it stays at (6.8, 15, -50.1) mm. Tip ball light grey (0xd2d6dc), opacity 0.225 (25 % more see-through). Released after #294 (ba6c916, owner pushes). Copied to cly3dj-dev.

Copied to cly3dj-dev.

Plan: one palm + fingers box per hand against the existing sleeve colliders (obbPush, sphere cull, well under 0.1 ms a frame), eased push-out of the drawn hand / glove only (ball, grab point and real-hand tracking unchanged), off while a grab is held so the O still wraps a cap, same on the phone. Per-finger bending on contact later.

New ctlhands.driveGloveHand (Quest and phone): no fingertip shift; each frame the glove's middle knuckle is put on the skin hand's (PC: knuckles within ~8 mm, glove Root ~4 cm behind the wrist = its cuff). In GLOVE mode the hidden skin hand that drives it relaxes to 45 % of its usual curls (POSE.gloveSoft; the grip still closes the fist fully, trigger / grip still make the O): the robot's thick fingers no longer crush into each other in a tight fist. 302. (5 Oct) Glove, owner: (1) on the trigger its thumb slid under the index (the robot's thumb is longer): glove-only O = thumb turn at 20 % (swing / roll), index curl at 80 % (POSE.gloveO; PC: glove tip to tip 33 mm -> 11 mm); (2) cuff capped: a black disc closes its open end (ctlhands.gloveCap, found from the Root-skinned ring, rides on the Root bone, hidden with the glove), and the glove material is double-sided. Quest and phone. Copied to cly3dj-dev.
303. (5 Oct) Glove (owner: cap it properly, smoother without more triangles). Done in the app on the glove's own geometry, the licensed GLB untouched, no Blender: (1) smooth shading: vertices at the same spot average their normals when their faces are within 50 deg (plate edges stay crisp), triangle count unchanged; (2) the cuff closed with a fan from its own rim (9 triangles, rim vertices duplicated with the cap's normal, skinned like the rim, centre on Root) replacing #302's black disc. 1688 -> 1697 triangles. Quest and phone. Copied to cly3dj-dev.

Reflects the scene's environment light like the helmet. Quest and phone. Copied to cly3dj-dev.

(5 Oct) Robot v2, our own look on the licensed AvatarRobot (owner): new web/models/avatar/avatar_robot_v2.glb (source blender/robot_v2/robot_v2.blend, renders renders/robot_v2_*.png); the original avatar_robot.glb is untouched. Headband and old cups removed, and the headband's dark shadow painted out of the helmet's occlusion map. Headphones = the Gemini logo extruded: black cylinder cups (r 4.25 cm, 2.7 cm deep; owner asked for 15 % smaller than the first try) with the logo strokes (outer ring, inner ring, two bowed bars, posts, dots) raised in red (GeminiCup / GeminiLogo materials), skinned to Head. Jaw: a centre ridge pulled 9 mm forward, side corners narrowed. Breast plate decimated to 30 % and flat shaded (faceted). Glove: the three white pads painted out (dark), subsurf level 1 applied, and a chrome dart on the back of each finger from the base knuckle to before the nail (UVs in a white strip so ctlhands.chromePads turns them chrome); 10.8k tris per hand. Code: robot2.js exports ROBOT_GLB (used by main.js, xr.js glove, phonehands.js, spectator-client.js), skips the old crest decals when the model has Gemini materials, and runs chromePads on the hand material. sw.js saves avatar_robot_v2.glb on install.
306. (5 Oct) KITT voice bars on the visor (owner): no gap any more; three touching columns, the middle one full height and the two side ones at 80 %, for a more talking look (robot2.js bars [0.8, 1, 0.8], columns COLS/2-1, COLS/2, COLS/2+1).
307. (5 Oct) Robot v2 pass 2 (owner). Glove: the finger and thumb knuckle pads removed; the back-of-hand pad is chrome like the darts (UVs in the white strip); darts rebuilt centred on each finger (from the finger's own width, not the bone line) from the base knuckle to the base of the nail (DIP + 30 % of the way to the tip), 1.5 mm thick, smooth shaded with crisp rims; the #305 subsurf was decimated back (body 45 %). Breast plate and collar: the frilly 30 % decimation replaced by the original geometry cut and pushed into 4 flat sides (front |angle| < 35 deg, two sides to 110 deg, back), with custom normals per side so each reads as one flat facet (3 seen from the front, matching the jaw). Logo strokes decimated to 40 %. Triangles: head 5092 (was 6088 in the original), cups 1062, glove 3817 per hand (was 1688): robot 13.8k vs 9.5k originally (+4.3k, drawn twice with AVACAM on). The #305/#307 glove mesh had picked up 60 vertex groups (Root.001 / Root.002 duplicates from the dart weight transfers) on both glove objects; not reproduced on the PC (both hands pose the same with demo poses), but that mesh is gone: the glove is back to the licensed model's own 1688-triangle mesh with clean 20 groups, all its pads (finger segments, thumb, back plate) UV'd onto the white strip so chromePads makes them chrome, the body on the dark albedo. 311. (5 Oct) Glove pads as painted chrome squares (owner, starting from the cly3dj-dev #310 robot, nothing else changed): the 15 raised finger and thumb pads (3 layered pieces each) removed from the glove mesh (1688 -> 1118 triangles); in their place, on the glove body, chrome-painted squares (new 1024 GloveAlbedo: body 0.073 as before, white squares + the white strip for the back plate) with a matching tangent-space normal map (GloveNormal) that fakes a raised square with a 1 mm bevel, both rasterised from each removed pad's own position and size (frames in blender/robot_v2/glove_pad_frames.json). The chrome back-of-hand plate stays as geometry. Robot 7.8k triangles. 312. (5 Oct) Fix: right-hand GLOVE look had fingers stretched like spider legs (owner screenshots; left fine). Cause: xr.js / phonehands.js capped the left glove's geometry once (gloveCap, #303) and gave it to both hands. That was fine with the delivered GLB (two identical meshes) but since the Blender export (#305) the two hand meshes sit in different local spaces (the left is mirrored), so the right hand skinned the left's vertices with its own bind matrices: fingers dragged up to 65 cm. Now each glove is capped on its own geometry. Reproduced and checked on the PC with synthetic controller hands: farthest vertex from the wrist 0.65 m before, 0.247 m for both hands after.
313. (5 Oct) Visor LEDs (owner, KITT voice box photo). Fix: the LED grid was laid out with fixed VIS numbers for the delivered GLB, whose visor mesh is head-relative (y ~0); since the Blender export (#305) it sits in model space (y ~1.6), so the visor stayed black (no scanner, no voice bars). robot2.js now measures VIS (angle range, bottom, height) from the visor's own vertices at load. Voice bars: grow up and down from the centre line, the outer bars always at 62 % of the centre bar (no random sides), each bar bright at the centre fading to dim red at its ends (outer bars a step dimmer, fading harder). A medium voice now fills the centre bar (heights x2.0 instead of x1.4). Loud (talk > 0.55): brighter (up to x1.9), pink-white core, less fade, and a short fourth and fifth bar outside (36 % of the centre): five touching bars in all. Checked on the PC (quiet / medium / loud renders).

Checked on the PC (close-up render).
