317. (5 Oct) Owner: spindle twist x4, both directions (unfreezes that one #274 number). xr.js SPINDLE_GEAR 1/64 -> 1/16: 10 deg of wrist = 3.1 ms (was 0.8 ms). Controllers and hands; the desktop mouse wheel is unchanged.
318. (5 Oct) Owner: halve #317's spindle multiplier. SPINDLE_GEAR 1/16 -> 1/32 (10 deg of wrist = 1.6 ms, 2x the frozen #274 value). Owner also noticed the twist shows in neither the BPM readout nor the platter dots: by design since #105 it is a phase shift (worklet 'shift', the record slides over the mat by the twisted amount over ~20 ms) while the motor, so the platter, its strobe dots and the BPM (motor speed) don't change. Asked whether it should act on the platter instead.
319. (5 Oct) Spindle twist through the platter (owner: a real spindle is part of the platter; BPM and dots must show it). deck-worklet.js: 'shift' no longer slides the playhead (legacy model still does); it queues platter angle (spinPend = seconds x W33) that the fingers turn in over ~20 ms as an extra platter speed (spinW, added to wp, and to wr while the record grips the mat; removed again next sample). So the strobe dots, the record, the heard audio, the BPM readout and the servo all respond: on the PC a 20 ms twist gave platter speed +56 % for a moment, the quartz servo pulling it back with a -4 % undershoot, steady again within ~0.25 s (PLL memory off: the shift stays, on: the servo wins it back, as before). On a slick slipmat a hard twist can slip under the record. main.js deck panel: the green / amber +/- readout now shows the platter's live speed (prate / speed - 1, eased ~150 ms, shows the fader value when within 0.015 %, ignores > 25 % jumps such as starts / stops), so twists and rim nudges show. Backups backups/deck-worklet.js.pre319, main.js.pre319.
320. (5 Oct) Owner: back-cueing kicks don't sound like vinyl. Answer: back-cueing already moves only the record (hand spring, 60 Hz), the platter keeps turning under the slipmat, which is right for a 1200 with a slipmat. What was missing is the cartridge and phono stage: the sound was plain resampling (same level and tone at any speed). deck-worklet.js now models a moving-magnet cartridge (output proportional to groove velocity) feeding the RIAA de-emphasis: output = rate x E(f) x D(rate f), as three first-order sections at the output rate whose time constants slide with the record's speed relative to the deck's set speed (33/45 x pitch fader), so normal play is untouched (checked: 0.00 dB at 15 Hz-5 kHz) and only off-speed moves (back-cue, scratch, nudge, start / stop, spindle) change: e.g. at a quarter speed lows -2.9 dB, mids -5.4 dB, highs -0.6 dB (offline check matches the analog formula to 0.05 dB); a held record is silent (no stuck DC sample), backwards inverts polarity. No allocations in the audio loop. Checked on the PC with a synthetic kick: plays, holds, rocks, no NaN. Backup backups/deck-worklet.js.pre320.
321. (5 Oct) Owner: now the spindle turns the platter, drop the artificial gearing. xr.js SPINDLE_GEAR 1 (was 1/32 after #317 / #318, 1/64 frozen in #274): wrist angle = platter angle, 1:1 like a real spindle (10 deg = 50 ms of a 33); the quartz servo pushes back (#319). Haptic tick every 10 ms of audio (2 deg) instead of every 1 ms. Kept: the ~20 ms smoothing in the worklet (it only joins the per-frame hand steps, it doesn't scale anything) and the grab starts from the hand's current angle (no jump). The desktop mouse wheel keeps its own small steps.
322. (5 Oct) Owner (from experience: twisting a 1200's spindle barely changes pitch or speed): re-checked, #319 / #321 were wrong. #319 imposed the twisted angle on the platter whatever torque that took; a real twist is fingers pinching a 7.2 mm polished pin pressed into the platter, so it is a friction torque, small (two fingers ~6 N, mu ~0.5, r 3.6 mm: ~0.022 N m) and far under the quartz servo's 0.09 N m that it holds with no speed change. Now: xr.js sends the fingers' turning speed (wrist yaw rate, smoothed) while the trigger pinches the spindle (ctx.spindleHold / spindleRelease, message 'spin'); the worklet adds SPIN_T 0.022 x tanh((finger speed - platter speed) / 0.3 rad/s) to the platter torque. SPINDLE_GEAR is gone. Measured on the PC (classic MK2, PLL memory off): pinching and holding still for 1 s = speed dip < 1 %, record 1.8 ms later; a 0.2 s forward twist = +1.4 ms whether gentle or hard (beyond the friction the fingers slide on the pin); repeat flicks to move further. The servo's angle term absorbs most of it, as on the real deck. The desktop mouse wheel still uses the old fixed shift. Backups backups/*.pre322.
323. (5 Oct) Owner batch. (1) Reverse spindle twist: works in the #322 model (PC: 0.2 s back-twist -1.35 ms, forward +1.35 ms); physically a back twist and a still pinch are the same (fingers already slower than the platter, the friction on the pin is at its limit). The owner's test was probably the older build (#322 wasn't pushed yet). (2) Hand tracking: the grey ball now sits at the pinch point (between index and thumb tips, where a pinch grabs), not on the index tip; buttons are still poked with the index tip. (3) Hands drawn mangled but working: three's XRHandModelFactory loads the mesh once per slot for the first hand seen there; when the Quest swaps which hand is in a slot, a left mesh was driven by right-hand joints. xr.js now rebuilds the slot's hand model when the handedness changes. (4) Needle lift: was a fixed 7 cm (armDrag: hand raised more than that while holding the arm on a playing record lifts the needle); now settings.needleLift (cm, default 7, 1-10 in 0.5 cm steps) on DJ TOOLS > HAND FIT > NEEDLE LIFT, saved on the headset. (5) Tonearm carried by its finger lift goes anywhere over the record and lands where it is let go (the #104 auto-drop at the lead-in while carrying is off); let go over the lead-in, it lands in the first groove and plays from the start. Backups backups/*.pre323.
324. (5 Oct) Owner, bare-hands (no controllers) mode. (1) The grey ball back on the index fingertip (undoes #323's pinch-point ball), half size (6.75 mm). (2) No pinch on the platter: a pinch at the platter's side now does nothing (consumed); the fingertip on the platter's side nudges either way (pokes / touch, unchanged). Pinching the record (scratch) and the spindle stay. (3) START / STOP and 33 / 45 / X2 take a deliberate pinch with bare hands (grabStart 1b / 0b now also for hands); none of them, nor the target lamp, react to a touch any more (pokes skip them for hands and controllers). (4) Target lamp, bare hands: pinch and hold 1.5 s at the lamp head to raise / sink it (st.direct kind 'lampHold'); controllers keep the trigger toggle. deck-glb.js: the lamp goes dark the moment it is pushed down (on = power && raised && t > 0.6), and its spot light is removed once it is in (#148, unchanged); checked on the PC (sinking: emissive 0, spot 0; in: spot hidden). Also: a sleeve lying about was still picked up by the trigger (#242 path missed in #316): grip only now. Backups backups/xr.js.pre324, deck-glb.js.pre324.
325. (5 Oct) Owner, bare-hands mode: no pinch-scratching; scratching (grooves) and nudging (platter side) are the index fingertip only, by touch. A pinch anywhere on the record (label to rim, not the spindle) now HOLDS the record still while the platter spins under the mat (cueing the first kick): xr.js grabStart kind 'pinchHold' (scratchBegin + one scratchMove at the pinch, then left alone, so scratchIdle keeps it still); pinch and lift 3 cm takes the record off (pickUpFromDeck). A pinch also ends a fingertip scratch in progress. Controllers unchanged. Backup backups/xr.js.pre325.
326. (5 Oct) Owner: the pinched record must also move with the hand (rocking it on the kick for the cue thump, which is a scratch). xr.js pinchHold: scratchMove every frame with the pinch point (still hand = record still, as scratchIdle holds it); lift 3 cm still takes it off. So bare hands: pinch = hold and rock / back-cue, fingertip = scratch and nudge.
327. (5 Oct) Owner, bare hands: half the wrist turn for knobs. xr.js knob gain x2 for hands: full travel (0 to max) = 125 deg of wrist (was 250, controllers unchanged). Sticky PAN (#254) and hard stops unchanged.
328. (5 Oct) Owner: with the 3D hands, turning a knob counter-clockwise turned it clockwise. Cause: knobs read the turn as the yaw (seen from above) of the controller's X axis; that only matches a wrist twist with the nose pointing down. The O grip (#294) holds the controller level or nose-up. Checked numerically, 10 deg clockwise twist: nose 60 deg down +8.7 deg, 20 down +3.5, level 0, 20 up -3.5 (reversed). Fix (owner chose: hand looks only): with 3D HANDS / GLOVE the knob follows the twist about the controller's own long axis (grip -Z, swing-twist of the frame-to-frame rotation), clockwise seen from behind = clockwise: +10 deg at every pitch. The CONTROLLER look and bare hands keep the old reading (no change in feel). Backup backups/xr.js.pre328.
329. (5 Oct) Owner: flight case top up to 5 ft. flightcase.js CASE_LIMITS.H max 0.98 -> 1.524 m (default still 0.88 m). Checked on the PC: resized to the max the case is 1.524 m and the decks ride on it (1.542 m), back down they return to 0.898 m.
330. (5 Oct) Release: #316 to #329 copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (js/deck-glb.js, deck-worklet.js, flightcase.js, main.js, xr.js): grip-only records / sleeves, spindle as a friction torque, platter-speed readout, cartridge + RIAA sound, bare-hands mode, hand mesh handedness, NEEDLE LIFT setting, free tonearm carry, lamp off when sinking, 3D-hand knob twist, 5 ft flight case. PROJECT.md / project doc updated (spindle, cartridge, LOCK, tonearm, bare hands, knobs, case, status, to-do). Committed locally; the owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
331. (13 Oct) Owner: our own live streaming instead of BeamXR (paid, and it streams the headset output, so the CUE channel leaks into the stream). New js/stream.js: the Quest streams to Twitch by itself over WebRTC to Twitch's WHIP ingest (https://g.webrtc.live-video.net:4443/v2/offer, stream key as Bearer; checked from a page on the PC: CORS passes, a fake key gets 403 'Stream key not found'), so no PC or relay at the gig. Sound: audio.js streamTrack() taps the master after the limiter (music + MIC when routed through the mixer) into a MediaStreamDestination, before the split-cue / normal headphone outputs, so CUE never reaches the stream; Opus stereo 160 kbit/s (answer SDP munged stereo=1, maxaveragebitrate, FEC), audio sender priority high. Picture sources (TOOLS > STREAM, saved as vire.streamSrc, switch live with replaceTrack): CARD (Cly3DJ card + now playing artist / title / BPM, 1280x720, redrawn 1 fps, a frame every 100 ms, 600 kbit/s), AVACAM (DJ CAM target tone-mapped ACES + sRGB + flipped in a copy pass to 960x540 8-bit, async read-back, 2.5 Mbit/s; the DJ CAM keeps running while the stream uses it even if the LED wall shows something else; unmirrored), HEADSET CAM (Quest camera via getUserMedia, sent as is, 3 Mbit/s) and PHONE (the spectator phone's LIVE composite passed on, 2 Mbit/s; LED mode changes no longer stop the phone's LIVE while the stream uses it; phone lost = stream falls back to CARD). H.264 preferred (as OBS sends Twitch). Reconnects up to 3 times on a dropped link. Tablet: TOOLS home has a third button STREAM (shows LIVE + time when on air), STREAM page = status (OFF AIR / LIVE hh:mm:ss + Mb/s / errors), 4 source buttons, GO LIVE, END STREAM takes two taps within 2.5 s; TOOLS lit and '● LIVE ON TWITCH' on the main page while on air. Start page: new 'Live stream (Twitch)' section with the stream key (password field, localStorage vire.twitchKey, only ever sent to Twitch). sw.js caches js/stream.js. window.vire gets streamer and setTools (tests). Checked on the PC: fake key -> 'Twitch refused the stream key'; AVACAM read-back gives an upright tone-mapped frame; CARD draws; master track is live stereo; tablet pages drawn. Not yet tested on the Quest (H.264 encode, load while in XR, a real key). Backups backups/pre331/.
332. (13 Oct) Release: #331 (live stream to Twitch from the headset: master bus only, never CUE; CARD / AVACAM / HEADSET CAM / PHONE sources; TOOLS > STREAM; stream key on the start page) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (index.html, sw.js, js/audio.js, main.js, stream.js); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop. Not yet tried on the Quest with a real key.
333. (13 Oct) Owner: Twitch stream works, but HEADSET CAM showed only the camera picture, no decks / crate (it sent the raw camera track). HEADSET CAM is now mixed reality (stream.js): each Quest camera frame -> the scene (no background: passthrough) rendered from the camera's pose into a 960-wide HalfFloat MSAA target on transparent black -> copy pass lays the tone-mapped (ACES, sRGB) gear over the raw camera picture (MR_FS, premultiplied alpha from MSAA) and flips it -> async read-back -> the stream canvas (3 Mbit/s). Camera pose = centre-eye pose from a ~0.5 s history at the frame's capture time (requestVideoFrameCallback captureTime; without callbacks, e.g. in the immersive session, a new picture ~30 /s taken as 45 ms old) minus LAG, times the lens offset (default x -32, y -19, z -75 mm, tilt / turn / roll 0, horizontal FOV 73 deg at the camera's own aspect: starting guesses for the Quest 3 RGB camera, Meta publishes no numbers). The camera video is attached to the page (2 px, invisible) so frame callbacks can run, and the VideoTexture is flagged for upload every MR frame (three's waits for callbacks). New tablet page TOOLS > STREAM > CAM FIT: live preview thumbnail (redrawn 4 /s), steppers LEFT / RIGHT, DOWN / UP, FWD / BACK (5 mm), ZOOM (0.5 deg), TILT / TURN / ROLL (0.5 deg), LAG (5 ms), TEST (runs the MR picture off air, ends when leaving the page), GEAR ON / OFF (off = raw camera, switched live with replaceTrack), RESET; saved as vire.mrcam. Checked on the PC with a test-pattern camera: gear composited over the picture, neon glow adds over it, studio room covers it in desktop mode as it should (hidden in passthrough). Needs the owner's fit on the Quest. Backups backups/pre331/stream.331.js, main.331.js.
334. (13 Oct) Owner, three asks. (1) Cost of the HEADSET CAM second draw, measured on the PC (RTX 4050, GPU timer queries, warm clocks, 960x720): scene into the 4x MSAA HalfFloat target 0.68 ms (0.44 without MSAA), copy pass + camera upload 0.06 ms, async read-back 1.33 ms (largest piece), main desktop view 0.52 ms for comparison; CPU ~2.5 ms per MR frame. Draw calls 84 main / 97 MR (own view, incl. the copy pass); triangles 31.2k, of which the neon sign is 9.2k (hidden: 22.0k). Hiding the neon recompiles the lit shaders once (~0.5 s on the PC: its point light leaves the light count). Frustum culling works per camera (three culls each render), but the neon's PointLight is never culled, and an LED wall on AVACAM renders a third full view regardless. Not changed. (2) 🎧 MIX knob (models.js, id 'hpmix', 0 / 0.123, r 7.2 mm, yellow pointer) above the crossfader, legend '🎧 MIX' to its left (behind the knob it was hidden by the knob, in front by the crossfader cap), CUE / MST at the ends. audio.js: the headphones (normal output, SPLIT off) = master x min(1, 2v) + cue bus x min(1, 2(1-v)) into their own limiter; centre (default) = both full, left = cue only, right = master only; SPLIT unchanged; the stream tap (master limiter) and the master are untouched. Before this the cue bus reached the headphones only with SPLIT on. Hover label 'Headphones ... (stream untouched)'. Not reset by RESET MIX, not saved (centre each load). LEVEL legend of the meters moved between the front pair of LEDs (z 0.113 -> 0.098, 2.4 mm type). (3) Mic LEVEL range: full is now +20 dB (MIC_MAX 10, was +6 dB): left half of the knob = off below 2 %, then -40 dB rising evenly to +20 dB at the centre (0 dB at 2/3 of the half); right half unchanged (mic full, music ducks to -24 dB). engine.micGainOf(v), mixLabel shows the dB. Checked on the PC: knob and legends rendered close up, gain tables. Backups backups/pre334/. (Also: backups/pre331/main.js and audio.js were overwritten by mistake and restored from git a2957ec.)
335. (13 Oct) Release: #333 (HEADSET CAM mixed reality + CAM FIT) and #334 (🎧 MIX knob, mic +20 dB, LEVEL legend) copied from cly3dj-dev to cly3dj (js/stream.js, audio.js, main.js, models.js); CLAUDE.md copied to cly3dj-dev; committed locally, owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.
336. (13 Oct) Owner: visuals on the stream, like his reference shot (camera on the audience side facing the robot DJ, decks in front, visuals as the background). Stream sources now CARD, AVACAM, SHOW, VIDEOWALL, HEADSET CAM, PHONE (stream.js). SHOW (3.5 Mbit/s, 1280x720): layer 1 = what the LED wall shows, drawn full frame ('cover') from its own element (main.js wallSource: CLIPS video or image (ledwall.userData.curImage, flipped), VIDVINYL both deck videos with their mixer gains (B added), LIVE / SOLO the phone video; AVACAM / OFF = dark gradient); layer 2 = robot + gear rendered from the DJ CAM camera into a 960x540 HalfFloat MSAA target on transparent black (showPrep hides the LED wall, room, skybox, wall scroller mesh, XR hands / glove, and the neon for front / low / faders / booth), SHOW_FS tone-maps it and keeps alpha, async read-back, drawn over the visuals (~30 fps); layer 3 = flat overlays: the scroller's own canvas along the bottom (~20 % of the frame), NOW PLAYING pill top-left (artist - title, BPM), Cly3DJ logo top-right. VIDEOWALL = layers 1 + 3 only (no 3D render). Toggles on the STREAM page (vire.showOpts): ROBOT (AV layer), GEAR (layer 0), SCROLLER, NOW PLAYING, LOGO; ROBOT / GEAR dim unless SHOW. The DJ CAM keeps posing the robot and aiming for SHOW, but only renders its own picture when the LED wall or the AVACAM source needs it (needsCamRT). New DJ CAM angle BOOTH (director.js: camera at mixer x, top + 0.30 m, 0.55 m out on the audience side, looking at top + 0.36 m over the DJ, fov 64), also in the AVACAM tab. Known: with the LED wall switched off (silent clock) no video is decoded, so the background is the dark gradient; the movable GOPRO camera, Twitch chat (channel 'dirrogate', anonymous read over wss://irc-ws.chat.twitch.tv, checked working from the PC) come next. Checked on the PC with a test background: SHOW (robot, decks, scroller, logo over the visuals) and VIDEOWALL frames, tablet page. Backups backups/pre336/.
337. (13 Oct) Owner: GOPRO, a movable camera for SHOW. New js/gopro.js: a GoPro-sized prop (62 x 45 x 33 mm body, lens front-left looking along its -Z, red tally lamp lit while SHOW or AVACAM is on air, shutter button, mount foot, viewfinder on the back), a rig child, not mirrored to the phone. Tablet STREAM page: GOPRO (tap = pop in / hide; double-tap within 0.35 s = back to the start spot and on, the first tap undone) and a zoom button WIDE / NORMAL / TIGHT (vertical fov 62 / 46 / 30); start spot = the BOOTH place (mixer x, deck top + 0.30 m, 0.55 m out on the audience side, facing the DJ). xr.js grabStart 0a0: grip (bare hands: pinch) within 3 cm of the body picks it up, keeping its pose relative to the hand (any angle, no gravity, stays where let go); state, pose and zoom saved in 'vire.gopro'. GoPro.tick: the camera follows the lens, eased (1 - e^(-7 dt)) while held, exact at rest. While it is out it IS the DJ CAM camera (goproToDjcam replaces the preset aim), so SHOW, the AVACAM source and the LED wall's AVACAM all film from it; the neon is no longer hidden for the front angles then (the GoPro sees what is in front of it). Viewfinder: the SHOW layer while SHOW is on air, else the DJ CAM picture (tone-mapped in the viewfinder shader) while the wall shows AVACAM or for 3 s after the GoPro is let go (aiming: the DJ CAM renders for it then), dark otherwise. The GoPro is hidden from the DJ CAM and SHOW renders. Also: the stream decodes the LED playlist's current clip itself when the LED wall is switched off (silent clock): streamClip() opens the same file in its own muted video, seeks to the playlist clock (kept within 0.5 s), images via createImageBitmap; closed 3 s after the stream stops asking. sw.js caches js/gopro.js. Checked on the PC: tap / double-tap, hold() pose, zoom cycle, SHOW frame from the GoPro, viewfinder while aiming, a test clip decoded by the stream with the wall off (in step with the clock, its colour in the frame). Grabbing needs the headset. Backups backups/pre337/.
338. (13 Oct) Owner test + reorganising. Bugs: (a) picking BOOTH in the AVACAM tab switched the picture but no GoPro object appeared (the object only came out from the STREAM page's GOPRO button). Now GOPRO is an AVACAM angle (BOOTH renamed): picking it brings the GoPro out, FRONT / FADERS put it away (setDjPreset drives gopro.setOn; saved 'booth' becomes 'gopro', at load the GoPro follows the saved angle); double-tap the GOPRO angle (AVACAM tab) or the STREAM page's GOPRO button = back to the start spot. (b) SHOW / VIDEOWALL showed no visuals or scroller: the scroller only ticked while the LED wall was visible (so with the wall switched off its canvas stayed empty), and the stream only took visuals when the wall itself was on CLIPS. Now the scroller also ticks while SHOWTIME! is on air, and the background is what the VISUALS pages are playing whatever the wall shows: VIDVINYL decks, LIVE / SOLO, the wall's own clip / image when it is decoding, else streamClip() follows the running playlist or, when the playlist is stopped (wall on AVACAM / OFF), runs the LED LIST itself (next clip at the end, images 8 s, a single clip loops). Organising (owner): VIDEOWALL source removed; its slot on the STREAM page is VJ SETUP (opens the VISUALS pages like the main page's VISUALS button); SHOW renamed SHOWTIME! (internal key still 'show'); a VISUALS toggle (background on / off) joins ROBOT, GEAR, SCROLLER, NOW PLAYING, LOGO (ROBOT + GEAR off = the old VIDEOWALL). AVACAM angles reduced to FRONT, FADERS, GOPRO (director.js CAM_PRESETS; old saved angles fall back to FRONT); the neon is hidden only for FRONT / FADERS (the GoPro sees what is in front of it). Checked on the PC: test clip as the background with the wall on AVACAM and with the wall switched off, scroller drawn with the wall off, GOPRO angle on / FRONT off, STREAM page layout. Backups backups/pre338/.
339. (13 Oct) Owner: see what is going out on the tablet. The live Twitch page itself can't be shown (an immersive session can't put a web page or Twitch's player on a 3D surface, and Twitch's video can't be pulled cross-site), so PREVIEW shows the stream's own outgoing frame. GO LIVE is split (owner): its right part is PREVIEW (STREAM page, both orientations). PREVIEW slides out the tablet's preview lid (#196, shared with the phone PREVIEW: switching one on switches the other off) and draws the frame there ~7 fps (384 x 216 canvas texture, letterboxed; the phone source shows the phone video, HEADSET CAM with GEAR off the raw camera) with a badge: '● LIVE hh:mm:ss' (red), 'CONNECTING…' or 'REHEARSAL · OFF AIR', and the source name. Off air it rehearses: stream.js rehearse flag / active getter: the chosen source is drawn (SHOWTIME! layers, AVACAM, CARD, HEADSET CAM via TEST) without connecting; setSource follows while rehearsing; stats only when live. Viewers see it a few seconds later than the preview (Twitch delay). Checked on the PC: lid out with the SHOWTIME! rehearsal frame and badge, split button. Backups backups/pre339/.
340. (13 Oct) Twitch chat (owner, channel 'dirrogate'). New js/chat.js: TwitchChat = read-only anonymous IRC over wss://irc-ws.chat.twitch.tv:443 (CAP tags/commands, PASS SCHMOOPIIE, NICK justinfanNNNNN, JOIN #channel; PING/PONG; reconnects with backoff up to 30 s; CLEARCHAT / CLEARMSG remove messages); keeps the last 60 messages with display name, colour (or a hashed colour), /me, emote ranges; '@<channel>' mentions and '!request' lines highlighted. Emotes drawn from static-cdn.jtvnw.net (CORS open, so canvases stay usable as textures). drawChat(): newest at the bottom, word-wrapped, emotes inline, optional fade (maxAge) and per-message strips. ChatPanel: a 36 x 45 cm screen in the room (512 x 640 canvas texture, purple CHAT header with status), default left of the mixer turned toward the DJ, grip / pinch moves it like the GoPro (xr.js grab block now takes ctx.props = [gopro, chatPanel]), pose + on saved in 'vire.chatPanel', hidden from the DJ CAM / SHOWTIME! renders. CHAT layer), RECONNECT, status, the messages. SHOWTIME! toggles gain CHAT (off by default): the newest messages on the right above the scroller, each fading out 40 s after it arrived. 341. (13 Oct) Owner: CHALKBOARD on the chat panel, and the panel resizable like the tablet (DJ classes). chat.js ChatPanel: tablet CHAT page CHALKBOARD toggle (also brings the panel out) and WIPE (tap twice); state saved with the panel ('vire.chatPanel': on, board, pose, scale). Board = 1024 x 1280 canvas: a greenish-black slate (#1d2a24, faint smudges, old wipe marks, grain; drawn once) + an ink layer, composed and uploaded only while writing (up to ~15 /s). A wooden ledge under it holds three chalks (classic white f2f0e8, yellow f3dc6a, soft pink f2a7c3) and a duster; touching one with a fingertip or the controller ball picks it (it lifts, buzz). Writing = touching the board (main.js each frame feeds xr.tips() to chatPanel.touch while in XR; no button): within 4 mm in front to 3 cm behind the surface (world metres), deeper = thicker (radius 3..8 px), each step scatters 5 grainy dots (alpha 0.25..0.8), fast strokes skip (up to 55 %), jumps over 200 px don't draw; a light buzz every 90 ms while writing. The duster rubs out where it goes (soft circles, a few passes to clean). In board mode the panel is part of the scene (not hidden from the DJ CAM / SHOWTIME! renders), so the GoPro / AVACAM film it; in chat mode it stays the DJ's own. Resize (owner): a silver corner tab top-right (trigger / pinch and drag: size = distance from the centre) or a second grip while the other hand holds it (stretch), 0.5x to 6x (2.2 x 2.7 m); xr.js props with scaleTo / corner get propScale / propStretch; chalk and duster sizes stay real-world at any scale. Checked on the PC through the same touch path: picks, white H, yellow I, pink O, light-pressure line, duster removing ~43 % of the top half's ink in four passes, 3D render of board + ledge, scale clamp. Backups backups/pre341/.
342. (13 Oct) Owner: (1) VJ SETUP (the VISUALS pages) gets a SCROLLER button (landscape tab bar, tabs narrowed; portrait row 3 between AVACAM and DJ MIXER) that opens the scroller editor; its BACK returns to VISUALS (scrollFromVP; head() back 'visuals'). (2) 'No chalkboard button anywhere' (it was only on the CHAT page behind the STREAM page's small CHAT button): TOOLS home now has a fourth big button CHALKBOARD (lit while out): tap = board out (panel on, chalkboard mode) / away; double-tap = back to its start spot and size, on. 345. (13 Oct) Owner: FACETWALL + PORTAL. New js/facetwall.js FacetWall: a faceted backdrop in front of the DJ booth (150 deg arc, 9 flat panels, radius 1.85 m round the DJ side of the mixer, 2.15 m tall, forward-facing only: turning round still shows the real room) hiding the real room from the passthrough camera; one image runs across all the panels (cyclorama), MODE = FRACTAL (Julia shader into a 512 x 256 target, on the GPU) or SCREEN (env.wallSource(), the same layers as the stream's VIDEOWALL, 20 fps into a 1024 x 456 canvas); grip / pinch on the panels moves it; saved as vire.facetwall. New js/portal.js Portal: a rectangular real-world window (0.6 x 1.5 m, 0.4x to 4x by its silver corner tab or a second grip) cut into any mesh by Portal.cutInto(obj) (onBeforeCompile discard inside the rectangle, no depth test; FacetWall today, any glb room later; program cache key folds in earlier hooks), parked over the owner's floor mirror so HEADSET CAM still shows him; thin dark bezel; saved as vire.portal. Tablet STREAM page: WALL / MODE / PORTAL on both layouts (tap = on / off, double-tap = back to start). main.js imports, wall / portal block after the chat panel, STREAM page rows and hint, per-frame ticks, ctx.props, window.vire; sw.js APP list. Review before dev (cloud session): wall and portal had been placed behind the DJ (wall yaw PI -> 0, portal mixer z +1.9 -> -1.3), SCREEN was upside down (UVs) and 35 % stretched (canvas height from the chord, not the 4.83 m panel run), wall grab moved from the floor at the arc centre to the panels. Checked in headless Chromium from the DJ's eye: wall 0.8 to 2.2 m ahead, portal cut through it, SCREEN upright. Positions saved before the fix are kept: double-tap WALL and PORTAL once.
346. (14 Oct) Owner: PORTALS expanded. (a) Tablet STREAM page: PORTALS (tap = every frame on / off, double-tap = back to their start spots and size) + PORTALS SETUP (new tools page 'portals', both layouts): toggles PORTALS, WALL (tap / double-tap as before), FRACTAL / SCREEN, BEAT, DOME, DEFAULTS (two taps: wall shape, fractal speed / colour cycle, dome size); steppers PORTALS count (1-6), WRAP (60-360 deg, 30 deg steps), HEIGHT (0.5-4 m), DISTANCE (1-6 m), DOME SIZE (3-20 m), FRACTAL SPEED and COLOUR CYCLE (0-4x). (b) facetwall.js: slats ~53 cm wide; the count follows wrap x distance (up to 80), wrap / height / distance ease over ~0.3 s so slats fan out / fold in (positions rewritten in place, draw range); 360 = all the way round the DJ; screen canvas and fractal target follow the wall's shape once it settles (2048 / 1024 wide past aspect 3); a wide wall repeats the fractal every ~2.4 widths, mirrored (no flat colour, no seams). Saved in vire.facetwall with the shape. (c) portal.js Portals: up to 6 frames (MAX_PORTALS), each its own prop (grip moves it); size is per frame (half x / y, object scale stays 1): trigger / pinch on the silver side handle = width, top handle = height, corner L = both (xr.js propScale / propStretch unchanged: corner() picks the handle, scaleTo() applies the ratio to that axis), 16 cm to 5 m a side. The cut is now a true window: per eye (cameraPosition) only what lies beyond a frame, seen through it, is discarded (segment camera -> fragment crossing the frame's plane inside its rectangle), so a 360 wall is not cut behind the DJ and the dome is cut too; uniform arrays portalInv[6] / portalHalf[6] / portalN; plain ShaderMaterials (the sky dome) are patched in their own source. Start spots: first in front of the mirror (mixer z -1.3), then 28 deg right, left, right along the wall. Saved in vire.portals; the #345 vire.portal is taken over once. ctx.props is a getter (frames come and go). (d) DOME: the grounded sky (skybox.js, new setRadius 3-20 m) shown with DOME on in any mode, passthrough too, with whatever VISUALS > PANO > SKY picked (still or video pano; loaded on demand via ensureSky(force) whatever the light setting); applySky handles it (room hidden in VR, sky turn kept); the tablet page warns in amber when no sky is picked; vire.dome. (e) New js/beat.js: bass onset (40-160 Hz power from an analyser tapped after the master limiter, beat at 1.5x its 0.6 s running average, at most every 0.28 s; pulse 1 decaying over 0.12 s), read only while the wall is on with BEAT: the wall brightens up to 1.6x on each beat and the fractal kicks in 10 %. sw.js APP += beat. Tested in the cloud container only (headless Chromium, SwiftShader), NOT on the owner's PC GPU: 360 wrap (22 slats), 3 frames spread, side / top handle resizing, front view (three windows through the wall), back view (wall uncut), dome at 6 m with the windows through wall and dome, both tablet layouts, beat detector 12 of 12 synthetic 120 BPM kicks under a noise bed, no page errors. Not tried: grabs in XR, passthrough, a real music mix, frame rate on the Quest.
347. (14 Oct) PHONE CAM (owner): the M33 on a tripod as a locked-off third-person camera; the Quest composites. Phone (fixed camera mode, calibrated #208-#213) sends plain video; new stream source PHONE CAM (stream.js) draws the gear, LED wall, chalkboard and the DJ's drawn hands (whatever DJ TOOLS > CONTROLLERS shows: 3D hands, robot glove, controllers; no cut-outs, no robot) from the phone's pose (rig-space p / q, full-video fov ff, vw / vh, sent in the phone's 'cam' message 5x/s: fixedcam.js camState, spectator-host.js phoneCam) over that video, 1280 wide at the video's shape, 3.5 Mbit/s, SHOWTIME! overlays on top (scroller, now playing, logo, chat; stream.js _overlays, split out of _compose). Black forearm sleeves (new js/sleeves.js, like the Dirro hoodie): a tapered open tube per hand from 2 cm in front of the wrist to an estimated elbow (26 cm, half back along the hand, half toward the shoulder, then down), matte black, fading out over its last 60 %, drawn only in the PHONE CAM render. pcamPrep (main.js) hides the room, sky, GoPro, tip / hit dots, rays, occluders, the spectator frustum / panel. (1) GREEN SCREEN toggle: off = the phone video is the background (gear etc. as augments); on = the phone keys its full-quality camera picture (new js/pcampack.js, own WebGL2 canvas) and sends ONE 1920 x 720 frame: colour 1280 x 720 (spill cleaned) + matte 624 x 720 at x 1296 (16 px black guard), 4 Mbit/s (net-link.js setVideo(track, kbps)); the Quest cuts the person out over the background: BG VISUALS (what the VISUALS pages play, wallSource) or BG DOME (the grounded sky rendered from the phone's view). Layers: background, person, gear + hands, overlays. A packed frame is recognised by its 8:3 shape. AUTO KEY works in fixed mode (samples the plain video). (2) Key colour selectable (owner): new js/keycol.js (keyOf + despill in GLSL and JS: 0 green, 1 blue, 2 magenta = red AND blue above green), used by vset.js (AR virtual set, light wrap, AUTO KEY), pcampack.js; phone key panel 'Key colour', tablet CAMERA tab stepper and PHONE CAM SETUP KEY button; saved in vs.K.col. Blue screen also keys denim / blue clothes. (3) LIGHT MATCH (green screen): every 0.5 s the person's average colour (matte-weighted, from a 96 x 36 copy of the packed frame) -> gain per channel: cast neutralised (0.8-1.25), re-tinted toward the panorama's average colour (skyAvg, new in main.js), exposure levelled sqrt(0.42 / lum) (0.7-1.35), all scaled by LIGHT; EXPOSURE trim in stops; light wrap from 6 matte samples 8 px away x the background's average colour (WRAP); KEY GLOW = the silhouette edge facing the key light (key / pano brightest direction projected into the phone view) lit in its colour. The gear's key light comes from the pano's brightest area during the PHONE CAM render (as Full VR does). Tablet text names where to put a REAL lamp (from the pano light in the phone camera's frame: in front / behind, left / right, height). (4) LATENCY MATCH toggle (off by default): delay measured from the phone video's frame callbacks (captureTime, else receiveTime + 70 ms) or a manual value (DELAY, 150 ms default; DELAY TRIM +-10 ms once measured); hands, controllers, glove, joints and sleeves keep ~1 s of local poses and are drawn as they were that long ago; the stream sound goes through a new DelayNode (audio.js setStreamDelay, glides 0.3 s, ignores changes under 20 ms). Tablet: STREAM page source PHONE CAM (7 sources; portrait 3 columns) + PHONE CAM SETUP (landscape: beside PORTALS SETUP); new tools page 'pcam' (GREEN SCR, BG VISUALS / DOME, LIGHT, LATENCY, KEY colour, AUTO KEY, MATTE (Quest-side matte view), PREVIEW; steppers STRENGTH / SOFTNESS / SPILL (sent to the phone), LIGHT, WRAP, KEY GLOW, EXPOSURE, DELAY; status: phone mode / calibration, video size, delay, lamp hint). Settings 'vire.pcam'. The LED wall's LIVE keeps the PHONE CAM video flags (pcamOpts). sw.js += keycol, pcampack, sleeves. Tested in the cloud only (headless Chromium, SwiftShader; NOT the owner's PC GPU): fake phone video + pose in front of the decks, plain mode (gear over the room video), packed green-screen mode (person cut out over the VISUALS gradient, sleeves, logo), MATTE view, LATENCY MATCH running (movers recorded), packer keys green / blue / magenta test screens correctly, both tablet layouts, phone page loads with the key colour picker, no page errors. Not tried: the real M33 -> Quest link, frame rate on the Quest with PHONE CAM on, the measured delay on real WebRTC, the lamp hint against a real pano. Note: the neon sign stands between an audience-side camera and the DJ (switch it off in VJ TOOLS if it is in the way).

350. (14 Oct) Owner: finger-walking the crate with hand tracking was unreliable; bigger separation round the current sleeve. Cause found: with bare hands a pinch within 3.5 cm of the current sleeve takes the sleeve (#274) and one near the record riding out takes the record (step 4b), so 'dig' (pinch and move along the rack) only started when the pinch landed elsewhere, and then jumped the selection to the slot under the hand (digTo, absolute). Now: (a) TOUCH WALK (bare hands, no pinch): the index fingertip moving over the jacket tops (crate-local y 0.24 .. H + 6 cm, lid open) flips one record per 1.1 cm of travel along the rack, relative to where the finger came in; away from the DJ = next, toward = previous; mostly-vertical motion (reaching down to take the sleeve) doesn't count. The pinch is left free for taking the current sleeve / record. The pinch 'dig' inside the crate is relative the same way (no jump). (b) While walking, the current record stays down in its sleeve (no record riding out to catch the fingers) and pops up 0.45 s after the hand pauses (main.js crateWalkStep, crateState.walking). (c) THUMB SWIPE (owner asked about Meta's microgestures: the Quest browser doesn't pass them to web pages, so it is read from the tracked joints and works on any WebXR browser with hand tracking): hand over or near the crate, thumb tip resting on the side of the index finger (within 2 cm of the proximal..distal segment, not a pinch) for 3 frames, then sliding it toward the fingertip = next record, back toward the knuckle = previous, 1.2 cm a record. Controllers keep the thumbstick. (d) Lid open, the jackets pivot on their bottom edge: the ones flipped past fan forward at 23 deg (0.40 rad, was 0.32 rad about their middle), a sleeve's width apart, the front-most top just inside the front wall; 1.2 cm gap at the floor (about 14 cm at the tops); the current one upright and 1.2 cm higher; 3 cm gap; the next leaning back 6 deg; the rest packed behind (2.6 mm minimum) to the back wall; near the end of a long list the forward fan packs tighter so everything stays inside. Lid shut: as before. Side walking (along the jacket edges) considered and not used: the crate walls stand within 1.5 cm of the jacket tops, so fingers on the sides would be down inside the crate, hidden from the headset cameras by the walls and sleeves; the tops stay in view. Checked in the cloud (headless Chromium, SwiftShader; 60-record list faked): fan, gaps and the hidden record while walking, from the side (walls hidden) and from the DJ's view; no page errors. Untested on the Quest: the touch walk and thumb swipe feel (step sizes WALK_STEP 1.1 cm and SWIPE_STEP 1.2 cm in xr.js).

358. (14 Oct) Owner: (a) SHARE SCREEN from the spectator phone, so a VJ app on the phone (Vythm, MK Games) can play on the Quest's LED wall. Phone start page: 'Share this phone's screen with the LED wall' (enabled once linked): getDisplayMedia (Android asks: a single app or the entire screen; 1280 ideal, 30 fps, picture only) replaces whatever the link's video slot carried (3 Mbit/s); the phone sends ctl {k:'screen', on}; while sharing, LIVE / SOLO / PHONE CAM requests leave the slot alone; stopping (button, Android's Stop, link closed) restores the LIVE picture if one was asked for. Quest: spectator-host onScreen -> main.js phoneScreen; new LED mode PHONE SCREEN ('screen', 'LED PHONE SCREEN', after SOLO, only offered while the phone shares) shows liveVideo; the stream's VISUALS background uses it too; leaves when the phone stops or the link drops. UNTESTED where it matters: whether Chrome on Android lets a web page capture the screen (reports said it used to refuse); if it refuses, the page says so. (b) CURVED LED WALL: ledwall.js bends the panel, its frame and the fitted picture onto a cylinder whose centre is in front (edges come toward the DJ): LED CURVE 0-150 deg of arc (10 deg steps), the picture stays fitted whole on the arc, a portrait wall stays flat; LED SIZE now up to 8x (12.8 m wide, was 4x); both on TOOLS > VJ TOOLS (LED CURVE / LED SIZE steppers), curve saved as 'vire.ledCurve' and sent to the phone with the 'led' state so its wall bends too. Video textures on the wall get 4x anisotropic filtering (sharper on the angled edges). Note on sharpness: a 960 x 540 clip spread over a 5 m curved wall seen from 3 m gets about half the pixels the Quest can show there: fine for visuals, soft for small text; 1920 x 1080 clips look clearly sharper on a big wall. Checked in the cloud (headless Chromium, SwiftShader): a 960 x 540 test grid on the wall flat, 120 deg at 4.8 m and 150 deg at 8 m (upright, not mirrored, fitted), VJ TOOLS drawn both ways, phone page loads with the Share button, no page errors.
359. (14 Oct) Owner: the M33 would not connect and showed 'no status line'. The phone page's status line (#spSt) sat at the very bottom of the start panel, below every button, so it was off-screen on a phone. Moved right under Connect (bold). Code paths checked: no #358 regression (the link callbacks only run after startCamera has set everything up); the phone page loads and Connect runs (headless check in the cloud; the cloud can't reach ntfy.sh, so the real handshake wasn't tested here).
360. (14 Oct) Owner: M33 reached the relay but always got 'Quest not found' (right code, Quest restarted). Likely cause: ntfy.sh turns a message body over 4096 bytes into a file attachment, so its text (our OFFER / ANSWER) never arrives; since #238 the offer carries a video slot, 3.3 KB on desktop Chromium (11 codecs, no candidates) and larger on Android Chrome (more codecs + network candidates). net-link.js Relay: every signalling message is now gzipped (CompressionStream) + base64 and posted in parts of at most 3000 chars ({part, mid, i, n, z, d}), joined on arrival (30 s timeout per message); the offer went 3.3 KB -> 1.6 KB in one part. Both the phone and the Quest need the new code (old and new don't understand each other). Checked in the cloud with a two-page test over a simulated relay that drops bodies over 4096 bytes: connected in 3 s; forced to 400-char parts (4 per message) it also connected. Not verified on the real ntfy.sh / M33 from here (the cloud can't reach ntfy.sh).

Owner declined the PC screen-sender alternative (scrcpy + desktop page) and asked to remove the test from the phone page: the Share button, its note and the phone's share code are gone (spectator-client.js). Kept on the Quest: the LED PHONE SCREEN mode and its 'screen' message (only offered while a sender shares, so it never shows now). Curved LED wall and LED SIZE 8x from #358 unchanged. Checked in the cloud: page loads, link test connects.
