## #406 LASER head prop (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- New js/laser.js (in sw.js APP): one movable laser head, GoPro-style (grip / hands pinch moves it, no gravity, stays where let go; saved in vire.laser with all settings). 14 x 7 x 16 cm black anodised body, fins, front window that glows in the beam colour, key switch, green status LED (blinks on each beat in AUTO). Start spot: behind the DJ, 2.1 m up, firing forward (-z) over his head, tilted down 0.1 rad.
- Beams: one InstancedMesh of camera-facing ribbons (one draw call, up to 24 beams, lightBlend: additive, framebuffer alpha untouched, so they show over passthrough). Core 3 mm widening slowly, never under ~2 headset pixels (dimmed by the same ratio so far beams don't flare); halo grows with HAZE; fall-off along the beam with HAZE; forward-scatter boost when a beam points at the viewer; a glow at the window, a bright point when a beam hits your eye. A beam pointing down stops at the floor (rig y 0) and leaves a dot (instanced, rig space). Gear and walls don't stop beams (no ray casts).
- Patterns: BEAM, FAN, SWEEP, TUNNEL (cone), WAVE, SCAN. Colours: RED, GREEN, BLUE, YELLOW, CYAN, MAGENTA, WHITE, RGB (each beam its own hue, drifting). Steppers: BEAMS 1-24, SPREAD 10-140 deg, SPEED 0.25-3x, HAZE 10-100 %, LENGTH 2-15 m.
- AUTO (default on): uses beat.js (beat.want now also true while the laser is on with AUTO; the Beat is created whenever the facet wall or the laser exists). Every 8 beats a new colour, every 16 a new pattern (not BEAM), a punch on each beat, speed follows the beat interval (120 BPM = SPEED as set), dims to 40 % and slows after 4 s without a beat. Picking a pattern / colour on the tablet still works; AUTO carries on from it. Auto changes are not saved.
- Tablet: VJ TOOLS 14th tile LASER (lit when on) opens page LASER (both layouts): LASER ON/OFF (tap; double-tap = back to start, on), AUTO, six patterns, eight colours with swatches, five steppers, help. Picking a pattern or colour switches the laser on.
- On layer 0: headset, AVACAM, SHOWTIME!, HEADSET CAM, PHONE CAM, CLY3DTV and Twitch all see it. Not on the spectator phone (noMirror). Not in the PHONE CAM behind-the-DJ rule (beams usually run over his head); a beam crossing his cut-out paints over him.
- Checked in the PC pane (localhost, RTX 4050): fan and RGB wave render from the audience side, AUTO with 40 fake beats changed patterns and colours, floor dots (12 with the head tilted down), LASER and VJ TOOLS pages in both orientations (no laser labels cut). Pre-existing, not mine: in portrait every TOOLS page title gets cut ("LA...") since #389 put PREVIEW in the header. Not checked in the headset (grab, look in passthrough, frame time).
- Next (#371 order): SHOUTS can send text / emoji to LASER (scanned text not built yet).
- Backup: backups/pre-406/ (main.js, sw.js). No server restart (static files).

## #408 QUEST DEPTH TEST probe page (owner, 19 Oct; #371 order step 1) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: retro Workbench theme for the whole tablet dropped (only the LASER page keeps it, #407); later maybe an original or a free Amiga-style font with credits instead of drawing text as bitmap rectangles.
- New stand-alone page depthtest.html + js/depthtest.js (not in the app, not in sw.js): dirrogate.github.io/cly3dj-dev/depthtest.html in the Quest Browser.
- Asks immersive-ar with depth-sensing (gpu or cpu, luminance-alpha or float32), plane-detection, mesh-detection, hand-tracking; retries without depth if that request fails; 72 Hz like the app.
- FOUND in our vendored three r169: with depth-sensing granted, three draws the depth map into the depth buffer by itself every frame (WebXRDepthSensing / renderer.xr.getDepthSensingMesh(), group order -Infinity): real things hide virtual ones with no code of ours. Caveat: it binds the first depth texture it gets only; the probe counts whether Quest hands out a new texture object (report line 'depth texture object changed').
- Four modes (trigger / pinch): OCCLUSION (three's mesh on), NO OCCLUSION (mesh hidden, to compare frame rate), DEPTH COLOURS (depth texture linearised with the frame's depthNear / depthFar, colour 0-5 m, white contour every 0.5 m = first look at crowd contours), ROOM PLANES + MESH (plane outlines by semantic label, room mesh wireframe). A = session.initiateRoomCapture() if offered, B = panel in front.
- Test scene: 7 labelled pillars at 0.8 / 1.2 / 1.6 / 2 / 2.5 / 3 / 4 m on an arc ahead, a 7 x 4 cube grid at hand height 0.6 m ahead, the app's laser (fan, green, 12 beams; nothing saved over the app's vire.laser).
- Report on the page after exiting (Copy report), also localStorage vire.depthtest: granted features, depth usage / format, depth size / near / far / own projection / own pose, % valid, texture changes, CPU centre depth if CPU, planes and meshes by label, fps per mode (worst frame, frames over 20 ms), errors.
- Checked on the PC only that it loads without errors (no passthrough there). Not run on the Quest yet.

## #409 Pushing (owner, 19 Oct)

The cloud clone can't reach dirrogate.github.io itself; check the live files from the PC browser pane (fetch with cache no-store).

## #408b Depth test fix: black screen, no input (owner's first Quest run, 19 Oct)
- Owner: only a black screen; A, B and trigger did nothing. Likely cause: start() awaited session.updateTargetFrameRate(72) BEFORE renderer.xr.setSession(); that promise only settles once frames run, so three never started the session (no frames = black, no input handled). Now setSession first, frame rate after (not awaited). Rule: never await updateTargetFrameRate before the render loop runs.
- Also: three r169's depth-occlusion mesh (WebXRDepthSensing) writes no colour of its own; set its material.colorWrite = false (it could paint the passthrough black). The page starts in NO OCCLUSION; trigger steps on. Panel placed from the XR camera's position / quaternion on the 3rd frame (the old 300 ms timer read an identity matrix: panel under the floor). Whole frame wrapped in try / catch, page errors and promise rejections caught, errors shown in red on the panel and saved every 2 s (vire.depthtest) so the report survives a hang. Second page button: Start without depth (room only), to isolate depth trouble.

## #411 Real-world occlusion + lasers stop at the room (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner's #408 check: occlusion cut his hand / the pillars cleanly; DEPTH COLOURS sat well on real things.
- Passthrough sessions now also ask for depth-sensing (gpu-optimized) and plane-detection; if the Quest refuses, plain passthrough as before. Full VR unchanged.
- REAL OCCLUSION (start page checkbox 'Real things hide virtual ones', VJ TOOLS 15th tile; settings.occlusion, default on): three r169's own depth mesh (getDepthSensingMesh), its shader replaced once: colour writes off, real surface pushed back 3 cm + 2 % of its distance (so 3D hands / glove on real hands, gear on a real table, the neon's wall glow still show), window depth = 1 - 0.1 / z (Quest: near 0.1, far infinite, #408c). Off = the mesh hidden. All offscreen renders (DJ CAM, SHOWTIME!, HEADSET CAM, PHONE CAM, neon bake, fractal) already turn xr.enabled off, so the mesh only draws in the headset's own view; the stream pictures are unchanged.
- ROOM HITS (start page checkbox 'Lasers stop at your walls...', LASER page gadget ROOM ON/OFF; settings.roomHits, default on): stepRealWorld() reads frame.detectedPlanes 10 times a second, plane -> rig matrices (the rig can move with its anchor), polygons cached per plane until lastChangedTime changes; laser.setRoom(list). Each beam stops at the nearest of the floor (rig y 0) and any plane it crosses inside its polygon (ray in plane space, even-odd test), the dot turned to lie on that surface, 3 mm in front. 24 beams x ~12 planes a frame, negligible. People don't stop beams, but with REAL OCCLUSION on a beam is hidden behind a person.
- Checked on the PC: a fake wall 3 m ahead stops all 8 fan beams at 2.94-3.38 m with the dots on it; no room = full length; LASER and VJ TOOLS pages fit. Not checked in the headset.
- Backup: backups/pre-411/ (main.js, laser.js, index.html). No server restart.

## #412 Laser beams missing in HEADSET CAM (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Cause: the stream's offscreen renders draw the scene on transparent black and lay it over the camera / backdrop by its alpha (MR_FS: mix(camera, gear, a)). The laser (and the neon's halo / wall glow) uses lightBlend: it adds colour but leaves alpha alone (so it shows over passthrough), so in those renders it had alpha 0 and was dropped. Same in SHOWTIME! (SHOW_FS) and PHONE CAM (PCAM_FS).
- Fix (stream.js, all three): the light in a pixel with no coverage (alpha < 0.05, smoothstep) is tone-mapped and added on top: MR / PCAM = min(mix(c, gc, a) + L, 1); SHOW layer (drawn in 2D over the visuals and packed for the S8) gets alpha from its brightness (A = max(a, max channel of P), colour P / A) so it survives source-over. Neon glow now shows in those pictures too.
- Checked on the PC: SHOWTIME! rehearsal with the laser in the FRONT shot, 30k green beam pixels over the visuals. HEADSET CAM can't run on the PC (needs the Quest camera); same formula. Note: from its start spot (behind you, 2.1 m up) the laser head is usually just above the FRONT camera's frame; beams show where they cross the picture.
- Backup: backups/pre-412/stream.js. No server restart.

## #413 Occlusion lined up close to the headset + beams stop at real hands / people (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: (2) real hands hid the beams but the beams carried on past them; (3) with occlusion on, his real hand / controller showed ~5 cm above the drawn controller.
- Cause of (3): the Quest's depth map comes from its own camera (own projection and pose, #408c); three r169's occluder copies it straight onto each eye's screen as if seen from the eye, fine far away, a few cm off at hand distance.
- New js/realdepth.js (RealDepth, in sw.js APP): the depth map lifted back into the room: a 128 x 128 grid, each vertex = its depth texel through the depth camera's inverse projection and pose (XRWebGLBinding.getDepthInformation per view: projectionMatrix, transform, normDepthBufferFromNormView, imageIndex), pushed back 3 cm + 2 % along the depth ray, drawn from each eye into the depth buffer only (colorWrite off, renderOrder -1000), each eye with its own depth image; triangles touching a depth jump to a neighbour texel (> 8 % + 4 cm) or stretched on screen are discarded, empty texels too. Draws only in the headset's own eyes (other cameras: off-screen degenerate). main.js stepRealWorld: own XRWebGLBinding per session, RealDepth updated every frame; three's occluder (patched, #411) stays as fallback only when the depth info can't be read. REAL OCCLUSION switch drives it.
- (2): laser.js DEPTH_GLSL realGap / depthHit: in the beam vertex shader each beam marches 40 steps along its length through the depth camera's view (left eye depth image, laserData()), a real surface crossed between two samples (gap < step + 25 cm, so a hand merely in front of the beam from the depth camera doesn't count) is refined in 5 halvings and the beam ends there, with a brighter tip. Spots: hidden when their beam is stopped before reaching them (uOrigin = the aperture). Same depth view for every camera, so beams also stop at people in HEADSET CAM / SHOWTIME! / PHONE CAM. Follows the ROOM switch.
- Checked on the PC with a synthetic depth map (depth camera 6 cm above the eye, near 0.1 / far infinite, a 12 x 18 cm 'hand' box 0.6 m out and a wall): the occlusion hole matches the box's true outline from the eye; with the laser 35 cm behind the box, the 5 fan beams that meet it stop on its face and the 2 outer ones carry on. Not checked on the Quest (real depth conventions, frame time: grid 32k triangles depth-only per eye, beams ~10k depth reads a frame).
- Backup: backups/pre-413/ (main.js, laser.js, sw.js). No server restart.

## #414 Swirly laser smoke + light sheets (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Real haze only shows where light passes: laser.js SMOKE_GLSL = 3D value noise, domain-warped (noise displaced by noise: curls), drifting up and across with time, looked up at each point in the room (so swirls stay put while beams sweep through). Beams: brightness x smokeMod (0.12 .. 1.87 of normal) on their centre line. Light sheets: one triangle per neighbouring pair of beams (origin, end a, end b; TUNNEL wraps round into a cone; none for BEAM / SCAN), far ends cut by depthHit like the beams, filled with the smoke, fading along the throw and toward the ends; strength 0.1 + 0.3 x HAZE (tuned on the PC). AUTO: a haze puff on each beat (+50 % smoke, 0.35 s fall-off).
- Settings saved with the laser: smoke 'off' / 'beams' / 'sheets' (default sheets), drift 0.25-3 (default 1). LASER page: SMOKE gadget cycles NO SMOKE / SMOKE / SMOKE+ (beams + sheets); DRIFT stepper (6th); AUTO status shortened (BEAT n / NO BEAT / MANUAL).
- Cost: beams only per beam pixel; sheets add a big see-through surface with 5 noise lookups a pixel (watch the Quest frame rate; NO SMOKE / SMOKE as fallbacks). One more draw call.
- Checked on the PC: green fan with smoky sheet below it, RGB tunnel with a swirling smoke cone; both tablet layouts, no labels cut. Not checked on the Quest.
- Backup: backups/pre-414/ (laser.js, main.js). No server restart.

## #415 HEADSET CAM AUTO FIT + REAL OCCLUSION off by default (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: REAL OCCLUSION off is better (beams show inside DOME scenes; with it on his real hands looked jaggy, the 128 grid / edge discard of #413). Default now off (settings.occlusion false); beams still stop at real hands (the depth view for the laser is read whether occlusion is on or off).
- Owner: in his eyes the laser dot sits on his palm, in HEADSET CAM it falls a couple of inches short or right. Cause: the HEADSET CAM camera's place on the headset and its field of view were guesses (FIT_DEF x -32 y -19 z -75 mm, 73 deg); Quest Browser refuses WebXR 'camera-access' (Meta forum, Horizon OS v85, 2026), getUserMedia gives no lens data or pose.
- New js/camfit.js (in sw.js APP): solveCamFit(samples, W, H, fit) = Levenberg-Marquardt over camera position (head frame), turn / tilt / roll (Euler YXZ as stream.js) and focal length, pull toward the starting position 150 px/m; CAL_TARGETS = 9 crosshair places, centre first, CLOSE / ARM OUT alternating. Synthetic check (truth x -28 y -40 z -60, tilt -8, turn 3, roll 1.5, 82 deg; start = FIT_DEF; 3 px noise): recovered within 3 mm / 0.1 deg / 0.4 deg, rms 0.7 px.
- CAM FIT page: AUTO FIT (headset only): switches the stream source to HEADSET CAM, TEST on, REAL HANDS on during the fit (drawn hands / controllers would cover the real tip), red crosshair on the picture with n/9 CLOSE / ARM OUT; each trigger press (either controller, press edge, read from the gamepad in camCalStep()) stores the controller's front tip (grip space (0, -0.007, -0.05), #140) in the head's frame (inverse of renderer.xr.getCamera().matrixWorld) with the cross's pixel; after 9 the fit is solved and written with setFit (x, y, z, tilt, turn, roll, hfov; LAG untouched); result line with the average pixel error (over 12 px = run it again). CANCEL n/9, UNDO. Both tablet layouts (portrait has the bigger picture). Test hook vire._laser.camCal.
- Not checked on the Quest.
- Backup: backups/pre-415/ (main.js, sw.js). No server restart.

## #416 Ball back on the index fingertip, no O (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: with the robot GLOVE (and 3D HANDS) the O pinch looked way off: the glove seemed to push the fader with its middle finger while the thumb and index made an O behind it. Asked to revert the ball to its original place on the index fingertip.

Ball / touch point = the relaxed index fingertip, 6 mm past its tip joint, fixed in grip space (as #279 / #293): (3.8, -50.5, -47.3) mm, the same point #293 measured. POSE.index.rest back to straight [18, 22, 12] (#295's curl into the palm [60, 80, 45] gone).
- The O is gone too: a press is now a pointing press, index [20, 26, 14] (its tip stays 6 to 8 mm from the ball), thumb [0, 15, 15], swing 60, roll 40 (comes up beside the index, tips 41 mm apart). Node search over the generic hand: the thumb can't get closer than 38 mm to a straight index pad while holding the controller; closing the O needs the index curled until its tip is 47 mm off the ball (the #293 problem). Old O values kept in a comment.
- Same code drives the GLOVE (through the skin hand's joints), the phone's hands and the AVACAM robot, so all follow. The glove's fingers are longer than the skin hand's (#287), so its index tip may land a little past the ball: HAND FIT can pull it back.
- Measured in Node on the PC (three r169 + generic-hand/right.glb). Not tried in the headset.
- Backup: backups/pre-416/ctlhands.js. No server restart.

## #418 Controller hand trigger pose + Full VR exit diagnostics (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev, commit 7f22980)
- Owner: with 3D HANDS / GLOVE, pulling the trigger swung the thumb into the palm beside the index while the index hardly moved. Cause: #416 kept the press pose (POSE.O: thumb swing 60 / roll 40, index rest [18,22,12] -> [20,26,14]) and drove it from max(trigger, grip).
- ctlhands.js pose(): the TRIGGER curls only the index, rest -> shut [45, 60, 35] (a full squeeze round the trigger); the GRIP only middle / ring / pinky; the thumb only lowers (thumb.down) when it touches the stick / A-B / X-Y. No O, no thumb swing or roll. Glove keeps its looser relaxed fingers (gloveSoft). POSE.O / gloveO left in the file, unused.
- xr.js: the hand follows the analog trigger and grip travel (gamepad buttons 0 / 1, eased; held state forces a full press if the value reads 0) and the thumb-touch flag again. Safe now because the touch point / ball is h.tipRest, fixed in grip space (#416), so finger curl never moves it.
- Checked on the PC: rendered the right hand on the Touch Plus model at rest, trigger, trigger + grip + thumb: index wraps the trigger, thumb stays on top. In an emulated Quest 3 session (IWER) the right trigger gave tA 1.0, pose key 1.00|0.00|1.
- Full VR dropping to the 2D page: not reproduced in an emulated Quest 3 immersive-vr session (stayed in for 20+ s with laser on, trigger pulled, no errors). main.js: frame() now wraps the old body (frameBody) in try/catch and always renders, since a throw skipped renderer.render and left the headset without frames. The first frame error, frame count, session time and visibility are kept (xrDiag); when a session ends a 15 s toast says e.g. "Full VR ended after 3.2 s, 210 frames · 1 frame errors, first: ...", also saved in localStorage 'vire.xrLastEnd'. Owner to report that line if it still exits.
- Backup: backups/pre-418/ (ctlhands.js, xr.js, main.js). No server restart.

## #419 Ball on the live index fingertip (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: with 3D HANDS / GLOVE the transparent ball stayed put and lagged behind when the trigger curled the index (#418). Causes: the ball / touch point was h.tipRest (relaxed tip, fixed in grip space, #416), 63-66 mm from the curled fingertip; and st.tip was computed before ctlHandStep posed the hand, so it was one frame old.
- xr.js: ctlTipLocal returns the live fingertip (h.tipLive). After ctlHandStep the tip and ball are recomputed from this frame's pose; the ball uses the hand root's matrix (poke lift included). While the trigger is untouched (tA < 0.08) the fingertip is kept as st.aimTip. On a trigger press the grab is tested first at st.aimTip (where you aimed before the curl), then at the live tip. A grab freezes the touch point in grip space where it latched (st.grabTip, cleared on release), so the curling finger never drags a held fader / knob / record.
- Checked in an emulated Quest 3 session (IWER): ball-to-fingertip 0 mm at rest, full trigger and release (fingertip travels 63-66 mm); aimed at the crossfader cap and pulled the trigger: grabbed the slider, crossfader unchanged during the curl, moved when the controller moved 3 cm.
- Full VR (#418 toast): "ended after 0.0 s, 0 frames, no frame errors" on the owner's Quest: the browser ends the session before the first frame. Parked by the owner (works in Android Chrome VR).
- Backup: backups/pre-419/xr.js. No server restart.

## #420 Ball glued to the drawn fingertip + pinch snap on trigger grabs (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: the ball still floated in the 3D hand, and with the GLOVE it sat in the air between index and thumb. Causes: the ball was the SKIN hand's index tip pushed 6 mm past the tip joint; the glove's fingers are longer and shaped differently (#287), so the skin tip is not the glove's tip.
- Owner's design: ball on the index fingertip no matter what; when the ball is on a trigger control and the trigger is pulled, the hand / glove moves itself to pinch that control, so the hand shows how far to travel a fader or twist a knob.
- ctlhands.js: tipLive = the skin hand's index tip joint itself (no 6 mm), plus thumbLive. POSE.pinch = the #280-#292 O (index [25,70,50], thumb [5,30,15], swing 45, roll 20; glove scaled by gloveO). pose()/update() take pin 0..1 (pose key includes it). New gloveTips(H, idx, thumb): the glove's own drawn index / thumb tips (farthest skinned vertex of Index_Distal / Thumb_Distal).
- xr.js: after ctlHandStep the ball (tipDot) = the drawn index tip (glove tips in GLOVE look, else the 3D hand's), world. st.ballLocal = the same point in root space (no poke lift / slide) = touch point when nothing is held; st.aimTip from it while the trigger is untouched; st.pinchRoot = middle of drawn index + thumb tips. snapTarget(st): trigger grab of kind knob / slider (mixer.userData.controls[id]), pitch (pitchCap), power (powerKnob) -> cap world position + 8 mm (power 10 mm). ctlHandStep: st.pin eases 0 <-> 1 over 0.1 s; st.snapS = grip-space slide putting last frame's pinch centre on the cap, applied to the hand root with smoothstep(pin); release slides back. The hand passes pin to h.update. The touch point stays st.grabTip, so the control follows the real controller.
- Checked in an emulated Quest 3 (IWER), crossfader: 3D hands and glove, ball 0 mm from the drawn tip at rest; pull trigger: grabbed the slider, pinch centre 0 mm from the cap, crossfader unchanged; controller 2 cm sideways: crossfader 0 -> 0.67 and the pinch still 0 mm from the moving cap; release: hand back on the controller in ~0.1 s. Renders: aim / pinch for both looks.
- Not done: phone / AVACAM mirror of the slide (they get the finger pose only). Buttons are pokes, no snap. Not tried in the headset.
- Backup: backups/pre-420/ (ctlhands.js, xr.js). No server restart.

## #421 Ball 1 cm ahead + knob twist gain + hand kept out of the mixer (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: (1) the 3D hand's ball must be ~1 cm ahead of the fingertip; (2) knobs need far too much twist and the hand / glove ends up buried in the mixer.
- ctlhands.js: h.tipBall = index tip joint + 10 mm along the last finger bone. xr.js: in 3D HANDS the ball and touch point use tipBall; the pinch centre still uses the real tip (tipLive) + thumb tip. GLOVE unchanged (its own drawn tip).
- Knob gain: controllers now go 0 -> full in 90 deg of twist (was 250 deg: dy / 300 deg x 1.2); bare tracked hands unchanged (125 deg).
- Knob pinch pose: the drawn hand no longer follows the wrist. On latch it keeps the grip's orientation (st.knobSnap.qGrip) and turns about the cap by 30 % of the knob's own rotation (= the wrist twist that caused it: 90 deg for the knob's 300); pinch centre composed onto the cap (st.knobM), blended in / out with pin.
- Burying: the pinch slide pulled the hand ~5 cm down (pinch centre is below the ball). Each frame the lowest palm joint (wrist, metacarpals, proximal phalanges, middle / ring / pinky intermediate) is measured; the hand is lifted (world y, st.snapLift) so it stays 10 mm (glove 20 mm) above the control's base (snapFloor = knob / fader / cap origin).
- Checked in an emulated Quest 3 (IWER), controller pitched 40 deg down, knob A.mid: twist 45 deg -> 0.50 -> 0.85, 90 deg -> 1.0; ball 10 mm ahead of the tip; lowest palm joint went from 44 mm below the knob base (before) to 10 mm above (3D hand) / 20 mm (glove) and stays there while twisting. Renders: aim, pinch, 45, 90 for both looks.
- Backup: backups/pre-421/ (ctlhands.js, xr.js). No server restart.

## #422 Controller grab restored + ball on the index pad + PINCH FIT sliders (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: #419-#421 broke the controller grab zone. Cause: down() tested the grab at st.aimTip first (a grip-space point left over from the 3D-hand look, never cleared), and with no hand tip (CONTROLLER look) it never tested st.tip at all; in the hand looks the touch point followed the curling finger.
- Restored the #416 grab: down() = one grabStart at st.tip; no aimTip / grabTip. The touch point is ONE point fixed in grip space again (CONTROLLER look: st.tipLocal as always).
- Ball = touch point, on the INSIDE (pad) of the relaxed index finger: ctlhands.js place() h.padRest = middle of the distal bone + 7 mm toward the palm (-Y of the joint), h.padDir. Right hand grip mm: pad (8.0, -38.4, -42.4) vs the old tip (3.8, -50.5, -47.3), 13.7 mm apart. GLOVE: its own relaxed pad (middle of Index_Distal to its drawn tip, +9 mm along padDir), measured while the trigger is untouched and not pinched (st.gloveRest), then fixed. Plus the BALL offsets.
- No automatic lift any more (the #421 palm clearance is gone). Pinched pose (snapM): knob = grab-time angle turned about the cap by TWIST/300 of the knob's rotation; others = the controller's angle; then PINCH TILT (about the hand's level sideways axis) and PINCH TURN (about vertical, mirrored for the left), pinch centre on the cap, raised by HAND LIFT / GLOVE LIFT. Blended in / out with pin (0.1 s).
- DJ TOOLS > HAND FIT > PINCH FIT ▸ (new page, sliders, landscape 2 x 4 / portrait 8 rows + RESET PINCH FIT): BALL IN/OUT -25..25 mm (bx, mirrored left), BALL UP/DN -25..25 (by), BALL BK/FWD -30..30 (bz), KNOB TWIST 30..300 deg (kt, default 90; controllers' wrist turn for 0 -> full), HAND LIFT -20..60 mm (lh, default 10), GLOVE LIFT -20..60 (lg, 15), PINCH TILT -60..60 deg (pa), PINCH TURN -90..90 (pt). Stored in CTL_POSE.adj / 'vire.handFit' with the HAND FIT values. Defaults: main.js PINCH_FIT_DEF and xr.js PF (keep equal).
- Tablet sliders can now be dragged: tSlider hits carry drag: true; mixScreenPress remembers it (screenDrag), mixScreenDrag(uv) moves it while the fingertip stays on the screen (xr.js pokes: not-armed + inside) or the mouse is held (pointerMove 'screen'); release clears. Applies to every tSlider (PANO TURN etc.).
- Checked in an emulated Quest 3: CONTROLLER look trigger on the crossfader = slider grab; 3D HANDS same, pinch onto the cap; pad position rendered on a bare hand; tablet drag on the desktop: KNOB TWIST 88 -> 212 -> 299 following the drag, nothing after release; HAND LIFT dragged to 37, saved.
- Backup: backups/pre-422/ (ctlhands.js, xr.js, main.js). No server restart.

## #423 Hand on the record: no more warble + TURNTABLE FEEL sliders (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: holding the record to back-cue gives extraneous warbling and seems to move it too fast; is the math following the hand?
- Math check: the record turns by the hand's angle about the spindle, 1:1 (a finger stuck to the vinyl): 1 cm at 12 cm radius = 4.8 deg = 24 ms of audio. That part was right. Measured in Node on the real worklet (tools/handtest: harness.mjs runs deck-worklet.js; realside.mjs runs main.js's own scratchBegin/scratchMove; test8.mjs) with 0.3 mm tracking noise and 4 ms frame-timing jitter, 72 Hz: BEFORE a hand held still shook the record by 0.154 x normal speed RMS (the warble), a 2 cm / 1 Hz back-cue 0.181 and overshot 4 % (follow gain 1.044). Causes: the rate sent was a raw frame-to-frame difference over performance.now() at callback time (jittery), the worklet dead-reckoned with it, and each new reading jumped the spring's target.
- Fix: main.js scratchMove (record hand only; rim nudge unchanged): angle x SCRATCH & DRAG into a one-euro filter (HAND SMOOTHING k: min cutoff 0.25 + 1.75 (1-k) Hz, beta 4 + 36 (1-k); default 85 % = 0.51 Hz / 9.4), every reading stamped with the frame's own time (frame(t) -> frameT; XR frame time when presenting, else performance.now). scratchIdle stamps a hold reading. deck-worklet.js: stamped readings are queued and played back on the audio clock (offset = fastest delivery, creeping +0.2 ms per reading; HAND_DELAY 8 ms margin), linearly interpolated, extrapolated at most 30 ms then held; unstamped readings chase each one over a frame interval (HAND_PRED 1). AFTER (real main.js, smoothing 85): hold 0.016, slow 1 cm back-cue 0.047 (was 0.155), 2 cm back-cue 0.097 (was 0.181, floor ~0.056 from 72 Hz sampling itself), gain 0.96; scratch 6 cm 4 Hz: max error 27 ms (was 42). Smoothing 0: gain 1.007, scratch error 8 ms, but the noise warble is back.
- DJ TOOLS > TURNTABLE FEEL (new page, button beside GEAR LOCKDOWN; sliders, 'vire.feel'; RESET TO AS BUILT): PLATTER FRICTION 25-300 % (x slipmat muS / muK, worklet fricK), SCRATCH & DRAG 25-150 % (record turn per hand turn), HAND SMOOTHING 0-100 % (85), SPINDLE TWIST 25-300 % (x SPIN_T pinch torque in the worklet, spinK; x mouse-wheel twist), NUDGE AMOUNT 25-300 % (rim finger: x friction force and x the 0.5 nudge gain, capped at 1; nudgeK). 100 % = as built. Physics values go with the 'physics' message (sendPhysics).
- tSlider takes lwMax (label width) now. Checked on the PC: page drawn, drag on PLATTER FRICTION saved; emulated Quest session ran with the new worklet, no errors. Not tried on the Quest.
- Backup: backups/pre-423/ (deck-worklet.js, main.js). No server restart.

## #424 RADIUS EVEN-OUT (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: back-cueing a kick / snare, its pitch changed with where the record was held: near the label it sounded sped up, near the edge real.
- Cause: true vinyl geometry. The record turns by the finger's travel / its radius, so the same hand travel near the label (7 cm) turns it ~2x as far as at the outer music area (12-14 cm). On a real deck the finger feels the grooves go by and moves less; in VR nothing tells the hand, so it moves the same distance. Measured (tools/handtest/test9.mjs, real main.js): 2 cm of hand travel = 81.9 ms of audio at r 7 cm, 63.7 at 9, 47.7 at 12, 40.9 at 14.
- main.js scratchMove (record hand only): da x r / (r + k (R_ref - r)), R_ref = record EDGE - 2 cm (12": 13 cm, 7": 6.6 cm), k = TURNTABLE FEEL > RADIUS EVEN-OUT (new slider, 0-100 %, default 100 %). At 100 %: 44.1 ms at every radius. 0 % = true 1:1 vinyl.
- TURNTABLE FEEL page now six bars (landscape rows 36 px), note line updated. Checked on the PC: page drawn, values saved.
- Backup: backups/pre-424/main.js. No server restart.

## #425 Dirrogram Phase A: audience page on its own (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Plan: project doc audience-ar-plan.md (Mode 1 "Hologram"). New web/dirrogram.html + js/dirrogram.js. No relay, no Quest link yet (Phase B).
- Owner's layout (his photo of three DJs): the owner on the left in the headset, the robot DJ beside him (default x -2.3 m, outside left; ROBOT LEFT / RIGHT and BACK / FRONT steppers), the hologram scroller in front of the DJs (3.4 x 0.3 m at y 1.15, z +1.3), the LED wall behind and above (4.8 x 2.7 m, centre y 3.25, z -1.6), the laser right under the wall, and the robot behind its own decks.
- Stage frame: origin on the stage floor under the centre DJ, +x audience's right, +z toward the audience. AIM: the ring is pointed at the centre DJ's chest (stage 0, 1.3, 0), placed DISTANCE metres along the phone's view (default 6), stage turned to face the phone. STAGE HEIGHT moves it up / down.
- Looking around: deviceorientation (relative; iPhone asks on START), mouse drag on a PC. SELFIE flips to the front camera (3D camera turned 180, canvas mirrored by CSS). Camera picture = full-screen quad drawn first (raw sRGB, no tone mapping), cover-cropped; the 3D camera's fov is worked out from the crop and CAMERA ANGLE (long side of the camera picture, default 64 deg).
- Booth: flight-case table (0.92 m) with the GLB turntables (makeGlbDeck, platters spin at 33, a black record with a red / white label on each) and makeMixer, turned 180 so they face the robot. Robot = RobotAvatar2 (AV_LAYER enabled on the camera).
- Robot: live poses first (feedPose(msg) takes the #278 'av' state tick; the DJ's standing spot maps onto the robot's, turned to face the audience; controllers and tracked hands as spectator-client.js), with the idle head nod mixed in at 30 %; with nothing for 1 s it eases back to its own dance (nods on the beat, sways, right hand on the mixer, left hand rocking the left record, a hand up every 8 bars). PC check with a fake Quest feed: head turn, raised hand, KITT bars; mirror correct; back to idle after the feed stops.
- LED wall: Julia fractal on a 160 x 90 LED grid, beat kick; a promo card (CLY white / 3DJ red, LIVE ON VINYL, twitch.tv/dirrogate) 6 s in every 24 s; NOW SPINNING band 10 s in every 30 s. Cly3DTV copy and promo JPGs come with Phase B.
- Laser: laser.js Laser in the stage (settings assigned directly, never saved), smoke 'beams' for phones, auto patterns on a local beat clock (BPM stepper, default 124); on AIM the head turns to point at the phone and the beam length reaches it.
- Scroller: canvas text strip, cyan with magenta / blue fringes, scanlines, flicker, edge fade. Fix found on the PC: three's additive blend multiplies by alpha, so the shader writes alpha 1.
- Settings saved per phone ('dirro.set'): distance, stage height, robot x / z, camera angle, BPM, scroller text, NOW SPINNING text (?np= and ?scroll= in the link too).
- PC test: ?demo=1 uses web/_test/stage_demo.png (the owner's photo; _test is not shipped). Checked in the browser pane (landscape and portrait). Not tried on a phone yet: open it over HTTPS (Pinggy to the PC, or cly3dj-dev on GitHub Pages) for camera and gyroscope.
- No server restart.

## #426 The robot DJ is called DIRRODJ (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: the robot is DIRRODJ from now on (not robodj / robotdj). Dirrogram labels renamed (DIRRODJ LEFT / RIGHT, BACK / FRONT, start text). Helmet and hands only is deliberate: people see through him.

## #427 Dirrogram Phase B: live link Quest -> relay -> audience phones (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: straight to Phase B; keep the contemporary cyberpunk palette and the current scroller font (no acid-yellow / rust re-theme).
- Relay: ViRe_Custom/tools/dirro-relay.mjs (also cly3dj-dev/tools/ for a phone to fetch). Plain Node, no packages (minimal RFC 6455 framing), runs on the PC or an Android phone in Termux. ws(s)://host/r/<ROOM>?role=dj|fan. Copies every DJ message to the room's phones, keeps the last 'c' (config) for phones that join late, answers {k:'ping'} with {k:'pong'}, tells the DJ {k:'fans', n} every 2 s, pings sockets every 15 s and drops dead ones, skips sending to a phone with 512 KB queued, 64 KB message limit, 2000 phones a room. Needs wss for the https pages: put Pinggy Pro in front (ssh -p 443 -R0:localhost:8090 <token>@pro.pinggy.io). Tested in Node: fan-out, late config, pong.
- Quest: new js/dirro-host.js (DirroHost). TOOLS home has a fifth button DIRROGRAM (landscape five across; portrait under CLY3DTV) -> page: DIRROGRAM ON / OFF, RELAY… (keyboard), NEW ROOM (two taps; the old QR stops working), the room's QR (new vendor/qrcode/qrcode.mjs = qrcode-generator 1.4.4, MIT, Kazuhiko Arase, made an ES module), status (phones, round trip, KB/s). Relay saved per headset (vire.dirroRelay) or from ?relay=wss://… on the app's address (removed from the address after reading, like ?discogs=). Room key kept (vire.dirroRoom) so printed QR codes keep working. QR = dirrogram.html next to the app with #relay=…&room=….
- Sent: 15 a second {k:'p', t, h (head, rig space), hd (controllers / tracked hands as the spectator link), av (mic, handedness, trigger / grip / tip), b (bass onset count, last onset time, the loudest playing deck's BPM x its speed), l (laser settings + phase), qr (Quest round trip)}; {k:'c', scroll (scroller text when on), np (NOW SPINNING)} once a second when changed, every 5 s anyway; ping every 2 s. beat.js runs while the Dirrogram is on. Node test against the relay: 36 poses in 2.6 s, ~250 bytes each with no hands (~400 with a controller), config arrives, status "Live · 1 phone".
- Phone (dirrogram.html): connects from the QR's #relay / room (retries with back-off), status pill top right (LIVE + round trip / WAITING FOR THE DJ / CONNECTING). Clock: smallest (arrival - Quest time) over 10 s less half of each leg's round trip. DIRRODJ: poses drawn 110 ms back, interpolated between the two messages around that time (positions lerped, quaternions nlerped); own dance mixed in and back on its own 1 s after the link drops (Phase A). Beat: the last onset on the phone's clock plus the track's tempo (folded into 300 to 1000 ms), else the median of the onset gaps; no onset for 4 s = no beats. Laser: the Quest's pattern, colour, beams, spread, speed, haze, drift, auto and phase (light sheets become beams-only smoke on phones; its own random pattern changes suppressed); stays on its own when not live. Scroller text and NOW SPINNING from the Quest win over the phone's own; a new track shows NOW SPINNING for 10 s.
- Fix found on the PC: three allocates a texture's storage once, so the scroller texture is disposed when its canvas width changes (new text showed the old strip).
- PC checks (browser pane): fake Quest feed through the same path: laser switched to the Quest's magenta TUNNEL, DIRRODJ followed the head, beats at the feed's rate, new scroller text and NOW SPINNING caption, back to its own dance when the feed stopped; tablet page drawn in landscape (QR at the right); fan URL parsed. Not tried: Quest + relay + Pinggy + a real phone.
- sw.js APP: dirro-host.js, dirrogram.js, dirrogram.html, vendor/qrcode/qrcode.mjs. Backup backups/pre-427/ (main.js, sw.js). No server restart (the relay is a separate small program: node tools/dirro-relay.mjs).

## #428 Dirrogram PEOPLE: MediaPipe person cutout (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- New js/dirro-mask.js (PersonMask) + vendor/mediapipe/: @mediapipe/tasks-vision 1.0.1 (Apache-2.0; vision_bundle.mjs, simd and nosimd wasm, ~11 MB, loaded only when PEOPLE goes on) and the selfie model from @mediapipe/selfie_segmentation (Apache-2.0) with TFLite metadata added (tflite-support 0.4.4 writer: input mean 0 / std 255, label person; the Tasks API refused the bare model: "requires NormalizationOptions metadata"). Google's model host is blocked from both our shells, hence the packaged copy. iPhone: CPU delegate (mediapipe#6142), Android GPU, falls back to CPU.
- Page: PEOPLE button (SELFIE switches it on). Mask from the camera picture up to 20 a second, eased over frames, uploaded as a red-channel texture. After the show is drawn the frame is copied (FramebufferTexture) and the people are drawn back over it from the camera picture (mask smoothstep 0.4 to 0.8), so DIRRODJ, the wall and the lasers sit behind them; the show's light in a 14 px ring round each point (frame minus camera picture) spills onto them, strongest on the outline: a beam meeting a hand ends in a glow.
- PC check on the owner's photo (a phone shot of a TV, magenta light): the graph runs and the mask comes out (502 x 310), but confidence only reaches 0.57 on one head: not a fair test; needs a real phone camera.

## #433 Selfie: no mirror (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: in selfie mode the LED wall and scroller text read backwards. Cause: SELFIE mirrored the whole canvas with CSS (like a mirror). Removed: the selfie view is now the true view (as a friend standing there would see it), so the text reads the right way and the screen matches what a later RECORD will save (a recording takes the canvas without CSS). The 3D camera still turns 180 for the front camera. No server restart.

## #434 Dirrogram LED wall: your pictures, a CLY3DTV copy, tunable LED grid from the tablet (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: send the LED-wall extras (Cly3DTV copy, promo JPGs = the pictures from VISUALS > IMAGES playing on the LED wall) to the audience phones, small JPEGs over the air, and a pixel-grid effect controlled from the tablet.
- Quest (dirro-host.js): when the LED playlist shows a picture (led.on && led.img), it goes out once per connection as {k:'i', id, d}: cover-fitted 512 x 288 JPEG, quality stepped down until under 60 KB (the relay's 64 KB limit). The config carries img (that picture's id) and wall {src, cols, fill, glow}. WALL = CLY3DTV: {k:'f', d} frames of streamer.canvas (when streamer.active: live, rehearsal or the S8 studio) at 192 x 108 JPEG q0.6, 1-6 a second (default 2, ~3-6 KB each). Settings saved on the headset (vire.dirroWall).
- Relay core: keeps the last 8 pictures per room and hands them, then the config, to phones that join later (tested in Node: a late phone got p2..p9 then c). Server restart needed (the core is loaded at start).
- Tablet DIRROGRAM page: WALL: AUTO / FRACTAL / PICTURES / CLY3DTV, LED GRID (OFF, 48 to 320 across), LED DOT (40 to 100 %), LED GLOW (0.5 to 2x), TV FRAMES / S (1 to 6); landscape fits beside the QR (checked in the browser pane), portrait stacked above the QR.
- Phone: AUTO = the Quest's picture whenever one is up, else fractal with the promo card 6 s in 24; PICTURES = the Quest's picture (fractal when none); CLY3DTV = frames (fractal when none for 3 s); FRACTAL. Grid shader: cells from LED GRID (rows = 9/16), dot size from LED DOT, brightness from LED GLOW, no grid at OFF. Checked on the PC with a test picture (96 across, 60 % dots) and test frames (grid off).
- Bandwidth: CLY3DTV at 2 frames a second is ~10 KB/s per phone through the tunnel (100 phones ~1 MB/s); PICTURES costs one 30-60 KB send per picture.
- Backup backups/pre-434/.

## #435 HoloSMS: audience messages, moderated on the tablet (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: a 140-character message field on the Dirrogram page; a moderation page on the tablet picks which ones go to the hologram scroller and, optionally, the sine scroller, and deletes the rest. Also asked: does the Quest send to each phone? No: the Quest sends each update once to the relay (the PC server); the relay copies it to every phone, so the PC's upload grows with the crowd, the Quest's does not.
- Phone: HOLO SMS button -> box (textarea, 140 max, counter, SEND / CANCEL; text selectable there). Sends {k:'sms', t} when live; toasts "Sent to the DJ. It goes up if the DJ picks it" / "wait N s" / "Not connected".
- Relay core: a phone's sms goes to the DJ only, never to other phones: control characters and runs of spaces cleaned, 140 characters, one per 20 s per phone (else {k:'smsw', s}), numbered per room; the sender gets {k:'smsok'}. Tested in Node: trimmed to 140, the second within 20 s refused, the other phone got nothing. Server restart needed.
- Quest (dirro-host.js): sms list (newest first, 60 kept), a toast in the headset for each; smsHolo(id) toggles a message onto the hologram list (6 at most, oldest drops) which goes out in the config ('holo'); smsSine(id) puts it on the Quest's own scroller (cleaned to the bitmap font's letters, 120 max); smsDelete, holoClear. Nothing reaches a screen without HOLO or SINE.
- Tablet: DIRROGRAM page HOLOSMS (n) button (lit while any are on holo) -> HOLOSMS page: 3 per page in landscape, 9 in portrait, each with its age, HOLO / SINE / ✕; footer ◀ page ▶, "n on holo", CLEAR HOLO. Checked in the browser pane.
- Phone scroller: the DJ's scroller text, then the approved messages, joined by ✦. Checked in the browser pane. window.vire.dirro added for tests.
- Backup backups/pre-435/.

## #436 HoloSMS wording and 3-minute gap (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Phone toast after sending: "Sent! It goes up if the DJ picks it." One message per phone every 3 minutes (relay SMS_GAP 180000 ms, was 20 s; owner: keeps the anticipation); the wait toast shows minutes and seconds. Tablet help text updated. Server restart needed (relay core).

## #437 Dirrogram RECORD (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: a quick record button, no Instagram / share upload yet; no watermark (the scene carries the branding).
- ● REC (first in the bar): canvas.captureStream(30) = exactly the screen picture (camera, the show, the PEOPLE cutout; the buttons are HTML, never in it) + the phone microphone (asked on the first REC, echo cancellation / noise suppression / auto gain off so the club sound stays; no mic = a silent clip with a toast). MediaRecorder: MP4 (avc1 + AAC) where the phone can, else WebM VP9 / VP8 + Opus; 6 Mbit/s; 30 s at most; the button shows ■ and the seconds; tap again to stop; the page hidden (phone locked, app switched) stops and keeps the clip. Saved straight away as cly3dj-dirrogram-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS.mp4 (Android: Downloads; iPhone Safari: the Files app's Downloads).
- PC check (browser pane, desktop Chrome): 3.2 s clip, MP4, 1024 x 768, 2.2 MB, plays. Not tried on a phone.
- Backup backups/pre-437/. No server restart.

## #438 AIM WITH BEACON (DirroBeacons) (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: keep AIM as the top button; it opens two choices, AIM and AIM WITH BEACON, with "DirroBeacon venues only" under the second. Discussion before: a beacon that lives only in the Quest's room map is invisible to phones (no shared map between the Quest and phone browsers), so the beacons are real, visible things (lamps, ends of the neon sign, speaker stacks) that the DJ marks with the Quest.
- Quest: DIRROGRAM page row MARK BEACON L / MARK BEACON R (as the crowd sees them) / CLEAR BEACONS (two taps): tap, then touch the light with the controller tip and pull the trigger (the next trigger press after 0.7 s; buzz + toast). Saved on the headset (vire.dirroBeacons, rig space), sent in the config (bcn) when both are set. Status line: "Beacons x m apart, y m up". Landscape layout checked (fits 304 px).
- Phone: AIM menu at START and on AIM (above the button bar, which wraps to two rows on a phone; the settings and HoloSMS boxes now also sit above its real height). AIM WITH BEACON (greyed / refused without beacons; back camera only): three ring taps, LEFT beacon, the DJ in the headset (the live head pose), RIGHT beacon. Solver (bcnSolve): yaw-free horizontal angles between the taps + each tap's tilt + a 1.45 m phone-height prior; grid search 0.25 m then 0.02 m over the audience side (~5-7 ms); then the yaw turn (circular mean). The stage = the Quest rig turned 180 (rig (x, y, z) -> stage (-x, y, -z)), so DIRRODJ, wall, laser and scroller sit in the real layout; DISTANCE / STAGE HEIGHT not used; a plain AIM ends it.
- Why three taps: tested in Node with 0.7 deg hand-tap noise, beacons 3 m apart at 1.98 m: two beacons alone leave a circle of answers (median miss 1.5 m at 6 m, 1.8 m at 10 m, 10 m at 15 m); with the DJ's head as the third point 0.33 / 0.58 / 1.21 m (beacons 1 m apart: 0.5 / 1.35 / 2.4 m). Wider beacons are better.
- PC check (browser pane): fake config + live head, desktop camera aimed as a phone at (1.2, 1.4, 8) with a 0.6 rad gyro turn would: solved (1.21, 1.40, 7.99), 1.7 cm off; afterwards the DJ and both beacons project to the screen centre when looked at (NDC within 0.001).
- Backup backups/pre-438/. No server restart.

## #439 LED WALL page: RETRO-SCENE, MANDEL, GRAB LOOP, phones on FOLLOW, people FX on the phones (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- One central LED-wall control. WALL button on the mixer's bottom bar (landscape after STUDIO; portrait in the STREAM / CLY3DTV / STUDIO row) opens the LED WALL page: big tiles OFF · CLIPS · PICTURES · MANDEL · RETRO-SCENE (the owner's RETRO SCENE floppy icon, web/retroscene.png) · AVACAM · LIVE, one tap each; then GRAB LOOP, AUTO (OFF / 16 / 32 bars: MANDEL and RETRO-SCENE swap on the bar), PHONES: <wall source>; then LED GRID / DOT / GLOW (the same switches as the DIRROGRAM page's).
- The Quest's own wall is the master: SHOWTIME!, Twitch and CLY3DTV already draw it (wallSource -> fxBig for the new modes), and the Dirrogram phones copy it on FOLLOW.
- js/wallfx.js (+ wallfx-worker.js): one 160 x 90 engine for Quest and phones. MANDEL = 64 s zoom into a boundary spiral (-0.77568377, 0.13646737) down to 2.5e-12, colours cycling on the beat, 3 s melt back to the start (the seahorse point ran into the set and cost 100 ms frames; this one stays ~25 ms in Node). RETRO-SCENE = the op-art dancer (moire ring sets, the figure filled with rings from her chest, 4 moves of 8 beats, cyan / pink swap every bar) and, every 8 bars, a GRAB LOOP when there is one. Sums in a worker. Quest: drawn 4x into fxBig (640 x 360) with the LED grid / dot (multiply mask) and glow (brightness), a CanvasTexture on the wall (ledwall setCanvas).
- GRAB LOOP: 3 s at 12 fps, 128 x 72 cells on a 6 x 6 sheet (JPEG under 60 KB, ~38 KB in test). Source: the SHOWTIME! shot while the stream runs (not CARD), else HEADSET CAM's camera, else the headset camera opened for the 3 s. Goes on the wall at once (RETRO-SCENE, loop), played forward and back with a 64-colour Bayer dither and darker odd lines. Last 4 kept (session only).
- LedPlayer.kind: CLIPS = the LED LIST's videos, PICTURES = its images (toast when there are none). LED NOW / the VISUALS LED button = the whole list as before; the VISUALS cycle also passes MANDEL and RETRO-SCENE.
- Phones: WALL_SRC gains FOLLOW (default; a saved AUTO becomes FOLLOW once). Config wall.q = { m, s, t0, lp, lt0 } (Quest clock). MANDEL / RETRO-SCENE drawn on the phone from the clock offset and the Quest's beat count (bp = onset count + time since / beat); loop sheets go once as an 'i' message (relay caches them for late phones); clips / AVACAM / LIVE / VIDVINYL as the 192 x 108 frames (TV FRAMES / S); PICTURES as before; wall OFF = the phone's own AUTO.
- People FX (phone, FX button after PEOPLE; turns PEOPLE on): OP-ART (rings from the person's middle, 2 a beat, outline), NEON (outline on the kick, person dimmed), ECHO (fading trail of the last second), HOLO (scroller look). In the recording too. dirro-mask.js keeps the trail and centroid.
- Checked in the browser pane on the owner's PC (localhost:8000/vire): tiles, AUTO, page in landscape and portrait, worker frames 2-4 ms, wallState / loopData, GRAB LOOP with a fake camera (the pane blocks cameras): 36 frames, 38 KB, wall switches to the loop; Dirrogram ?demo=1 with a fake Quest: FOLLOW MANDEL, RETRO dancer and the loop on the phone's wall; the four people FX on a test mask (the demo photo's person isn't picked up well).
- ToDo filed: SEND TO WALL (phone sends a 3 s silhouette loop to the tablet for approval, like HoloSMS).
- Backup backups/pre-439/. No server restart.

## #440 No edge culling on records, slipmats and the tablet (owner, 19 Oct) (web/ only)
- Owner (Quest 3): the record and slipmat vanish in the right-eye right-corner periphery, and the tablet flickers low in the view. REAL OCCLUSION was off, so not the depth path. Suspect: three r169 frustum culling against the union of the two canted eye frustums.
- frustumCulled = false on: every Record3D disc mesh (models.js), the tablet's meshes (models.js at build, plus a sweep in main.js frameBody once per 60 frames for the lid, glass and corner L added later), and the slipmat meshes (deck-glb.js u.slipmat, plus matPivot, same sweep). Cost: a handful of draw calls always submitted.
- Syntax checked (module mode). Not tested on the Quest. If it persists: fixed foveation 0.5 (owner's rule: don't change) or laser smoke sheets (#414) are the next suspects.
- Not yet copied to cly3dj-dev (the GitHub clone was not connected to this session). Backup backups/pre-440/. Reload only, no Node restart.

## #441 Culling kept with a margin; floor records return after 3 minutes (owner, 19 Oct) (web/ only)
- Owner asked whether #440 (frustumCulled = false) makes off-screen records and slipmats render for nothing. It did: they were always submitted. Replaced by models.js padCull(): culling stays on, the bounding sphere gets +30 cm once per geometry, so the canted Quest eye frustums don't clip a piece at the periphery while things behind you are still skipped. Used on Record3D discs, the tablet meshes (build + the 1 s sweep in main.js frameBody) and the slipmats (deck-glb.js).
- A loose record resting on the FLOOR (L.onFloor) for 180 s (FLOOR_RETURN_S) is filed back into the record crate with a toast. Records on decks, cases, gear, milk crates or leaning stay. The timer resets if it is picked up (it leaves the loose list) or is not resting.
- Syntax checked. Not tested on the Quest. Reload only, no Node restart. Not yet copied to cly3dj-dev.

## #440 Knob resistance restored (owner, 19 Oct) (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: mixer knobs feel too loose since the hand / glove fit work. Cause: #421 changed the controllers' knob gain from 250 deg of wrist twist for full travel (dy / 300 deg x 1.2) to 90 deg, and #422 made 90 the default of PINCH FIT > KNOB TWIST.
- Default back to 250 deg (xr.js PF.kt, main.js PINCH_FIT_DEF.kt; slider range 30 to 300 unchanged, so it can still be tuned). Exactly the old gain: dy / 250 deg. Bare tracked hands were never touched by #421 (125 deg, #327).
- A saved KNOB TWIST of exactly 90 in 'vire.handFit' on the headset (e.g. from RESET PINCH FIT, which writes every default) is removed once (flag 'vire.kt250'); other saved HAND FIT values and any other KNOB TWIST value stay.
- Checked on the PC: saved {kt: 90, lh: 22} became {lh: 22} after a reload; the PINCH FIT page shows KNOB TWIST 250.
- Backup backups/pre-440/. No server restart.

## #441 CLIPS active after a grab, 5 s GRAB LOOP, "+1" sub-menu marker
- A grabbed loop now counts as a clip: the WALL page CLIPS tile is no longer ghosted when there is a loop (it plays the newest loop if the LED LIST has no clips) and shows red while a loop runs. VISUALS > Video lists the grabbed loops first (LOOP n (5 s), tag LOOP / WALL); select one and LED NOW (or + LED) puts it on the wall (showLoop). Long press deletes it from the session.
- GRAB LOOP is 5.3 s: LOOP in wallfx.js is 8 x 8 cells, 64 frames at 12 fps, 112 x 63 per cell (sheet 896 x 504); the quality ladder gained 0.22 and 0.15. Test on noisy video: 57.8 KB (limit 60 KB). Phones use the same constants.
- Convention: a button label ending in the superscript +1 draws a tiny "+1" in its upper right (btnLabel), for buttons that open a page of sub menus that is not obvious. Used on the main WALL button (landscape + portrait) and a new WALL +1 button on VISUALS (landscape tab row: SCROLLER and the MIXER / PREVIEW / BACK buttons narrowed; portrait: last row of four).
- Backup backups/pre-441/. No server restart.

## #442 RETRO-SCENE +1 page, VECTOR-DANCER (matte clips + moire rings), METABALLS
- The WALL page's RETRO-SCENE tile has a "+1" and opens a RETRO-SCENE page (setTools 'retro'): DANCER (the old vector dancer + grab loops), MANDEL, VECTOR-DANCER +1, METABALLS (BALLS stepper 2 to 9) and GRAB LOOP.
- VECTOR-DANCER page (ledMode 'vdancer', tools page 'vdancer'): 6 matte slots; slot 1 is the bundled web/mattes/matte1.mp4 ("DANCER 1", the owner's white-on-black matte, 640 x 360, 464 KB, in the sw.js precache). An empty slot opens VISUALS > Video in pick mode (vd.pick, red frame): the next clip tapped is loaded into the slot (vdAssign). REPLACE / CLEAR (RESET 1 for slot 1) / RINGS ONLY (no matte) / SWAP MATTE; CIRCLES 1-3 sets; BEAT OFF / PULSE (rings run out on the beat) / BOUNCE (centres bounce); colours ACID / NEON / MONO; RING GAP slider (1.5 to 8 LED px). Saved in localStorage 'vire.vd'.
- Matte: a hidden video drawn to 160 x 90, light pixels (red > 110) = the figure (vdMask). The figure shows the same moire in other colours (yellow / red), like the op-art film.
- METABALLS (ledMode 'meta'): 2 to 9 balls bouncing, touching the floor on the beat, palette-coloured with a bright rim.
- wallfx.js: renderRings / renderMeta (run on the main thread inside WallFx.frame, no worker; modes 'rings', 'meta'), RP_DEF, MP_DEF. LED_LABEL / nextLedMode / isFxMode know 'vdancer' and 'meta'.
- Phones (FOLLOW): rings alone and metaballs are drawn on the phone from wallState rp / mp; VECTOR-DANCER with a matte goes as frames (wallState m 'vmat', FRAME_M / FOLLOW_FRAMES), since the matte clip is not on the phones.
- Backup backups/pre-442/. No server restart. Tested in the browser pane only (not on the headset or phones).

## #443 VECTOR-DANCER ZOOM
- A ZOOM slider (0.6x to 3x, rp.z, saved with the other VECTOR-DANCER settings) next to RING GAP: the matte scales about the middle of the frame (a little low, where the feet are); the rings do not scale. wallfx.js renderRings. No server restart.

## #444 PIXELS 0 to 10, hi-res Quest grab, 8-level dither, TAP BPM
- PIXELS slider (WALL page and RETRO-SCENE page, setWallPx): render size + LED grid together (wallfx.js PX): 0 = 480x270 smooth (no grid), 1 = 384x216 smooth, 2 = 320x180 / 320 across (the new default), 3 = 240, 4 = 192, 5 = 160x90 / 160 (the old look), 6 = 128, 7 = 96, 8 = 64, 9 = 48, 10 = 32 across. MANDEL stays 160x90 on its own 160 grid at every level (Quest fxPaint, host cfg and phones override cols). The output canvas (WallFx.canvas, fxBig) is a fixed 640x360; levels 2 and up are drawn blocky into it. The slider sets dirro wall cols (WALL_COLS gained 32); LED GRID can still be nudged after. First run applies the default grid once (vire.wallPxA).
- Rings / METABALLS now run in wallfx-worker.js too (renderMode; the matte goes along as a size-matched byte copy, vdMask(W, H)). Level 0 costs about 10 ms in the worker.
- GRAB LOOP: the Quest records its own 224x126 cell sheet (1792x1008) as well as the phones' 112x63 one (JPEG 52 KB, unchanged); the wall plays the big one.
- Dither: 8 levels a channel (was 4), ordered, from PIXELS 2 up; the darker odd lines from 5 up; none at 0 and 1.
- TAP BPM (WALL page, RETRO-SCENE page): tap the beat; the tempo is the mean of the last 8 taps (60 to 200 BPM), each tap snaps the beat; the effects, the grab/dancer timing and the phones (getBeat / tapBpm) follow until a long press on the button hands the beat back to the music (also times out after 10 minutes). The button shows TAP <bpm>.
- Backup backups/pre-444/. No server restart. Tested in the browser pane only.

## #445 CARD page: pictures and animated GIFs on the stream card
- STREAM > CARD now has a "+1": it puts the CARD source on air and opens the STREAM CARD page (tools page 'card'). Slot 1 is always MAIN CARD (the Cly3DJ card with the Twitch QR); the rest are everything in IMAGES (GIFs tagged GIF; thumbnails as on the other pickers). Tap one: it becomes the card (ON AIR tag when the card is the live source). The choice is remembered (localStorage 'vire.cardPic'; restored at start).

## #446 CARD pictures and GIFs on the LED wall (and the phones)
- Picking a picture / GIF on the CARD page now also puts it on the LED wall (ledMode 'card', label LED CARD) while the page's LED WALL: ON button is on (saved, 'vire.cardWall'); OFF returns the wall to what it showed before; MAIN CARD does too. The restored card at startup does not take the wall.
- Still: drawn whole on black at the PIXELS render size (WallFx mode 'pic', then the LED grid); to the phones it goes as the normal LED-wall picture (wallState m 'pics', getLed returns the card file, 512x288 JPEG under 60 KB).
- GIF: decoded with ImageDecoder (gifLoop in main.js), at most 64 frames sampled evenly across it, with its own speed (n / duration, 2 to 24 fps), played forwards only. Same sprite-sheet rule as GRAB LOOP: 112x63 a cell as a JPEG under 60 KB for the phones (sent once, wallState m 'card', lp / ln / lf), 224x126 a cell for the Quest. WallFx loop mode takes ln, lfps and fwd. Without ImageDecoder a GIF shows as a still. A GIF longer than 64 frames is thinned, not cut.
- Tested in the browser pane (4-frame GIF: card mode, sheet 14.8 KB, frames cycle in order at 4 fps; a PNG goes the pics route). Not on the headset or phones. Backup backups/pre-446/. No server restart.

## #449 (20 Oct 2026) Dirrogram EXPOSURE (cly3dj-dev)
- SETTINGS gains EXPOSURE, -2.00 to +1.00 EV in 0.25 steps (S.exp, saved per phone, default 0). applyExposure(): where the camera track reports exposureCompensation (Android Chrome on most phones) it sets the camera's own exposure (the sensor stops clipping on beams, the cutout sees the adjusted picture); otherwise a software gain 2^EV on the camera picture (bgMat uGain), the same gain in the people shader's cam() so cutout effects match. The recording gets either. The person mask itself is still run on the camera element, so software gain does not change the cutout.
- Tested in the browser pane (demo picture): four taps on - gives -1.00 EV, picture darker, setting saved. Not on the M33 yet (hardware path unverified: depends on the phone's camera caps). Backup backups/pre-449/. No server restart.

## #450 (20 Oct 2026) Dirrogram: SCROLLER and NOW SPINNING fields removed from SETTINGS (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: remove them, keep the defaults in code. They were Phase A leftovers (no relay); live, the DJ's text wins (`liveCfg.scroll || S.scroll`). buildPanel no longer draws the two text rows; S.scroll / S.np are reset to DEF after the saved settings load (so old saved text can't linger); the ?scroll= link override still works for tests.
- Syntax checked only. Backup backups/pre-450/. No server restart.

451. (21 Oct) Owner: records. (3) Realistic grooves, in recordMaterial's shader only (textures.js; no new textures, no extra load time): hairline concentric rings (340 per unit of normalised radius, each with its own random brightness plus a faint ridge profile, faded by fwidth so they never moire at a distance), two opposed soft sheen wedges that stay fixed in the world while the record spins under them (angle from the world position relative to the disc centre, added as emissive), faint dust specks and a few micro-scratch arcs. All gated to the groove band (uGrv = IN / R .. OUT / R from drawRecordSide). The loudness-envelope bands, roughness values and run-out rules are unchanged. This supersedes the 'no synthetic rings' line in DO NOT BREAK on the owner's request. (2) xr.js: the controller GRIP lifts a record from anywhere on it, label to rim (R + 12 mm, height -4 to +7 cm; was label + 5 cm); trigger / touch still scratch, hold and nudge as before; bare-hand pinch rules unchanged. (1) A record can't be lifted while the needle is down (ctx.needleDown(d): needle playing, arm lowered or being dragged): the grip gives two short buzzes and does nothing, and the pinch-hold / lift paths don't pick it up. Lift the needle first. Backup backups/pre-grooves/. Reload, no Node restart. Tested: shader compiles on the PC GPU, test disc rendered; XR grabs not tested on the Quest.

451. (21 Oct) Owner: records. (3) Realistic grooves, in recordMaterial's shader only (textures.js; no new textures, no extra load time): hairline concentric rings (340 per unit of normalised radius, each with its own random brightness plus a faint ridge profile, faded by fwidth so they never moire at a distance), two opposed soft sheen wedges that stay fixed in the world while the record spins under them (angle from the world position relative to the disc centre, added as emissive), faint dust specks and a few micro-scratch arcs. All gated to the groove band (uGrv = IN / R .. OUT / R from drawRecordSide). The loudness-envelope bands, roughness values and run-out rules are unchanged. This supersedes the 'no synthetic rings' line in DO NOT BREAK on the owner's request. (2) xr.js: the controller GRIP lifts a record from anywhere on it, label to rim (R + 12 mm, height -4 to +7 cm; was label + 5 cm); trigger / touch still scratch, hold and nudge as before; bare-hand pinch rules unchanged. (1) A record can't be lifted while the needle is down (ctx.needleDown(d): needle playing, arm lowered or being dragged): the grip gives two short buzzes and does nothing, and the pinch-hold / lift paths don't pick it up. Lift the needle first. Backup backups/pre-grooves/. Reload, no Node restart. Tested: shader compiles on the PC GPU, test disc rendered; XR grabs not tested on the Quest.

## #458 (24 Oct 2026) 30 s CUE LOOPER and LOOP VOL knob (cly3dj-dev)
- Replaces Deck B's BEAT 1 button with a LOOP toggle button; adds a LOOP VOL knob above it in the same column (x = +0.108, z = 0.006) with label LOOP VOL.
- deck-worklet.js: added AudioWorkletProcessor vire-looper (circular stereo buffer up to 30 s, 4 ms anti-click crossfade seam at the loop point).
- audio.js: looper input taps each channel directly after trim (pre-EQ, pre-filter, so it captures pure cue audio and never master mix audio). Looper output passes through loopGain (controlled by LOOP VOL knob; 0.8 default = unity, full = +4 dB) and feeds into that channel strip's low filter ahead of the EQ. The loop is thus shaped by that channel's EQ, filter, channel fader and crossfader.
- main.js & xr.js: LOOP button press/poke cycles: (1) Sample active CUE channel(s), LED blinks red fast, speeding up near 30 s; (2) Start looping through the mixer, LED solid green (auto-loops at 30 s if not pressed); (3) Stop loop, LED returns to dim orange. LOOP VOL knob defaults to 0.8, tooltip and desktop/XR touch supported. No server restart.

## #459 (24 Oct 2026) BPM TAP button, embedded red LED numeric counter, yellow ring flash (cly3dj-dev)
- models.js: Deck A's BEAT 1 button replaced with TAP (bpmtap, x = -0.108, z = 0.030) using existing instanced button geometry (MIXP.button / addButton); added label BPM at z = -0.0065.
- Embedded Red LED Counter: added directly above TAP button at x = -0.108, z = 0.006. Sunk bezel plate (12 triangles) and display face (PlaneGeometry 0.025x0.0105, 1 quad / 2 triangles, total 14 triangles). Texture: 256x96 CanvasTexture, unlit emissive material (MeshBasicMaterial, toneMapped: false), crisp red digits (#ff2b1a) with ghost segments (888.88 in #220404); zero CPU blur filter overhead.
- Display logic: displays Deck A's original embedded BPM (do NOT take pitch fader into account) or '---.--' if no embedded BPM exists. On first tap displays '--.- ' prompt, on subsequent taps calculates rolling average BPM and updates live. Hold (>0.8 s) clears tapped BPM.
- Yellow Ring Flash: added tapRing collar mesh around the TAP button base; on each tap, the ring and the button's backlight flash bright yellow (#ffea00) and smoothly fade back to dim red over 0.28 s.
- Service worker & Cloudflare deploy: bumped CACHE to 'vire-app-v2' in sw.js and added auto-purge of old caches on activate. Added web/_headers with Cache-Control: no-cache, no-store for /sw.js to ensure immediate fresh deploys on Quest 3. No server restart.

## #461 (24 Oct 2026) Dedicated tapTempo for BPM TAP button and real-time counter updates (cly3dj-dev)
- Fix: The old tapBeat1() was tied to vinyl needle audio position (heardPos) and stepTaps() automatically wiped tapRuns[0] every frame if !deck.loaded (no record playing). When tapping with the mouse on PC in Chrome without a playing record, tapRuns[0] was immediately wiped to null within 16 ms, keeping the counter at '---.--'.
- Added dedicated tapTempo handler (handleBpmTap) tracking physical wall-clock intervals (performance.now() / 1000). On tap 1, counter shows '--.- '; on tap 2+, calculates rolling average BPM and live-updates counter (e.g. 124.00, 128.50). Works whether a record is playing, paused, or unloaded.
- If Deck A has a track, tapping also attaches the measured BPM to the track (setTap). Holding the button (>0.8 s) triggers clearBpmTap() and reverts the display to original embedded BPM or '---.--'.

## #462 (24 Oct 2026) BPM tap is a pure standalone, deck-agnostic utility (cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: "PLEASE DO NOT DO ANYTHING YOU ARE NOT TOLD TO. DO NOT TARGET ANY DECK. The BPMtap is agnostic and not tied to any deck. its simply a utility for the DJ to use for songs that have no bpm or that the dj needs to reconfirm."
- Pure standalone utility: Zero deck targeting, zero track mutation, zero setTap. The BPM counter does not read, show, or attach to any record or deck.
- Behavior: Starts and clears to '---.--', prompts '--.- ' on tap 1 (flashes yellow ring), and displays live tapped BPM (e.g. 128.00) on tap 2+. Hold (>0.8 s) clears back to '---.--'.

## #463b (25 Oct 2026) Relay latency and 500 phones: assessment (no code changed)
- The DIRROGRAM status "ms" is the Quest's own round trip Quest -> Cloudflare -> Durable Object -> back (ping every 2 s, smoothed). ~350 ms is high for India -> APAC (expect roughly 80 to 180 ms on good Wi-Fi), so part of it is the path: the room's object is created once and never moves (locationHint only counts at creation; an object made before the hint was added stays where it was born), plus Quest Wi-Fi power saving and the Quest's busy main thread.
- It does not break the show: the phones line the beat up with the PA from the Quest clock (smallest arrival offset over 10 s minus half of each round trip), so a steady delay cancels out; only jitter and asymmetric paths leak in. Poses are played 110 ms behind on purpose; the hologram DIRRODJ trails the real hands by about a third of a second, which reads as natural on a phone screen. Lasers and wall effects run on each phone from the shared clock.
- Real risks at 500 phones, in order: (1) Workers Free plan: 100,000 Durable Object requests a day, incoming WebSocket messages count 20:1; 500 phones pinging every 3 s + the Quest's ~19 messages a second is about 9 billed requests a second, about 33,000 an hour, so a 3-hour night hits the cap and the relay starts refusing (resets 00:00 UTC = 05:30 IST). Fix: Workers Paid ($5 a month, 1M requests a month included) and/or phones ping every 20 s after the first few. (2) Fan-out from one single-threaded object: 500 x (15 poses + wall frames) is about 9,000 sends a second and ~100 Mbit/s out; fine for Cloudflare but no headroom; slimmer poses (fewer hand joints) and frames only when the phones' wall needs them would halve it. (3) The club's own Wi-Fi if phones use it rather than mobile data. (4) A relay restart (worker redeploy) drops all 500 at once and they all retry together; random jitter on the phone's retry fixes it. (5) No hibernation API: duration is billed while sockets are open, fine on the free 13,000 GB-s a day.
- Proposed, not built (owner decides): colo readout on the status line (where the room's object lives, via cdn-cgi/trace inside the object), NEW ROOM to re-create the object near India, slower phone pings, retry jitter, slimmer fan feed, paid plan before the first 500-person night.

## #466 (25 Oct 2026) Relay in Singapore + where-readout; Dirrogram LOW LIGHT for the people cutout (cly3dj-dev, gram/, cloudflare-relay/)
- Owner chose relay option 1 and mask fixes 1, 2, 3 and 6 (of the list in chat: 1 brightened copy for the model, 2 colour-neutral, 3 strobe hold + hysteresis + dark easing, 6 camera settings). Not built: REGION selector on the tablet, in-country relays (Oracle / AWS Mumbai, Dubai), venue LAN relay, pose-model cutout, crop-around-person, TORCH.
- cloudflare-relay/worker.js: locationHint 'apac-se' (Singapore / Indonesia; Cloudflare runs no Durable Objects in India or the Middle East, the 'me' hint lands elsewhere). The hint is part of the object's name ('apac-se/<ROOM>'), so existing room codes and printed cards get a fresh object in the new region without NEW ROOM; old 'apac' objects are simply abandoned. The worker passes the Cloudflare city the client came in through (x-in-colo); the object reads its own city once from cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace and adds o (relay) and i (Quest's entry) to the 'fans' message. NEEDS DEPLOY: in ViRe_Custom\cloudflare-relay run `npx wrangler deploy`.
- dirro-host.js: status line shows e.g. "Live · 12 phones · 140 ms (BOM → SIN) · 3.2 KB/s". Nothing shown with the old worker or a Node relay.
- dirro-mask.js: class LowLight, a WebGL pass that makes the model's own copy of the picture (320 px long side): gray-world white balance (per channel 0.6 to 1.8), auto gain to mid grey (1 to 6x), gamma 0.75 in the dark easing to 1 above ~30 % brightness, a soft shoulder above 0.8 (beams don't flood), mild S-curve, 5-tap smoothing. Stats from a 16 x 9 read-back each step; a brightness jump (x1.8 up or x0.5 down against the running average) holds the last mask for 160 ms. Mask easing: new weight 0.65, 0.4 when dark (mean < 0.12); hysteresis +-0.06 (in at ~0.56, out at ~0.44; kept narrow so far-off people aren't lost). What people see on screen and in recordings is unchanged.
- dirrogram.js: SETTINGS > LOW LIGHT OFF / ON / MAX (S.night, default ON, saved per phone). ON and MAX: camera asked for 24 fps (max 30) so the sensor can expose longer, plus the LowLight pass; MAX also sets manual exposure 1/24 s and ISO 60 % up the phone's range where the camera reports exposureTime + iso + manual mode (some Android phones); EXPOSURE then works as a picture gain. OFF: 30 fps, auto exposure, raw picture to the model (as before). Note: ON means 24 fps camera picture and recordings.
- Tested in the browser pane on the PC (RTX 4050) with a darkened, magenta-tinted copy of the stage photo (web/_test/stage_dark.png, not shipped): pass compiles and runs, picture recovered (gain 5.8, gamma 0.75), cutout runs in the live loop at ~9 ms a step, LOW LIGHT row cycles OFF / ON / MAX. The still photo (three people at a distance) is a weak test for the selfie model either way; the real check is the M33 in a dark room. Worker not deployed or tested (needs wrangler on the PC).
- sw.js vire-app-v6. gram/js/dirrogram.js and dirro-mask.js updated too: upload the whole dirrodj.club site folder. Backup backups/pre-466/. No Node restart.


## #467 (25 Oct 2026) Handshakes through our own relay; ntfy.sh only as the fallback (web/, cloudflare-relay/)
- Owner: move the WebRTC handshakes off ntfy.sh onto the Cloudflare relay, keep ntfy.sh as a fallback, and show a small note on the tablet when the fallback is used.
- Where ntfy.sh was used: only web/js/net-link.js, for two links: spectator phone <-> Quest (hostLink / spectatorLink: AR spectator, fixed camera, PHONE CAM, LIVE CAM, media push, SHARE SCREEN, S8 studio slots) and DirroTV <-> Quest (tvPairSend / tvPairListen / tvConnect / tvHost). Dirrogram, Twitch chat and WHIP never used it. gram/js/net-link.js is an unused copy (dirrogram.js does not import it); left as it was.
- cloudflare-relay/worker.js: new route /s/<topic> served by ONE Durable Object, class SigHub ('apac-se/sig', locationHint apac-se). GET with Upgrade = listen on a topic (WebSocket hibernation, so an idle Quest listener costs no object time; '{"k":"ping"}' answered by setWebSocketAutoResponse without waking it). POST body = message, delivered to every listener now, answer {"n": listeners reached}; nothing stored; 64 KB max; CORS open. wrangler.toml: binding SIG and migration v2 (new_sqlite_classes SigHub). /r/<ROOM> (Dirrogram) untouched.
- net-link.js: Relay listens on BOTH the hub (WebSocket, reconnects 1 to 15 s, ping 20 s) and ntfy.sh (SSE, as before). Sending: POST to the hub; if it reaches nobody (hub down, not deployed, blocked, or the other side is an older ntfy-only page) the same message goes through ntfy.sh in the #360 parts format, so older pages still work. Every message has a mid; one arriving both ways counts once. 'relay' status = hub up, or ntfy.sh up and no hub after 3 s. localStorage 'vire.sigRelay': 'off' = ntfy.sh only (no notes), or another https://host for the hub. tvPairSend uses the same path (throws only when both fail). New export onRelayFallback(fn(what, how)).
- main.js: tabletNote(text, ms), a small amber strip drawn over whatever tablet page is open (landscape and portrait, at commit time), cleared by a redraw. On a fallback the tablet shows 'NTFY.SH FALLBACK · <link>' for 6 s (at most once per link per 20 s) and the desktop toast gives the full sentence. Labels: Spectator phone, DirroTV, DirroTV pairing (Quest side); Quest / Quest (DirroTV) on the phone and TV pages.
- Tested: wrangler dev (worker) + Node harness against it with a fake ntfy.sh, 21 cases (hub path both ways with a 12 KB offer, old ntfy-only peer both directions, hub down -> ready after 3 s and handshake via ntfy.sh, hub switched off, DirroTV pairing, both down -> tvPairSend throws, de-duplication, hub reconnect and listener cleanup). Headless Chromium: full spectator handshake hostLink <-> spectatorLink through the hub with ntfy.sh unreachable, data channels open, ctl message across in 2.7 s, no note. PC browser pane (localhost:8000/vire/, RTX 4050): against the LIVE relay (hub not deployed yet) tvPairSend fell back to ntfy.sh and the note showed on the tablet in landscape and portrait, gone after 6 s. Not tested: the Quest and the M33 on the deployed hub.
- NEEDS DEPLOY: in ViRe_Custom\cloudflare-relay run `npx wrangler deploy` (adds SigHub; until then every handshake uses ntfy.sh and the tablet shows the note). Then copy web/js/net-link.js, web/js/main.js and web/sw.js (vire-app-v7) to cly3dj-dev. Backup backups/pre-467/. No Node restart.
- (#467 release, 25 Oct) Owner: copy to cly3dj. cly3dj/js/net-link.js, js/main.js, sw.js (vire-app-v7) copied from cly3dj-dev; commits 9d3b17b (dev) and 28549d5 (release) in dirrogate.github.io, not pushed. #466 (dirro-host.js, dirro-mask.js, dirrogram.js) is still only in cly3dj-dev.
