## #469 (25 Oct 2026) Robot hands = the DJ's drawn hands (cly3dj-dev 8b39bd2, gram/)
- Owner: in DirroDj📹 (so Twitch and venue.html / TV too) the robot's glove should sit where the DJ's glove / 3D hand is; agreed approach: one source of truth.
- Before: the robot hands were rebuilt from the bare grip (guessed wrist 7 cm back, generic curl from trigger / grip, #247 fingertip nudge onto the ball), so none of the pinch slide, PINCH FIT or HAND FIT reached them.
- Quest: director.js xrPose: a controller with a posed ctlHand gives the robot that hand's 25 joints (world, after the pinch slide), via ctlhands.js skinHandEntry (p / f / s for the other avatars + joints, skin: true, gs = GLOVE SIZE). robot2.js update: skin hands are driven by driveGloveHand (middle knuckle on the skin knuckle, no fingertip shift, as the headset glove) and scaled by gs; other hands keep the synthetic path (node position / scale restored). xr.js ctlHandStep: in CONTROLLER look the hand is still created and posed but hidden (holder off, no pinch slide), so the robot gets real fingers there too; h.args = [t, g, thumb, pin] kept by ctlhands update().
- Phones: new hand entry kind 'k' = the drawn hand root's pose (rig space) + its pose numbers. Camera phone (spectator-host handsTick): [x y z qx qy qz qw, t, g, th, pin, soft, grip x y z q] (grip for the phone's CONTROLLER look); phonehands.js poses its hand from it (pin included); spectator-client.js robot poses a hidden skin hand (ctlhands makeSkinPoser) and drives the glove from its joints. Dirrogram (dirro-host): [x y z q, th, pin, soft] (trigger / grip already in av.c), about 3 numbers more per hand than before; the show config 'c' now carries ha (HAND FIT / PINCH FIT values) so the phones place the hand the same; dirrogram.js interpolates 'k' like 'c' and poses the robot from a skin poser (robot root scale included). Older phones read 'k' as a controller (fallback).
- Checked in an emulated Quest 3 on the crossfader (controller tipped 40 deg): GLOVE look: robot middle knuckle 0 mm and robot index tip 0 mm from the headset glove's, at rest and pinched; 3D HANDS: knuckle 0 mm, tip 8 mm (rest) / 24 mm (pinched) from the skin tip (the glove's fingers are its own shape, as the headset glove). Renders: headset vs robot, both looks. Dirrogram page fed 'k' poses: robot hand skin-driven, glove size 0.94. Camera-phone hands: root at the drawn root.
- sw.js vire-app-v8. gram/js: dirrogram.js, robot2.js, ctlhands.js, director.js copied (upload the whole dirrodj.club site folder incl. gram/). Backup backups/pre-469/. No Node restart.

## #470 (25 Oct 2026) LOOPER refined: 4-state button with LED ring, LOOPER edit page, 4 slots, loop banks (web/)
- Owner's mechanic. The mixer LOOP button (deck B column) has an LED ring (models.js loopRing, same rounded collar as the TAP ring, one draw call). EMPTY = ring off; press = sample the CUE channel(s) after TRIM (ring RED, blinks in the last 5 s of 30); press = loop it at once (GREEN); press = stop, loop kept (AMBER BLINKING); press = play again from IN; hold 0.8 s in any state = clear (ring blinks yellow), next press records. Cleared = the slots keep theirs. Hold works with the mouse and fingertip / controller-tip pokes (xr.js holdLooper keep-alive, 0.15 s tracking gap cancels, as BEAT 1); a trigger grab-press does not hold. Holding to clear a stopped loop plays it for the 0.8 s first (the press acts on the way down, like real loopers).
- deck-worklet.js Looper: states empty / rec / play / stop; in / out (region a..b, samples) with the 4 ms seam fade at both; stop / clear fade out 6 ms (no click); 'load' arms a saved loop (state stop); after a take it posts the recorded audio once ('data', transferred) for the waveform and slots; playhead 'lpos' 20x a second while playing. audio.js: looperClear, looperRegion, looperLoad.
- New js/loopbank.js (sw.js APP, cache vire-app-v9): browser private storage (OPFS) as plain files for later export: vire-loops/slots/slot<n>.json + slot<n>_A.wav / _B.wav (16-bit stereo WAV per mixer channel sampled, full take; JSON = in / out, source, BPM at the take, time) and vire-loops/banks/<name>/ the same set per saved bank. Slots load at the context's sample rate (resampled if the WAV differs).
- Tablet LOOPER page (DJ TOOLS > LOOP, or LOOP on the mixer page: landscape bottom bar after WALL; portrait bottom row after RESET MIX; the button shows the looper state colour, amber blinking when armed): transport button (same four steps, long press = clear), SLOT 1-4 (tap a filled slot = load + ARM the mixer looper, amber blinking; tap an empty slot = store the current loop there; STORE ▸ then a slot = overwrite; long press a slot then tap = empty it), SAVE BANK (name on the tablet keyboard, saves all 4 slots), LOAD BANK (LOOP BANKS page: tap = load into the 4 slots, long press = delete). Overview waveform of the whole take: drag = the nearer bar (IN green / OUT red), outside dimmed, white playhead. Close-up around the selected bar (ZOOM ±1 s / ±¼ s / ±50 ms): drag slides the audio under the centred bar. IN / OUT pick, ◀◀ 10 ◀ 1 1 ▶ 10 ▶▶ ms. Desktop keys on the page: Left / Right 1 ms (Shift 10, Alt 100), Up / Down switch IN / OUT. Readout: IN, OUT, loop length, beats at the take's BPM (ID3 / RB / tapped BPM x speed) when known, source, slot. Editing a loaded slot's points saves them to that slot.
- GEAR HERE pin (XR only): landscape 120 px wide (LOOP joined the bar); portrait moved above the button rows. Test hook window.vire._loop.
- Tested in the browser pane on the PC (RTX 4050, localhost:8000/vire/) with an oscillator into channel A's trim: states + ring colours, audible loop out of LOOP VOL, region / playhead, store slot 1 + 2 (WAV 355 KB for 2 s), hold-clear, save bank, wipe, load bank (points kept), slot tap = armed then plays from IN, slots back after a reload; LOOPER, LOOP BANKS, DJ TOOLS and mixer pages in landscape and portrait. Test data removed. Not tried in the headset.
- Backup backups/pre-470/. No Node restart.

## #473 (25 Oct 2026) LOOP MASTER: UNDO (20 steps) and the clear hint (web/, cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: hint reads 'HOLD PLAY/REC BTTN TO CLEAR' (landscape on two lines beside UNDO, portrait one line); an UNDO gadget under +10MS (amber, dim when empty; no step count shown, owner), at least 10 levels, so a mistaken clear or wrong IN / OUT can be put back.
- main.js loopUndo (max 20). Steps: IN / OUT changes (drag, nudges, RANGE ALL; changes within 0.8 s of each other = one step), CLEAR of the current loop (undo = the loop back ARMED), loading a slot over the current loop (undo = previous loop back ARMED), storing into / overwriting / emptying a slot (slot files rewritten), LOAD BANK over the 4 slots (undo = old 4 slots back), deleting a bank (its audio is read into memory first, undo writes the bank back). Undo is refused while recording. UNDO also on the LOOP BANKS page; Ctrl+Z on the PC. Undo history is for this session only (not saved).
- Tested in the browser pane on the PC: every step kind, 20-step cap, both layouts. INCIDENT: the pane's storage already held the owner's own loops (slots 1-4 and bank 'BANK 25 OCT 1426', music from deck B); the test overwrote working slots 1 and 2. Restored all 4 slots from his bank (unchanged), test bank removed. Rule: before a looper test, check vire-loops in the pane's storage and keep / restore what is there.
- Backup backups/pre-473/main.js. No Node restart.

## #474 / #475 (25 Oct 2026) LOOP MASTER: CUT / COPY / PASTE across slots, REC/PLAY/CLR, no hint (web/, cly3dj-dev)
- Owner: the row under the nudges is CUT / COPY / PASTE / UNDO (no step count on UNDO); the hint is gone; the big button reads REC/PLAY/CLR (landscape: stacked on three lines; colour = state; the cyan line above still says READY / REC-> / LOOP-> / ARMED). RANGE ALL was cut off on its button: landscape IN 44 px, OUT 50 px, RANGE ALL takes the rest.
- Sample-editor rules (main.js loopClip, loopCopy, loopPaste, loopReplace): COPY = the IN..OUT audio of each channel to a clipboard (kept for the session, across slot changes). CUT = copy, then that part leaves the take (IN..OUT = the whole rest; refused if under 0.25 s would be left). PASTE = insert the clipboard at the selected marker (IN or OUT) and widen IN..OUT by its length (COPY + PASTE at OUT doubles a loop); takes stay within 30 s (the paste is cut short and says so). With no take, PASTE makes the clipboard the loop. A playing loop keeps playing after an edit. Ctrl+X / C / V on the PC.
- Across slots (owner): an edit on a take loaded from a slot is written into that slot at once; UNDO (kind 'edit') puts the take and the slot file back. Tapping an EMPTY slot now selects it as the target (looper empty, slot n; PASTE fills and saves it); STORE then a slot still stores the whole current loop (the #470 'tap an empty slot = store' is replaced).
- Tested in the browser pane on the PC with the owner's own slot files backed up first and restored byte for byte afterwards: half a second copied from slot 1 pasted at OUT into slot 2 (slot grew and saved), UNDO restored slot 2 exactly; slot 4 emptied, selected, PASTE filled and saved it; earlier run: COPY / PASTE doubling, CUT while playing, UNDO chain, PASTE into nothing. Layout checked (RANGE ALL whole, REC/PLAY/CLR stacked). Not tried in the headset.
- Backups backups/pre-473/main.474.js. No Node restart.

## #477 TRIM RANGE -12 .. +24 dB (25 Oct)
Owner: old records sound quiet even with TRIM up. Chain at defaults sits ~8.7 dB under the file (fader 0.8 = -3.9, bus -2.5, master 0.8 = -2.3) and old max was trim +12, so a -20 dBFS file never got near the limiter. TRIM knob is now asymmetric: left half 0 .. -12 dB, right half 0 .. +24 dB (centre still unity, so existing knob positions are unchanged below centre; above centre the same position gives twice the dB). audio.js setTrim, main.js tooltip text. sw.js cache v10. Dev only; release not copied. Not heard on the Quest.

## #478 MOVIE PROPS (25 Oct)
Owner: set-dressing props for filming the CyberGrunge scenes (Mumbai alley, later old Dubai, Singapore). New page VJ TOOLS > MOVIE PROPS (16th tile, `TOOLS_NAME.props`, page `drawPropsPage` in main.js), new module `web/js/movieprops.js` (`MovieProps` manager, built in main.js next to `fx`, `mprops`; test hook `vire._mp.mp`).
- PUDDLE: plane cut out by a MATTE picture (white = wet; premade SCATTERED / BIG PUDDLE / GUTTER / CRACKED made in code, or your own pictures kept in OPFS `vire-props/matte`), WETNESS, RIPPLES, RAIN DROPS, REFLECTION, DARKEN, INVERT, SIZE. One custom-blended shader: darkens the ground and adds reflections of the 4 nearest NEON signs (ray-plane maths in the shader, blurred by the ripples; no second render pass, cheap on the Quest). No sky / panorama reflection (a dusk gradient only).
- RAIN: line-segment streaks falling in a box (amount, speed, length, wind, height, size). FOG: 30 soft instanced puffs (FOG low / SMOKE rises), density, drift, colour, height, size.
- NEON: typed text (English / Hindi, Intl.Segmenter graphemes for the vertical stack), fonts NOTO OUTLINE / NOTO SOLID / MONOTON, 7 colours, FRAME, FLICKER, GLOW, PRESETS (Hindi words), EDIT TEXT through the tablet keyboard. Additive card, `Intl.Segmenter` vertical, shaped by the browser. GRAFFITI: PNG card (graff1 = black line art, tinted white by default; graff2 = colour sticker; ADD PNG), TINT, BRIGHTNESS, OPACITY, GLOW. GLB: any .glb added to storage (ADD GLB), scaled to 1.2 m on load.
- Handles (DJ-only, USER_LAYER), same as the WATER (#379): floor props (puddle, rain, fog) carry by the ring on its post (stay level) and size w x d with the cube on the other post; wall props (neon, graffiti, GLB) carry by the ring (any angle) and size uniformly with the cube, or grip with both hands and stretch (`scaleTo`).
- Saved in localStorage `vire.movieprops` (a list), restored at start. CLEAR ALL (tap twice).
- Files: `web/fonts/` (Noto Sans Devanagari Bold, Monoton: SIL OFL), `web/graff/graff1.png`, `graff2.png` (owner's), sw.js cache v11 + the new files. Backup `backups/pre-478/main.js`, `sw.js`.
- ADD buttons open a file picker: works on the desktop page; in a headset session the picker may not open (add files from the desktop / before entering VR). Added files live in the browser's private storage (OPFS) of the device they were added on, so they do not travel to the Quest by themselves.
- Credits for the end of the movie: the three Sketchfab "Standard licence" GLBs in CyberGrungeRef (air conditioner by Prince Obrymec, steampunk street light by assetfactory, PS1 GT1-style car by Aike) are NOT in the repo and must stay out of the public one; check each licence before using them in a published video.
- Not tested on the Quest. No Node restart needed (static files only).

## #479 MOVIE PROPS PLACEMENT LASER (25 Oct)
Owner: filming mode (not performing), wants to place props with a pointer. Right controller B (gamepad button 5) toggles a beam (also PLACE LASER (B) on the MOVIE PROPS page). Class `Placer` in movieprops.js, called from xr.js `update()` (right controller only, not bare hands).
- Aim at a prop: its box lights up (rain / fog are aimed at their ground footprint, so a puddle inside wins). Trigger takes hold, release puts down.
- Held: wall props (neon, graffiti, GLB) ride the beam like a lever (hand moves it); floor props (puddle, rain, fog) slide along the floor where the beam lands and stay level. Thumbstick back / forward = closer / away; left / right = turn (yaw); grip + stick up / down = bigger / smaller.
- While the laser is on, the right controller's trigger / grip no longer grab gear and its thumbstick no longer nudges faders / scrolls the crate (xr.js `down` and the thumbstick block check `ctx.mprops.placer.active(st)`). The left controller is unchanged. B again turns it off; the beam is DJ-only (USER_LAYER) but a Quest recording of the eyes would show it, so switch it off before filming.
- Tested with a simulated controller in the PC pane (aim, grab, carry, pull, turn, resize, floor slide); not on the Quest. Backup `backups/pre-479/`. No Node restart needed.

## #480 WET COBBLES, LAMP, LIT + DEPTH-AWARE FOG, FOCUS PULL (25 Oct)
Owner (filming mode, bloom / quality over live frame rate): the Mumbai / foggy-cobble look. All in movieprops.js + `drawPropsPage` in main.js.
- WET COBBLES: puddle matte `COBBLES` (button WET COBBLES; 6 x 8 staggered stone tile, tiled by STONE SIZE). Every stone is a small dome (its slope tilts the reflection), tops are damp and rougher, joints hold the water; lamp / neon reflections are smeared into long streaks along the sign's height (5 taps). Uses the same 4-nearest-light reflection as before (neon signs AND lamps).
- LAMP prop (new, LAMP +): a halo card that always faces the viewer (colour, brightness, flicker, size). It is reflected in the puddles, lights the fog, and (out of focus) opens into a bigger, fainter disc (bokeh). Cheap stand-in for bloom: no post-processing pass.
- FOG: lit per puff by the 4 nearest lamps / neon signs (colour and falloff; AMBIENT slider, LIT BY LAMPS / NEON toggle), and DEPTH-AWARE: fog fades out against real walls / people / hands using the Quest depth map (`realGap` copied from laser.js, fed by `mprops.setDepth(realDepth.laserData())` in `stepRealWorld`; only while the headset gives depth). Not testable on the PC; needs a Quest check. REAL OCCLUSION (VJ TOOLS) also hides fog behind real things (hard edge).
- FOCUS PULL (FOCUS button on the page header): FOCUS ON/OFF, DISTANCE (0.3 .. 30 m), APERTURE, FOCUS ON SELECTED PROP, SET A / SET B, RACK TIME, RACK A > B / B > A (smooth rack focus). Softens only the virtual layer (neon / graffiti by mip bias per object, lamp halos by size, puddle reflections per pixel); the passthrough picture cannot be blurred. Saved in localStorage `vire.movieprops.cam`.
- sw.js cache v12. Backup `backups/pre-480/`. Test hooks `vire._mp.mp`, `vire._mp.setTab('cam'|'props')`. No Node restart needed. Not tested on the Quest (depth-aware fog in particular).

## #482 SURFACES (texture-map ground)
- GROUND + (VJ TOOLS > MOVIE PROPS) = a Puddle prop with `surf` set. Shader `surfColor()` in movieprops.js: parallax from the displacement map, relief lighting from displacement/normal, AO, roughness, damp darkening, water pooling in the low parts when WETNESS rises, lamp/neon reflections, lit by the 4 nearest lamps/neon; controls WETNESS, COVER, AMBIENT LIGHT, REFLECTION, RIPPLES, RAIN DROPS, size.

Slots: DIFFUSE, DISPLACEMENT, ROUGHNESS, NORMAL (OpenGL), AO. User pictures go to OPFS kind `surf` (`NAME__slot.ext`), per device. TILE SIZE (real metres per picture) and DISPLACEMENT DEPTH per surface.
- UI: puddle controls > EDIT / ADD SURFACES (mpTab 'surf'): cycle surface, NEW SURFACE (keyboard), DELETE, TILE SIZE, DEPTH, each slot (tap = add picture, CLEAR). Old procedural mattes unchanged (USE A TEXTURE SURFACE / USE A MATTE INSTEAD).

`Decal` (ground, floor prop, `type 'decal'`) and `WallDecal` (`'walldecal'`) in `web/js/movieprops.js`, subclasses of Graffiti with a lit ShaderMaterial (`DEC_F`): nearest 4 lamps (diffuse + wet specular glints), WETNESS darkens / glosses, RAIN RINGS (ground) or RAIN STREAKS (wall). Any transparent PNG (ADD PNG) or stock `web/graff/decal_*.png` (lane, arrow, tyre, oil, leaves, poster, rust, stickers; `DECAL_GROUND` / `DECAL_WALL`). Props page buttons GROUND DECAL + / WALL DECAL +. Also fixed: `biasMat` was never defined, so NEON and GRAFFITI props threw on spawn; now defined at the top of movieprops.js. Cache `vire-app-v25`. No Node restart.

## #494-#495 backdrop reflection, rivulets, crate float, wet decks
- Ground shader (`surfColor`) traces the reflected ray to the BACKDROP plate and shows the photo in the wet ground (uPT/uPI/uPOn/uPB, set in Puddle.tick from the visible plate; ripples and roughness blur it).
- Wall decal rain is clear RIVULETS (`DEC_F`): wandering, stop-start runs, fake refraction of the picture behind, bead lens at the head, no tint (darker rim + lamp glints). RIVULET AMOUNT (density, rainAmt) + RIVULET SPEED sliders (`flow`, accumulated in uTf so changing it never jumps). Ground decal keeps RAIN RINGS.
- CRATE: FLOAT toggle + FLOAT HEIGHT (faked bob / rock / drift, no physics).
- Decks and records get a wet look (darker, glossier) when a RAIN prop's box covers the decks (`tickGearWet`, `wetGear` in main.js). The alley scene's rain sits down the street, so decks stay dry until a rain prop is moved over them.
- Cache `vire-app-v26`. No Node restart.
- #495c rivulet refraction no longer samples outside the decal: the decal's shape comes from the unbent texture, only colour is bent (it had punched holes).
- #496 wet decks also get world-space water beads and a wet rim sheen (shader patch per deck / record material, `wetPatch` in main.js).

## #497 random ripples, decks dry again
- Ground ripples (`height()` in PUD_F) now use 3 drop sizes and re-roll every cycle per cell: whether a drop lands, where, how big, how hard (before: one fixed ring per cell, same spot, same size, repeating). Ground decal rings use the same idea.
- The wet look on decks / records (#494 tickGearWet, #496 beads) was removed again: it looked fake. Decks and records are not affected by rain props.
- #497b drop frequency raised (rr > 0.3) and ring size 0.14-0.38 of a cell; test hook `vire._deck` = { pullRecord, placeOnDeck, setMotor, dropNeedleAt, held } puts a library record on a deck from the console.
- #497c ripple cells made smaller (6.0 / 4.2 / 3.0 per metre, biggest ring about 13 cm radius); the first sizes were far too big.
- #497d RIPPLE SIZE slider (ground / surface puddles, `ripSz` 0.3-2.5x, default 1; scales the ripple grid so drops and rings grow or shrink together).

## #498 GUIDES also hide the ring / cube handles
The cyan ring (carry) and grey cube (size) on every movie prop are DJ-only handles. The GUIDES toggle (was GUIDE BOXES) on the MOVIE PROPS page now hides them together with the blue boxes (`MovieProps.tick`). Grabbing with the B-button laser does not need them. Cache `vire-app-v27`. No Node restart.

## #499 Fixed camera: camera picker + lens distortion k1 / k2 (owner, 26 Oct, for the DirroVJ laptop rig with a wide webcam e.g. Lenovo 300 FHD)
- fixedcam.js. Camera picker: a dropdown (`#fxDev`, shown when 2+ cameras) lists `enumerateDevices` video inputs; the choice is kept in `vire.fixcam.dev` and opened with `deviceId: exact`; an unplugged camera falls back to main / selfie. Calibration is saved per device (`vire.fixcam.d.<id>`), main / selfie keep their old keys. The Main / selfie button clears the device choice. The lens-touch prompt names the device.
- Distortion: `solvePose(pts, pix, W, H, lens, opts)` refines the best pinhole fit with radial k1, k2 (pixel = centre + f n (1 + k1 r^2 + k2 r^4)) when there are 6+ points with the lens touch (7+ without), with a gentle pull toward 0 (K_W 6 px per unit); kept only if |k| < 1 and the pixel rms drops by 10%+. Saved as `pose.k`. Info line shows `lens k1 …`.
- Picture: two videos now. `raw` = the camera (corner detection, taps and point images read it; shown while calibrating). `video` (exported, used by the gear background, studio, look, PHONE CAM / LIVE) = raw, or when `pose.k` is set a WebGL2 undistorted copy (own small context, closed-form per pixel, same size and focal length, so `fov` / `ff` and every render stay plain pinhole), sent through canvas.captureStream(0) with requestFrame per camera frame. `route()` switches on start / stop / calibrate / cancel / solve / reset view. After a solve the check marks are moved onto the straightened picture (`undistPx`).
- Tests: synthetic 1920x1080, 60 deg vertical, k1 -0.28 k2 0.08, 6 points (4 table + 2 wall) + lens, 1 cm touch noise, 20 runs: pinhole rms 8.8 px, fov 63.7 (wrong); with k1 / k2 rms 3.2 px, fov 60.3. Headless Chrome: the shader straightens a barrel-distorted grid (1.7% pixel mismatch vs the straight grid, 13.5% before); captureStream -> video plays at full size. Not tested: a real webcam, phone performance with correction on (only active when a calibration found k).
- sw.js cache v28. Backup backups/pre499/.
- Committed in the PC clone: b160cf9 (cly3dj-dev) and d552d48 (release cly3dj = dev), fixedcam.js + sw.js. Owner pushes in GitHub Desktop.

## #504 DirroVJ phase 2: RENDER: LAPTOP (26 Oct)
- Quest (stream.js): STUDIO_DEF.local (default on); `stuLocal` = SHOW + local + DirroVJ says lr + source show + 3D on. Then _layerFrame sends only the show camera pose ('scp') and the overlays pack (_stuPack(null)), no scene render. 'stu' message carries local and O {gear, robot, neon}. Tablet DIRROVJ page: RENDER: LAPTOP / QUEST button (QUEST* = linked page can't render, e.g. the phone).
- DirroVJ (studio.js, vj flag): renders the mirror scene from the scp pose at questNow - 70 ms - TRIM into a half-float MSAA target (transparent), COMP_FS layer 2b tone-maps it (ACES + sRGB, additive light like MR_FS) between the backdrop and the Quest's overlays. sst carries lr: 1. sceneLag = TRIM also holds the mirror back (spectator-client loop), so TRIM is the lip-sync control. prepLocal (spectator-client): hides room, sky, head marker, reticle; shows the rig; OBJECTS toggles for gear / robot / neon; locCam layers 0 + 6 (robot).
- Bug found in test: the deck parts are instanced (deck-inst.js) and copy the decks only while visible; on DirroVJ the rig is hidden until a camera mode, so decks were drawn at zero size (bodies and slipmats missing). prepLocal calls deckInst.update() after showing the rig (and again on undo).
- PC test: DirroVJ 33 fps out, local renders 32/s, framing matches the Quest's FRONT shot, records on both decks, overlays (logo) on top. Quest frames take a while to start after a reconnect (~1 min seen, old behaviour); the local scene renders meanwhile from the poses. Robot: the mirror's robot only moves with XR data, so not seen in the desktop test. Not measured yet: the Quest's frame time saved. Test hook studio._U. sw.js v33. Backup backups/pre504/.

## #505 Mirror part numbering (owner: DirroVJ showed black pucks on the decks, deck B slipmat missing)
- Cause 1: the mirror finds parts by child-index paths. The Quest moves each deck's slipmats into a matPivot inside the platter on its first frame (main.js #275); the phone / DirroVJ never ran that, so the platter's children were numbered differently: mats hidden, parts swapped. spectator-client now builds the same matPivot every frame if missing.
- Cause 2: the 45 adapter (pPipe1, the 'spider') sits in deck A's group at child 4 on the Quest; spidersApply on the mirror re-adds it (appended last), so every later deck child was off by one: deck feet / parts drawn on the deck top and over the mixer (the black pucks). Fix: spiders get userData.mirrorSkip; spectator-host numbers children without records, noMirror and mirrorSkip objects, and spectator-client resolve() filters the same way. Old PerfCap takes recorded with the old numbering may place some parts wrong on replay.
- Test (two tabs): pucks gone, feet under the decks, both slipmats shown. Background-tab throttling on the PC stops the Quest page's packets after a few minutes (records then hide on the mirror after 1.5 s): a two-tab artefact, not on real devices. sw.js v34. Backup backups/pre505/.

## #506 RENDER: QUEST default; DirroVJ phase 4 override (owner, 26 Oct)
- Owner's call after the #504/#505 discussion: the Quest's packed frame (colour + half-res matte) is the truth; the mirror misses robot (needs XR data), lasers / smoke, GoPro, chalkboard, chat panel, LED content without a synced copy, dome without the pano, and every new feature needs mirror code. STUDIO_DEF.local false + one-time migration (q506). RENDER: LAPTOP stays for headroom / 1080p decks-only shots.
- Pairing: studio.js sends a device id (vire.vjId, random, kept) in every 'sst', plus a hello on link open and every 3 s while the studio is off. Quest (stream.js) keeps allowed ids in vire.vjOk (last 12); an unknown id sets vjAsk and the DIRROVJ page shows '<DirroVJ|Camera phone> (<id>) wants the show' with ALLOW / DENY. Until allowed, _stuSync attaches nothing and sends no 'stu', and start() refuses to send the stream key. forgetVjs() clears the list (no button yet). Old phone pages without an id can't run the studio until reloaded.
- Heartbeat: studio on + allowed + no 'sst' for 10 s -> vjLost: red 'DirroVJ silent for 10 s.' + TAKE BACK. TAKE BACK (also a plain button whenever DirroVJ is on): DirroVJ off; if it was live on Twitch the Quest starts its own stream 1.5 s later (DirroVJ stops on the 'stu' off, or a minute after it lost the link).
- PC test: ask shown, nothing attached before ALLOW, attached and running after; simulated silence showed the red row; TAKE BACK turned DirroVJ off. Not tested: the Twitch handover with a real key. sw.js v35. Backup backups/pre506/.

## #507 DirroVJ phase 3: the VJ desk (owner, 26 Oct; floppy page dropped by the owner)
- New js/vjdesk.js (output side, made by studio.js when vj) + vjdesk.html (control window, opened from the DirroVJ start page or the studio bar's 'VJ desk'; BroadcastChannel 'dirrovj', same browser). studio.js draws its composite into compRT and desk.output() mixes the program to the screen (and so to Twitch, which captures the canvas).
- Program: buses A / B (QUEST = the studio composite, FRACTAL, WEBCAM = fixed camera video as it is, LED CLIP = DirroVJ's copy, BLACK), crossfader, CUT, AUTO (0.8 / 2 / 5 s), keys SPACE = AUTO, C = CUT. Desk gets a 256 x 144 JPEG program preview 4 / s.
- FRACTAL: Mandelbrot height map (512 x 512, smooth escape count, set = plateau 0.62, edge spikes) + a heightfield ray march at 320 x 256 nearest-upscaled (chunky); side / top shading by comparing the previous column (fixed concentric rings). Palettes GREEN (plain, contours, brown slopes, grey-mauve top) and ISLAND (sea bands, sand, woods, olive top). Flight: ping-pong zoom (0..7) toward 5 targets, orbit; SPEED / HEIGHT / ZOOM / REACT (bass of the Quest's sound via an AnalyserNode; manual fader, no beat sync). Settings kept in vire.vjFr.
- Scroller desk: DirroVJ reads Twitch chat itself (chat.js, anonymous) on the channel the Quest sends in 'stu' (ch); the Quest forwards new HoloSMS (dirro.sms) as 'vjsms' from _stuSync. Queue with APPROVE / ✕ and a typed line; APPROVE sends 'vjsine' -> spectator-host onVj -> main.js puts it on the Quest's sine scroller (only if streamer.vjAllowed).
- LINE IN: an audio input on the laptop replaces the Quest's sound for Twitch (studio twAudio(), swapped live by the stats loop).
- PC test (Quest tab + DirroVJ tab + desk tab): desk connected, chat joined #dirrogate, FRACTAL on program (GREEN close to the owner's reference screenshots, ISLAND blue sea), CUT / AUTO, typed lines reached the Quest's scroller. Background tabs freeze the output's frame loop (fades finish when it is visible): on the gig the output window is full screen on the HDMI screen. Not tested: LINE IN with a real interface, REACT with music, Twitch with the desk. sw.js v36. Backup backups/pre507/.

## #508 DirroVJ fractal: more shapes, colours, beat (owner: 'the flat plateau is boring', wants the beat to drive the peaks)
- vjdesk.js height pass now writes r = height, g = colour detail, b = inside; four formulas (ftype): MANDEL, JULIA (c runs round a 0.7885 circle: the landscape morphs), SHIP (Burning Ship), MULTI (z^3 + c), each with its own start view and zoom targets (FRACTALS). Inside the set the height comes from orbit traps (min |z| -> domes, min |x|,|y| -> ridges), so no more flat plateau. Height map is HalfFloat now.
- Palettes: GREEN, ISLAND (now rock to snow on top), LAVA, ICE, COPPER (Amiga copper-bar rainbow, 16 hues), NEON (magenta / cyan on purple-black); CYCLE fader rolls LAVA / ICE / COPPER / NEON like Amiga palette cycling.
- BEAT: kick detection on the Quest's sound (bass bins 1-5 jump over 1.32 x their running average, over 0.22, at most every 230 ms); each kick sets a pulse (decays e^-5.5t) that lifts the outside spikes (up to +0.9 x, capped so a spike never reaches the camera) and the inside only 0.12 x, plus a small brightness flash. BEAT fader = amount; PUMP button / P key = a hit by hand; a dot on the desk flashes per kick. REACT (continuous bass swell) stays.
- PC test: GREEN with the textured plateau, SHIP + COPPER rainbow, JULIA + NEON (a cyberpunk city), LAVA domes; PUMP captured mid-jump (spike forest, plateau steady). First cap (1.6 x) let spikes fill the camera: lowered to 0.9 x with a cap. Not tested: kicks from real music (needs the Quest's sound). sw.js v37. Backup backups/pre508/.

## #509 DirroVJ phase 5: DirroTV from DirroVJ (26 Oct)
- How: a paired TV (venue.html) keeps calling 'whoever listens under its key' (tvConnect, retries every few s). While DirroVJ is on the Quest already closes its own tvHost (#380). Now the 'stu' message (only sent after ALLOW, #506) carries the TV key (tv) and the sound setting (tvA); studio.js tvFollow() runs tvHost(key) on DirroVJ: picture = its own canvas (captureStream, a frame per program frame, so the TV sees the program incl. the desk's buses), sound = what Twitch gets (twAudio: Quest sound or LINE IN), 'hi' on ctl open. DirroVJ off ('stu' off) closes it; the Quest reopens its own host (setStudio off -> _tvOpen) and the TV finds it again. No re-pairing either way.
- DirroVJ reports tv off / waiting / live in 'sst'; the DIRROVJ page sub-line shows 'DirroTV ON (from DirroVJ)'. DIRROTV page note: 'DirroVJ is on: paired TVs switch to DirroVJ by themselves.' Still one TV per key at a time (tvHost replaces the link per OFFER), as before.
- PC test (Quest + DirroVJ + venue tabs, a test key): TV live on the Quest; DirroVJ on -> Quest closed its host, DirroVJ 'tv live', the TV got DirroVJ's sound and frames (320 x 180 at the start, WebRTC ramps up; frames only while the DirroVJ tab was in front); DirroVJ off -> TV back on the Quest by itself. Test key removed. sw.js v38. Backup backups/pre509/.

## #512 Dirrogram clips (owner: on Android a clip stopped at 10 s saved as 30 s; fine on PC)
- Cause found: Chrome's MediaRecorder WebM has no Duration (a live format; Chrome itself reports Infinity), so Android galleries guess a length, apparently the recorder's maximum. Desktop players scan the file. Fix: dirrogram.js webmDuration(blob, ms) writes the real length into Segment > Info > Duration (float64, ms x 1e6 / TimecodeScale; overwrites one if present, else appends it and rewrites Info's size as an 8-byte vint; Chrome's Segment size is 'unknown' so nothing else moves). MP4 left as is. PC test: 3.088 s canvas WebM, raw duration Infinity, fixed 3.0878 s.
- Also: a 0.7 s tap guard on REC (a double tap could stop the clip and start a new one that ran to the limit), REC_MAX 45 s (was 30), button shows 's / 45 s', saved name dirrogram-<date>.<ext>, the toast says the real length. Not tested on an Android phone.
- gram/js/dirrogram.js = web copy: owner must upload the whole dirrodj.club site folder. sw.js v41. Backup backups/pre512/.

Both BEAT 1 buttons are gone for good (owner: not coming back; deck B's slot is LOOP, deck A's is BPM TAP). LOCK is therefore tempo only (meet-halfway pitch glide); the phase nudge in `stepSync` can't run because no beat 1 is ever tapped. Toasts no longer tell the DJ to tap BEAT 1. BPM comes from the ID3 tag, else the Rekordbox AverageBpm; no BPM = LOCK refuses. No Node restart.

## #516 DirroComics phase 1: the comic page on DirroVJ (owner, 27 Oct; plan: project doc claude/dirrocomics-plan.md)
- Owner: start building DirroComics; no Quest camera probe page for now; stereo comes from side by side clips captured on the Quest (adb) and imported. Consent log, Dirrogram vertical view, cinegraph masks, AI micro-drama: later phases.
- New js/comics.js (output side, made by vjdesk.js): a strip of panels drawn as a comic page at a 1920x1080 reference (REF): current panel 1120x850 in the middle, about a quarter of the previous / next panels at the edges (lighter), slides between panels, masthead DIRROCOMICS + gear + issue line, footer PANEL n / N + footer text. New bus source DIRROCOMICS ('comic') on A / B; DirroTV and Twitch get it like any program picture.
- Inking in one shader (PAGE_FS, 4 slots): HALFTONE (AM dots, darkness read at each cell centre), HATCH (62 deg lines + cross-hatch in the shadows), BEN-DAY (flat dot fields in 3 levels + solid), POSTER (black / paper), PHOTO; Sobel ink lines (INK), solid blacks; BRIGHT / CONTRAST; FRAME X / Y / ZOOM crop; cream paper 243,234,212, ink 24,21,20. Dots are in panel pixels, so they travel with the panel when it slides.
- ANAGLYPH (black and white, red = left eye): SBS panels (full or half SBS; auto when the name says sbs/3d/stereo/lr or the picture is 1.95:1 or wider) give each eye its half; CONVERGE shifts the eyes (+ = toward the viewer) to put the DJ at zero; SWAP EYES; SPLIT SCREENS gives the eyes different screen angles (halftone 15 / 75 deg, hatch 62 / 118, Ben-Day 0 / 45) so dots don't fuse at the screen plane. Flat panels sit at zero. Captions, frames, masthead: zero. Balloons + SFX: POP = total disparity in px (each eye half).
- Text: captions (Special Elite), speech / thought / shout balloons (Comic Neue Bold, uppercase), SFX (Bangers, white with black outline, tilt). Drawn on two canvases per panel (captions = zero layer, balloons / SFX = pop layer), packed into one RGBA DataTexture (R/G zero ink/alpha, B/A pop ink/alpha). Fonts vendored (OFL): fonts/ComicBangers.woff2, ComicType.woff2, ComicNeueBold.woff2, loaded via import.meta.url.
- Media: IMPORT (images, videos; video panels loop muted), GRAB QUEST (a still of the studio composite, read back from the GPU), GRAB WEBCAM (fixed camera). Stored in OPFS vire-comics/ (media files + strip.json: cfg, panels, cur), so the strip survives reloads. Media loads lazily when a panel comes on screen; off-screen videos pause. DUPLICATE shares the media file (deleted only when no panel uses it).
- Desk (vjdesk.html, DirroComics section): issue / footer text, ANAGLYPH, SPLIT SCREENS, AUTO (off / 8 / 15 / 30 / 60 s, loops), PREV / NEXT (also arrow keys), IMPORT / GRAB, default stereo + style for new panels, strip of thumbnails (click = select, double click = on air, red = on air), editor with a 640x360 preview of the SELECTED panel (not necessarily on air, 3 / s while watched) and all panel controls; text items with size / width / tilt and POS / TAIL buttons: arm, then click the preview to place (POS moves the tail along unless Shift; Alt keeps it armed). Messages: 'cx' ops add / grab / set / move / del / dup / go / next / prev / cfg / watch / list; output sends cxlist (thumbs), cxpv (preview), cxmsg; state carries C.
- Fixes found in testing: a comment pasted mid-line swallowed the rest of the 'del' branch (syntax error); Image.decode() never resolves in a hidden window, so images load with onload; fonts 404'd from the test page (relative to the page): now relative to the module; blank texture is white (paper while a picture loads, was solid ink); POP was applied in full to each eye (doubled).
- Tested on the owner's PC (browser pane, RTX 4050, localhost:8000): _test/comics_test.html runs comics.js alone (store 'vire-comics-test', T.step / T.snap for a hidden pane, and it answers the 'dirrovj' channel like vjdesk.js so vjdesk.html can be tested against it). SBS test image (_test/sbs_test_clyde.jpg, made from DSCF4210 with the person at zero and the wall 12 px behind) + alley clip + matte1: page, halftone, hatch, Ben-Day, captions, balloons, SFX, peeks, anaglyph with split screens checked in snapshots; desk: connect, strip, editor, style change, + THOUGHT, click-to-place, persistence across reloads. index.html?role=camera&vj=1 loads with the new modules. Not tested: the full chain with a live Quest (comic on a bus, GRAB QUEST from a real composite, Twitch / DirroTV), real adb SBS clips from the Quest (check which eye is first: SWAP EYES if inside out), a long video strip's memory use. Test store deleted afterwards.
- sw.js v45 (+ js/comics.js, 3 fonts). No Node restart needed. Backup backups/pre516/.

## #517 DirroComics: anaglyph depth for flat panels (owner: 'it's not actually rendering the anaglyph')
- Cause: in #516 only side by side panels gave the eyes different pictures; a flat picture sat exactly on the screen plane, so with ANAGLYPH on it looked the same as off (only balloons / SFX showed red / cyan).
- Now flat panels get 2.5D depth in ANAGLYPH: new panel fields dmode (0 on screen, 1 behind = whole picture pushed behind the frame, 2 ground = bottom at the screen, top furthest; default 2) and deep (px behind, default 6, max 12). The shader shades each eye at q +- d/2 (uniform G[4]), so the dots move with the picture and carry the depth (surface-locked). Keep deep under about 8 with 12 px dots (half the dots' horizontal period) or the dots lock onto their neighbours.
- Desk: 'Flat depth' (on screen / behind / ground) and 'Depth' next to Stereo; Flat depth is greyed out on SBS panels. Old panels pick up the defaults on load.
- PC test (pane): alley clip, ANAGLYPH, ground 8 px: red / cyan offsets grow toward the top. dev only. No Node restart. Backup backups/pre517/.

## #518 DirroComics: speed, depth maps, LIVE PEOPLE (owner, 27 Oct)
- Speed (owner: the compose panel was slow on the RTX 4050). Measured: page render ~4 ms; a text edit cost 30 to 38 ms (canvas -> getImageData x2 -> JS pixel loop -> DataTexture), on every input event; preview 3 / s as a JPEG data URL; vjdesk.js asked enumerateDevices on every state post. Now: one text canvas per panel (captions in rows 0..ph, balloons / SFX in rows ph+8..2ph+8) uploaded as a CanvasTexture, no pixel loop; redrawn at most every 50 ms while typing / dragging; masthead / footer canvas also straight to the GPU; preview ~8 / s, encoded with toBlob (sent as a Blob, desk shows an object URL); audio input list cached 5 s. Text edit now ~1.5 ms. Biggest remaining factor is Chrome throttling a BACKGROUND TAB (DirroVJ output behind the desk tab): put the output in its own window.
- Depth for flat panels in ANAGLYPH (owner: masks in white = front, gray, dark gray, black = back; CONVERGE applies depth proportionately; captions / balloons stay on POP). Panel fields depth (OPFS file <id>.d.png, in the picture's own space so it follows FRAME / ZOOM), dver (reload stamp), live. CONVERGE on a flat panel = depth in px (conv x 150, max 12): + = white at the screen, the rest behind in proportion to darkness; - = black at the screen, the rest in front. Without a map the Flat depth shape is used (behind / ground). 'deep' (#517) is gone: old panels get conv = deep / 150 on load; new flat panels start at conv 0.04 (6 px). Shader: per eye backward warp with one refinement step (disp at q, then at q +- d/2), dots shaded at the warped point so they stay on the surfaces.
- Desk DEPTH MAP (left column under the preview): EDIT DEPTH opens the panel's picture (read from the same OPFS folder; the store name comes in the state) with the map: brush in FRONT 255 / NEAR 170 / FAR 85 / BACK 0, brush size, PEOPLE (MediaPipe selfie segmentation, IMAGE mode, GPU then CPU, paints people in the picked tone), GROUND RAMP (black top to 200 gray bottom, also the starting map), FILL, SOFTEN (blur), IMPORT DEPTH (any grayscale image, white = near), REMOVE, view picture + depth / depth / picture. Saves itself (PNG) after each stroke / tool and sends set {depth, dver}. Video panels: painted on a frame near the start; LIVE PEOPLE (video panels): DirroVJ runs dirro-mask.js PersonMask on the panel on air and pulls people to the front (max with the map / ground ramp).
- Not built (plan in the project doc): Quest 3 depth for quick stereo (GRAB on the Quest: headset camera still + the WebXR depth map re-projected into that camera with the CAM FIT values, sent as the panel's depth), and painting masks with the Quest controller on the tablet.
- PC test (pane, test page + desk): text edit 1.2 to 1.9 ms; flat photo -> EDIT DEPTH -> PEOPLE: 23% painted FRONT, saved, DirroVJ side loaded it (G = 3, 10 px): the person at zero (no fringe), the wall behind growing toward the top; LIVE PEOPLE on a video panel loaded MediaPipe and bound its mask. Old #517 panel migrated (deep 8 -> conv 0.053). sw.js v46. dev only. No Node restart. Backup backups/pre518/. Test page has an importmap now (dirro-mask.js imports 'three').
- (27 Oct, owner) Quest depth capture is a DirroComics-only feature: a GRAB RGB-D button on the VJ desk (DirroComics section) asks the Quest for a 5 s clip (and a still) of the headset camera + WebXR depth re-projected into that camera, recorded as one side by side RGB-D video (colour left, grayscale depth right, white = near). It arrives as a new panel with stereo type RGB+D. Not part of GRAB LOOP, the LED wall, Twitch or DirroTV. Not built yet.

## #519 VJ desk: buses wouldn't switch, no program picture (owner, 27 Oct)
- Cause 1 (mine): _test/comics_test.html answers the desk on the same 'dirrovj' channel as a stand-in for vjdesk.js. It always reported bus A = DIRROCOMICS, ignored bus / fade messages and sent no program preview; open next to the real DirroVJ, the desk flipped between the two states (FRACTAL snapped back). Now its state is marked test: true; the desk ignores it for 4 s after any state from a real DirroVJ, and says when it is connected to the TEST page.
- Cause 2 (by design since #507): DirroVJ's program output only runs once the Quest turns DirroVJ on, so without the Quest the program window stayed empty. vjdesk.js state now carries live (a program frame in the last 1.5 s); the desk says 'waiting for the Quest' and shows a note over the empty program preview. Buses still switch.
- DirroComics editing without the Quest: vjdesk.js runs comics.tick() every 120 ms while no program frames come and a desk is open (it only draws into its own render targets), so the desk preview, GRAB WEBCAM and AUTO work before the Quest is linked.
- PC test: real DirroVJ (camera page, vj) + test page + desk: FRACTAL stays on bus A for 4 s+ (was flipping back), status 'waiting for the Quest', comic preview arrives. No Node restart.

## #520 GRAB RGB-D (owner: DirroComics only, a button on the VJ desk)
- New js/rgbdgrab.js (Quest). Desk GRAB RGB-D (5 s) / RGB-D STILL -> comics.js op grab rgbd / rgbds -> vjdesk.js requestRgbd -> ctl 'cxgrab' {still, ms} -> spectator-host -> main.js onVj (only for a DirroVJ the DJ allowed, streamer.vjAllowed) -> makeRgbdGrab.start. Needs passthrough with depth sensing (RealDepth ok).
- Capture: headset camera (HEADSET CAM's own stream if it runs, else opened for the grab with the same request: 1280x960 ideal, so CAM FIT's lens holds). Each camera frame (requestVideoFrameCallback captureTime) -> head pose at that time - LAG (own ~0.6 s pose history) x CAM FIT offset / tilt / turn / roll, vertical fov from hfov -> left half: the camera picture; right half: RealDepth's 128x128 depth mesh (same lift as #413, laserData of the valid depth view) drawn from that camera as gray (white 0.35 m to black 5 m; far / empty / depth-jump texels discarded = black). Both halves 640 wide, read back async (projection flipped so rows come top first), drawn on a 1280-wide canvas. Video: canvas.captureStream + MediaRecorder (mp4 avc1 if supported, else webm with its duration written in like #512), 8 Mbit/s, 5 s; still: PNG. Ticked from main.js stepRealWorld right after realDepth.update (the depth texture is only valid in that frame).
- Back to DirroVJ: spectator-host sendCx(blob, name): ctl 'cxput' {n, size, type} + 64 KB chunks on 'file' (refused during a media sync); 'cxst' status lines. spectator-client collects the chunks (cxrx, checked before the media-sync receiver) -> studio.onCx -> vjdesk.js onCx -> comics add. Name quest-rgbd-HHMMSS.mp4/webm/png.
- comics.js: stereo type 3 RGB+D (any file with rgbd / rgb-d in the name; desk Stereo select 'RGB+D'): picture = left half, depth = right half (white = near); CONVERGE = depth px as for flat panels; a painted map on top only adds FRONT (max). Depth editor shows the picture half; its map starts black for RGB+D. LIVE PEOPLE allowed on RGB+D video.
- Test (PC): DirroDJ page loads with the new import; RGB+D panel from a synthetic test_rgbd_clyde.png (photo | mask + ramp) renders with the person at zero and the wall behind (after #521). NOT TESTED: the capture itself (needs the Quest in passthrough with depth); check on the headset: depth lines up with the picture (if not, CAM FIT), mp4 vs webm, file arrives.
- Found while testing: the first #520 build of the page shader made the browser pane's GPU process reset; see #521.

## #521 DirroComics renderer: one small shader per panel (GPU process reset)
- Symptom (pane, after #520): WebGL context lost on the comic page, then no WebGL at all for the site (Chrome blocks 3D after GPU resets until the browser restarts).
- Cause: PAGE_FS drew all four panel slots in one fragment shader; every helper (euv, L, shade, disp, gray) was inlined per slot and per eye, dozens of copies. The #520 RGB+D version took long enough to compile (ANGLE / D3D11) to trip Chrome's GPU watchdog. In the cloud headless check the old shader timed out (> 30 s); the new one renders the first frame in ~7 s even in software.
- Now: paper quad, then up to four panel quads (PANEL_VS places each on its rect + 5 px frame; PANEL_FS shades one panel, shade() called twice at most: qa / qb per eye, edge lines from 2 forward differences instead of 4), then the masthead / footer as a blended quad on top. Same look, same uniforms per panel (slots[i].U). Test hook _slots.
- If a browser shows black comic pages or 'WebGL disabled' after testing #520: restart the browser (Chrome lifts the block on restart). The Claude desktop app's browser pane needs the app restarted.
- sw.js v48. No Node restart. Backups backups/pre519/, pre520/.

## #522 DirroComics issues and exports (owner, 27 Oct: save a whole issue as .dirrocomic, MP4 of the strip, 4-panel strip PDF)
- New js/cxfiles.js: store-only ZIP writer (CRC per 8 MB slice, the blobs stay as parts) and reader (store or deflate via DecompressionStream), minimal PDF writer (one full-page JPEG per page, DCTDecode; Info title / author DirroDJ). Checked: Python's zipfile reads the zip back, pdfinfo reads the PDF.
- Desk, DirroComics section, ISSUE row: SAVE ISSUE = DirroComics_<issue>_<date>.dirrocomic in Downloads: manifest.json {format 'dirrocomic', v 1, saved, cfg, cur, panels with thumbs} + media/<file> for every picture / clip / depth map (read straight from DirroVJ's OPFS folder). OPEN ISSUE (.dirrocomic / .zip): confirm, media written into the folder, then 'load' -> comics.js replaces the strip and deletes files no panel uses (the old issue's). NEW ISSUE: confirm, empty strip, issue number + 1.
- EXPORT MP4 (hold 3 / 4 / 6 / 8 s, 720p / 1080p): DirroVJ plays the strip as on the club screen (each still held, video panels for their own length up to 20 s, 0.8 s slides), renders each frame into its own target, async read-back to a canvas, MediaRecorder (mp4 avc1 first, webm fallback), 8 / 12 Mbit/s, real time: keep the DirroVJ window visible. STOP ends it early. The file comes back to the desk ('cxfile') and downloads.
- EXPORT PDF STRIP: every panel rendered at 2x (panel + frame only, as on screen incl. text and anaglyph if on), 4 per page in a row like a newspaper strip, masthead DIRROCOMICS + issue line, PAGE n / N + footer text; page = A4 landscape width, strip height.
- Test (cloud headless Chromium, software GL, since the pane's WebGL was blocked after #520): test page + real desk: SAVE (3 panels, 2.7 MB, zip valid), NEW (strip empty, ISSUE #8, folder cleared to strip.json), OPEN (3 panels and their text back, ISSUE #7), PDF (1 page 842 x 217 pt), MP4 (H.264 1280x720). The software renderer couldn't keep real time (a 12 s timeline became 6.6 s): check the MP4 length on the PC's GPU.

## #524 STAR IN IT: ⭐ in DirroComics! (Dirrogram card, YES / NO lines, consent log; owner decisions in dirrocomics-plan.md)
- Phone (dirrogram.js / .html, gram copy synced): bar button ⭐ STAR -> card sheet: TAKE PHOTO (the next drawn frame of the Dirrogram view incl. PEOPLE / FX, 480 px JPEG under 55 KB, read in the frame loop: globalThis.cxSnap), comic name (24), genre (NOIR / ACTION / HORROR / DRAMA / SCI-FI / ROMANCE), up to 2 own lines (100), 'I'm 18 or older', the terms (TERMS text; version T1-<sha256 first 8 bytes>), I'M IN -> relay 'star'. Device id: random, kept in localStorage 'vire.dirro.dev', sent as 'hi' on every connect. Questions arrive as 'ask' -> sheet with the exact line, YES / NO -> 'ans'. MY CONSENTS -> 'mine' (this device's records with receipts) + WITHDRAW -> 'wd'. Receipts kept on the phone ('vire.dirro.rcpt'). No contact details (owner).
- Relay (cloudflare-relay/worker.js, Room = SQLite Durable Object): table clog (seq, at, rec JSON, prev, hash = SHA-256(prev + rec)), meta (salt). Records OPT_IN (dev, name, genre, lines, terms version, media = SHA-256 of the photo, age true, ip = salted hash), LINE_YES / LINE_NO (dev, ask, text + SHA-256, media, panel), WITHDRAW. Relay time. Receipt = first 12 hex of the record hash. A phone may only speak for the device id it said 'hi' with; a card needs DJ connected, name, photo, age, terms; one card per 30 s. DJ 'ask' goes only to that device's sockets (held, last 10, until it says hi); answers only for an outstanding ask of that device. DJ 'clog' -> the whole log. Tested in wrangler dev (workerd): spoofing ignored, missing age refused, targeted ask, pending ask on reconnect, YES / NO / WITHDRAW, mine, chain verifies, a tampered copy fails.
- Quest: dirro-host.js passes star / ans / wd / clog to main.js onStar -> cxQ queue (toast for a new card); stream.js _stuSync sends unsent items to DirroVJ ('vjstar', up to 6 a second) while DirroVJ is on and allowed. DirroVJ -> Quest 'vjask' / 'vjclog' (allowed only) -> dirro.relay() -> relay 'ask' / 'clog'.
- DirroVJ (comics.js): stars in strip.json and in .dirrocomic (manifest.stars incl. photos and receipts; NEW ISSUE keeps the night's cast); STARS cards on the desk (photo, name, genre, own lines, receipt, ADD AS PANEL = the photo as a panel tagged star = device id). Balloons (speech / thought / shout) get a speaker select (anyone or a ⭐ guest) + ASK: the exact text goes to the guest's phone (with the SHA-256 of the panel's media and the panel id). Gate: a guest's balloon is drawn (screen, preview, MP4, PDF) only when ok = yes AND the text is still the asked text; editing it needs a new ASK. An answer that arrives while the desk holds an older copy wins. WITHDRAW: that guest's photo panels are deleted (files too), their lines removed, the card stays marked WITHDRAWN. CONSENT LOG button: the relay's log, chain re-checked in DirroVJ, downloaded as DirroComics_consent_<date>.jsonl + last hash shown (for the outside timestamp: commit it to the repo / OpenTimestamps by hand for now).
- Tests: phone sheets in a harness page with the real STAR code against the local relay + a scripted Quest (card, photo, age / photo checks, ask, YES, mine, withdraw); DirroVJ + desk headless (card on the desk, ADD AS PANEL, speaker, ASK message, gate before / after YES, edit -> hidden, withdraw). Fix during test: another mid-line '//' comment swallowed 'let cur, pos, ver' in comics.js (now /* */).
- TO DO by the owner: deploy the relay (cloudflare-relay: npx wrangler deploy; the new tables create themselves, rooms keep working) and upload the whole dirroclub_website folder (gram changed). Not tested: on a real phone / Quest / DirroVJ chain. The terms text needs a lawyer's read before real gigs.
- sw.js v51. No Node restart. Backups backups/pre524/ (+ gram/).

## #527 DirroComics STORYBOARD (AI) on the VJ desk (owner, 27 Oct)
- Owner: an LLM writes an 8 to 12 panel storyboard (SFX, speech / thought / narrator) from selected clips and pictures and a genre (FILM NOIR, HORROR, MICRO DRAMA, CYBERPUNK); the VJ assembles. Engines (owner's pick): switchable Gemini (Google free tier: Google may use the data, humans may read it) and Claude API (paid). Guest photos: owner said 'yes, any engine'; the desk's 'Send ⭐ guest photos' box is OFF by default until the terms mention AI processing (proposed wording to the owner; not added yet).
- New js/storyboard.js (desk side, new section under DirroComics; loaded by a module script at the end of vjdesk.html with a ctx: $, esc, cx, cxRead, CX getter, thumbs). Keys, model choice, genre, notes, ticked sources and the last storyboard in this browser's localStorage (vire.sb.*). ↻ MODELS lists what the key can use (Gemini v1beta/models filtered to generateContent text models, flash first; Claude /v1/models, haiku first) so no model ids are hard-coded.
- Request: system prompt (house style, genre note, rules: max 3 items, word limits, only named cast / STAR guests / narrator / off-panel voices may talk, unnamed real people never get speech or thoughts, no sexual content / slurs / mocking looks, JSON shape) + user parts: genre, panel count, cast notes, STAR cast (only when guest photos are allowed), then SOURCE n labels with frames (clips: 3 frames at 10/50/90 %, stills: 1; max 640 px JPEG; the left-eye picture for SBS / RGB+D). Gemini: generateContent with responseMimeType application/json. Claude: /v1/messages with anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access, max_tokens 6000. Token use shown after each run.
- Result: cards per panel (source thumb, shot, text lines); ADD / ADD ALL -> comics.js op 'sbadd' {src, v: fx, fy, zoom, style, items}: a new panel at the end of the strip sharing the source's file (star tag kept, so WITHDRAW still removes it). storyboard.js maps the AI's source coordinates through the panel crop (same maths as euv) to item x / y / tail; captions snap to top or bottom, balloons below a top caption. A speaker matching a STAR comic name gets item.star = device id (hidden until ASK + YES, #524 gate).
- Test: cloud headless with both APIs mocked (Playwright routes): model lists, request shape (key header, 5 frames, texts), 10 / 8 panel boards, ADD ALL -> 13 panels with framing, styles, items, 1 star line gated. PC pane: desk loads the section, sw v54. NOT tested against the real Gemini / Claude APIs (no keys here). sw.js v54 (+ js/storyboard.js). No Node restart. Backup backups/pre527/.
